{"actual_behavior":"控制台/WebTiles 接受数字或命名键码；负数是 Crawl 特殊键码，宏和 rc 中的数字键码使用 \\{n}，完整表还受键盘与构建差异影响。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:531","dynamic_menu":["crawl-ref/source/menu.cc:1552","crawl-ref/source/menu.cc:1832"],"lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2341"},"current_chinese":"<h>用于控制台和网页贴图版的键码</h>\n\n键码可以输入数字，在某些情况下，可以输入人类可读的字符串。\n下表给出了数字键代码和字符串；修改的键通常必须以数字形式输入。\n当输入一个键码作为宏操作的一部分时，应使用键码n的以下语法对其进行包装：\n<w>\\{n}</w>。当在rc文件中通过数字定义键码时，也应使用此方法。示例：\n定义触发F1的宏时，可以编写“\\{F1\\}”或“\\{-265}”。\n对于作为宏定义一部分的命名键，可以省略\\{}。\n\n正值是ASCII字符代码。负值是Crawl内部用于特殊键的键码。\n正确解释这些符号是必需的。键盘差异很大，因此并非所有键盘上都适用于这些绑定；\n常见的变体如下所示。\n\n下面是一些可能想要绑定的主键的便捷参考。\n\n<w>8</w>：Backspace（和/或Delete，取决于键盘）\n<w>9</w>：Tab\n<w>13</w>：Enter（和/或Return键，取决于键盘）\n<w>27</w>：Esc\n\n<w>-233</w>：Shift-Tab\n<w>-222</w>：Ctrl-Tab（在一些键盘上）\n<w>-255</w>：Delete（在一些键盘上）\n<w>-265</w>：F1   <w>-264</w>：F2   <w>-263</w>：F3   <w>-262</w>：F4   <w>-261</w>：F5\n<w>-260</w>：F6   <w>-259</w>：F7   <w>-258</w>：F8   <w>-257</w>：F9   <w>-256</w>：F10\n\n最高可定义达F22的键码，但可能不适用于所有结构。\n（在网页图像版，保留F11和F12。）\n\n<w>数字键盘和定位键：</w>\n\n在许多键盘上，用<w>numlock</w>切换数字键和定位键。\n这些键的修饰符可能不适用于所有键盘。当被修改的数字键盘键生效时，\nShift和Ctrl通常将这些键视为被修改的定位键，而不管数字锁的状态如何。\n\n定位键：\n\n<w>-245</w>: PgDn    <w>-234</w>: Shift-PgDn    <w>-223</w>: Ctrl-PgDn\n<w>-246</w>: PgUp    <w>-235</w>: Shift-PgUp    <w>-224</w>: Ctrl-PgUp\n<w>-247</w>: Clear   <w>-236</w>: Shift-Clear   <w>-225</w>: Ctrl-Clear\n<w>-248</w>: End     <w>-237</w>: Shift-End     <w>-226</w>: Ctrl-End\n<w>-249</w>: Home    <w>-238</w>: Shift-Home    <w>-227</w>: Ctrl-Home\n<w>-250</w>: Insert  <w>-239</w>: Shift-Insert  <w>-228</w>: Ctrl-Insert\n<w>-251</w>: Right   <w>-240</w>: Shift-Right   <w>-229</w>: Ctrl-Right\n<w>-252</w>: Left    <w>-241</w>: Shift-Left    <w>-230</w>: Ctrl-Left\n<w>-253</w>: Down    <w>-242</w>: Shift-Down    <w>-231</w>: Ctrl-Down\n<w>-254</w>: Up      <w>-243</w>: Shift-Up      <w>-232</w>: Ctrl-Up\n\n数字键盘键：\n\n<w>-1007</w>: NP7   <w>-1008</w>: NP8   <w>-1009</w>: NP9\n<w>-1004</w>: NP4   <w>-1005</w>: NP5   <w>-1006</w>: NP6\n<w>-1001</w>: NP1   <w>-1002</w>: NP2   <w>-1003</w>: NP3\n<w>-1000</w>: NP0\n<w>-1010</w>: NPenter\n<w>-1012</2>: NP/   <w>-1015</w>: NP*   <w>-1016</w>: NP+   <w>-1018</w>: NP-\n<w>-1019</w>: NP.   <w>-1021</w>: NP=\n\n一些些数字键盘上的标点键可能与“常规”标点键没有区别，这同样取决于键盘和结构。\n","current_english":"<h>Keycodes for console and webtiles</h>\n\nKeycodes can be entered as numbers, or in some cases, human readable strings.\nThe following tables give both the numeric keycode, and the string; modified\nkeys generally must be entered by number. When entering a keycode  as part of a\nmacro action, it should be wrapped using the following syntax for keycode n:\n<w>\\{n}</w>. This should also be used when defining a keycode by number in your\nrc file. Example: when defining a macro that triggers F1, you can write\n\"\\{F1\\}\" or \"\\{-265}\". For named keys as part of a macro definition, the \\{}\nmay be omitted.\n\nPositive numeric values are ASCII character codes. Negative values are\ncrawl-internal keycodes for special keys. The sign is required for these to\nbe interpreted correctly. Keyboards vary quite a bit, and so not all of these\nbindings are available on all keyboards; cases where there is often variation\nare indicated specifically below.\n\nThe following is a quick-reference for some of the main keys you might want to\nbind.\n\n<w>8</w>: Backspace (and/or Delete, depending on keyboard)\n<w>9</w>: Tab\n<w>13</w>: Enter (and/or the Return key, depending on keyboard)\n<w>27</w>: Esc\n\n<w>-233</w>: Shift-Tab\n<w>-222</w>: Ctrl-Tab (on some keyboards)\n<w>-255</w>: Delete (on some keyboards)\n<w>-265</w>: F1   <w>-264</w>: F2   <w>-263</w>: F3   <w>-262</w>: F4   <w>-261</w>: F5\n<w>-260</w>: F6   <w>-259</w>: F7   <w>-258</w>: F8   <w>-257</w>: F9   <w>-256</w>: F10\n\nKeycodes up to F22 are defined, but may not work on all builds. (On webtiles,\nF11 and F12 are reserved.)\n\n<w>Number pad and navigation keys:</w>\n\nOn many keyboards, <w>numlock</w> switches between the number keys and\nnavigation keys. Modifiers for these keys may not work on all keyboards. When\nmodified number pad keys do work, Shift and Ctrl typically treats those keys as\nmodified navigation keys regardless of numlock status.\n\nNavigation keys:\n\n<w>-245</w>: PgDn    <w>-234</w>: Shift-PgDn    <w>-223</w>: Ctrl-PgDn\n<w>-246</w>: PgUp    <w>-235</w>: Shift-PgUp    <w>-224</w>: Ctrl-PgUp\n<w>-247</w>: Clear   <w>-236</w>: Shift-Clear   <w>-225</w>: Ctrl-Clear\n<w>-248</w>: End     <w>-237</w>: Shift-End     <w>-226</w>: Ctrl-End\n<w>-249</w>: Home    <w>-238</w>: Shift-Home    <w>-227</w>: Ctrl-Home\n<w>-250</w>: Insert  <w>-239</w>: Shift-Insert  <w>-228</w>: Ctrl-Insert\n<w>-251</w>: Right   <w>-240</w>: Shift-Right   <w>-229</w>: Ctrl-Right\n<w>-252</w>: Left    <w>-241</w>: Shift-Left    <w>-230</w>: Ctrl-Left\n<w>-253</w>: Down    <w>-242</w>: Shift-Down    <w>-231</w>: Ctrl-Down\n<w>-254</w>: Up      <w>-243</w>: Shift-Up      <w>-232</w>: Ctrl-Up\n\nNumber pad keys:\n\n<w>-1007</w>: NP7   <w>-1008</w>: NP8   <w>-1009</w>: NP9\n<w>-1004</w>: NP4   <w>-1005</w>: NP5   <w>-1006</w>: NP6\n<w>-1001</w>: NP1   <w>-1002</w>: NP2   <w>-1003</w>: NP3\n<w>-1000</w>: NP0\n<w>-1010</w>: NPenter\n<w>-1012</w>: NP/   <w>-1015</w>: NP*   <w>-1016</w>: NP+   <w>-1018</w>: NP-\n<w>-1019</w>: NP.   <w>-1021</w>: NP=\n\nSome number pad punctuation keys may not be distinguished from their \"regular\"\ncounterparts, again depending on keyboard and build.\n","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"macros, platform keys, and wizard","display_context":"Specific HelpDB topic rendered by the formatted help platform keys, and wizard","display_context":"Specific HelpDB topic rendered by the formatted help scroller.","english":"<h>Keycodes for console and webtiles</h>\n\nKeycodes can be entered as numbers, or in some cases, human readable strings.\nThe following tables give both the numeric keycode, and the string; modified\nkeys generally must be entered by number. When entering a keycode  as part of a\nmacro action, it should be wrapped using the following syntax for keycode n:\n<w>\\{n}</w>. This should also be used when defining a keycode by number in your\nrc file. Example: when defining a macro that triggers F1, you can write\n\"\\{F1\\}\" or \"\\{-265}\". For named keys as part of a macro definition, the \\{}\nmay be omitted.\n\nPositive numeric values are ASCII character codes. Negative values are\ncrawl-internal keycodes for special keys. The sign is required for these to\nbe interpreted correctly. Keyboards vary quite a bit, and so not all of these\nbindings are available on all keyboards; cases where there is often variation\nare indicated specifically below.\n\nThe following is a quick-reference for some of the main keys you might want to\nbind.\n\n<w>8</w>: Backspace (and/or Delete, depending on keyboard)\n<w>9</w>: Tab\n<w>13</w>: Enter (and/or the Return key, depending on keyboard)\n<w>27</w>: Esc\n\n<w>-233</w>: Shift-Tab\n<w>-222</w>: Ctrl-Tab (on some keyboards)\n<w>-255</w>: Delete (on some keyboards)\n<w>-265</w>: F1   <w>-264</w>: F2   <w>-263</w>: F3   <w>-262</w>: F4   <w>-261</w>: F5\n<w>-260</w>: F6   <w>-259</w>: F7   <w>-258</w>: F8   <w>-257</w>: F9   <w>-256</w>: F10\n\nKeycodes up to F22 are defined, but may not work on all builds. (On webtiles,\nF11 and F12 are reserved.)\n\n<w>Number pad and navigation keys:</w>\n\nOn many keyboards, <w>numlock</w> switches between the number keys and\nnavigation keys. Modifiers for these keys may not work on all keyboards. When\nmodified number pad keys do work, Shift and Ctrl typically treats those keys as\nmodified navigation keys regardless of numlock status.\n\nNavigation keys:\n\n<w>-245</w>: PgDn    <w>-234</w>: Shift-PgDn    <w>-223</w>: Ctrl-PgDn\n<w>-246</w>: PgUp    <w>-235</w>: Shift-PgUp    <w>-224</w>: Ctrl-PgUp\n<w>-247</w>: Clear   <w>-236</w>: Shift-Clear   <w>-225</w>: Ctrl-Clear\n<w>-248</w>: End     <w>-237</w>: Shift-End     <w>-226</w>: Ctrl-End\n<w>-249</w>: Home    <w>-238</w>: Shift-Home    <w>-227</w>: Ctrl-Home\n<w>-250</w>: Insert  <w>-239</w>: Shift-Insert  <w>-228</w>: Ctrl-Insert\n<w>-251</w>: Right   <w>-240</w>: Shift-Right   <w>-229</w>: Ctrl-Right\n<w>-252</w>: Left    <w>-241</w>: Shift-Left    <w>-230</w>: Ctrl-Left\n<w>-253</w>: Down    <w>-242</w>: Shift-Down    <w>-231</w>: Ctrl-Down\n<w>-254</w>: Up      <w>-243</w>: Shift-Up      <w>-232</w>: Ctrl-Up\n\nNumber pad keys:\n\n<w>-1007</w>: NP7   <w>-1008</w>: NP8   <w>-1009</w>: NP9\n<w>-1004</w>: NP4   <w>-1005</w>: NP5   <w>-1006</w>: NP6\n<w>-1001</w>: NP1   <w>-1002</w>: NP2   <w>-1003</w>: NP3\n<w>-1000</w>: NP0\n<w>-1010</w>: NPenter\n<w>-1012</w>: NP/   <w>-1015</w>: NP*   <w>-1016</w>: NP+   <w>-1018</w>: NP-\n<w>-1019</w>: NP.   <w>-1021</w>: NP=\n\nSome number pad punctuation keys may not be distinguished from their \"regular\"\ncounterparts, again depending on keyboard and build.\n","english_key_line":660,"english_tokens":{"blank_line_bullet_count":0,"bullet_line_count":0,"lua":{"blocks":[],"errors":[]},"markup":{"balance_errors":[],"malformed":[],"tag_counts":{"</h>":1,"</w>":67,"<h>":1,"<w>":67},"tag_sequence":["<h>","</h>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>"],"technical_angle_counts":{},"technical_angle_sequence":[]},"numbers":["265","8","9","13","27","233","222","255","265","264","263","262","261","260","259","258","257","256","245","234","223","246","235","224","247","236","225","248","237","226","249","238","227","250","239","228","251","240","229","252","241","230","253","242","231","254","243","232","1007","1008","1009","1004","1005","1006","1001","1002","1003","1000","1010","1012","1015","1016","1018","1019","1021"],"option_literals":[],"path_literals":["and/","and/","NP/"],"quoted_command_literals":[],"urls":[],"w_contents":["\\{n}","8","9","13","27","-233","-222","-255","-265","-264","-263","-262","-261","-260","-259","-258","-257","-256","Number pad and navigation keys:","numlock","-245","-234","-223","-246","-235","-224","-247","-236","-225","-248","-237","-226","-249","-238","-227","-250","-239","-228","-251","-240","-229","-252","-241","-230","-253","-242","-231","-254","-243","-232","-1007","-1008","-1009","-1004","-1005","-1006","-1001","-1002","-1003","-1000","-1010","-1012","-1015","-1016","-1018","-1019","-1021"]},"fact_sha256":"3a93f160db184c021b8edfb86131c727b6e6d6a4a980b95bdd0325e9a63aab28","identity":"help:console-keycodes","key":"console-keycodes","kind":"help","lifecycle":"console-only","producer":[{"guards":["!USE_TILE_LOCAL"],"location":"crawl-ref/source/macro.cc:1481","path":"crawl-ref/source/macro.cc"},{"guards":["!USE_TILE_LOCAL"],"location":"crawl-ref/source/macro.cc:1524","path":"crawl-ref/source/macro.cc"},{"guards":["!USE_TILE_LOCAL"],"location":"crawl-ref/source/macro.cc:1591","path":"crawl-ref/source/macro.cc"}],"structural_differences":["format_tag_counts","format_tag_sequence","numbers","path_literals","w_contents"],"token_contract_equal":{"blank_line_bullet_count":true,"bullet_line_count":true,"format_tag_counts":false,"format_tag_sequence":false,"lua_block_count":true,"lua_comparison_literals":true,"lua_control_skeleton":true,"numbers":false,"option_literals":true,"path_literals":false,"quoted_command_literals":true,"technical_angle_counts":true,"technical_angle_sequence":true,"urls":true,"w_contents":false}},"proposed_translation":"<h>控制台与 WebTiles 键码</h>\n\n键码可以输入为数字，在某些情况下也可以使用易读的名称。\n下表同时给出数字键码和名称；带修饰键的按键通常必须用数字输入。\n当键码作为宏操作的一部分输入时，应使用以下格式表示键码n：\n<w>\\{n}</w>。在rc文件中以数字定义键码时也应使用这种格式。例如，\n定义由F1触发的宏时，可以写成“\\{F1\\}”或“\\{-265}”。\n宏定义中的命名按键可以省略\\{}。\n\n正数值是ASCII字符码，负数值是Crawl内部的特殊键码。\n负号不可省略，否则无法正确解释这些键码。键盘差异很大，\n因此并非所有键盘都支持以下绑定；下文会特别注明常见差异。\n\n下面列出一些常用绑定按键，供快速查阅。\n\n<w>8</w>: Backspace（和/或Delete，取决于键盘）\n<w>9</w>: Tab\n<w>13</w>: Enter（和/或Return键，取决于键盘）\n<w>27</w>: Esc\n\n<w>-233</w>: Shift-Tab\n<w>-222</w>: Ctrl-Tab（在某些键盘上）\n<w>-255</w>: Delete（在某些键盘上）\n<w>-265</w>: F1   <w>-264</w>: F2   <w>-263</w>: F3   <w>-262</w>: F4   <w>-261</w>: F5\n<w>-260</w>: F6   <w>-259</w>: F7   <w>-258</w>: F8   <w>-257</w>: F9   <w>-256</w>: F10\n\n键码定义到F22，但不一定适用于所有构建。\n（在WebTiles中，F11和F12为保留键。）\n\n<w>数字小键盘与导航键：</w>\n\n在许多键盘上，<w>numlock</w>会在数字键和导航键之间切换。\n这些键的修饰键不一定适用于所有键盘。带修饰键的数字小键盘按键可用时，\n无论numlock状态如何，Shift和Ctrl通常都会使这些按键被视为带修饰的导航键。\n\n导航键：\n\n<w>-245</w>: PgDn    <w>-234</w>: Shift-PgDn    <w>-223</w>: Ctrl-PgDn\n<w>-246</w>: PgUp    <w>-235</w>: Shift-PgUp    <w>-224</w>: Ctrl-PgUp\n<w>-247</w>: Clear   <w>-236</w>: Shift-Clear   <w>-225</w>: Ctrl-Clear\n<w>-248</w>: End     <w>-237</w>: Shift-End     <w>-226</w>: Ctrl-End\n<w>-249</w>: Home    <w>-238</w>: Shift-Home    <w>-227</w>: Ctrl-Home\n<w>-250</w>: Insert  <w>-239</w>: Shift-Insert  <w>-228</w>: Ctrl-Insert\n<w>-251</w>: Right   <w>-240</w>: Shift-Right   <w>-229</w>: Ctrl-Right\n<w>-252</w>: Left    <w>-241</w>: Shift-Left    <w>-230</w>: Ctrl-Left\n<w>-253</w>: Down    <w>-242</w>: Shift-Down    <w>-231</w>: Ctrl-Down\n<w>-254</w>: Up      <w>-243</w>: Shift-Up      <w>-232</w>: Ctrl-Up\n\n数字小键盘：\n\n<w>-1007</w>: NP7   <w>-1008</w>: NP8   <w>-1009</w>: NP9\n<w>-1004</w>: NP4   <w>-1005</w>: NP5   <w>-1006</w>: NP6\n<w>-1001</w>: NP1   <w>-1002</w>: NP2   <w>-1003</w>: NP3\n<w>-1000</w>: NP0\n<w>-1010</w>: NPenter\n<w>-1012</w>: NP/   <w>-1015</w>: NP*   <w>-1016</w>: NP+   <w>-1018</w>: NP-\n<w>-1019</w>: NP.   <w>-1021</w>: NP=\n\n某些数字小键盘标点键可能无法与对应的“常规”按键区分；\n这同样取决于键盘和构建。\n","reentry_trigger":"对应英文源文、生产 consumer、格式约定或 docs/glossary.md 裁决发生变化时重新审阅。","rejected_alternatives":["仅作零散词语替换：跨段落的事实、列表或格式问题无法得到完整修复。","原样保留基线译文：已确认的语义或结构缺陷会继续展示给玩家。"],"reviewer_rationale":"基线行文多处误译并含 `</2>` 非法闭合标签；整段重译才能同时保持全部键码、标记和平台限制。本卡逐字记录的 `keycode n:\\n` 中 `\\n` 是 JSON 换行转义，并非 Windows 路径。path-portability: allow-literal","terminal_conclusion":"retranslate"}

{"actual_behavior":"分支快捷字母先选择分支，再选择该分支的层数；Ctrl-P 前往上级分支，其他按键分别处理楼梯、导航点和祭坛。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:531","dynamic_menu":["crawl-ref/source/menu.cc:1552","crawl-ref/source/menu.cc:1832"],"lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2341"},"current_chinese":"<h>跨层移动（选择分支）：\n 使用分支的快捷键以移动到相应分支。\n\n 选择分支后，系统将提示你输入该分支的深度（这里有更多帮助）。\n\n <w>Tab</w>    ：重复上一次的跨层移动。\n <w>.</w>      ：移动到当前分支的另一楼层。\n <w><<</w>     ：上最近的楼梯。\n <w>></w>      ：下最近的楼梯。\n <w>Ctrl-P</w> ：移动到当前分支的上一楼层。\n <w>*</w>      ：显示所有可用导航点（如有设置）。\n <w>0</w>-<w>9</w>    ：移动到有编号的导航点。\n <w>_</w>      ：显示可用祭坛（如有发现）。","current_english":"<h>Interlevel Travel (choose a branch):\n Use the shortcut letter for a branch to select the branch for travel.\n\n Once you select a branch, you will be prompted for a depth in that\n branch (more help is available there).\n\n <w>Tab</w>    : Repeat last interlevel travel.\n <w>.</w>      : Travel to a level in the current branch.\n <w><<</w>      : Go up the nearest stairs.\n <w>></w>      : Go down the nearest stairs.\n <w>Ctrl-P</w> : Travel to a level in the branch above this one.\n <w>*</w>      : Show available waypoints (if any are set).\n <w>0</w>-<w>9</w>    : Go to the numbered waypoint.\n <w>_</w>      : Show available altars (if any are discovered).","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"map, search, travel, and annotation","display_context":"Specific HelpDB topic rendered by the formatted help    : Change default to one level below the current.\n <w>-</w> or <w>p</w> : Change default to the branch above this one.\n <w>$</w>      : Change default to deepest visited level in this branch.\n {"actual_behavior":"已知物品菜单可逐项或按类别覆盖自动拾取默认值，并在已知/未知页面切换、恢复默认或用正则批量选择。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:531","dynamic_menu":["crawl-ref/source/menu.cc:1552","crawl-ref/source/menu.cc:1832"],"lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2341"},"current_chinese":"                   <h>已知物品菜单</h>\n\n<h>定位和选择物品</h>\n使用通常的定位键（<w>方向</w>键、<w>pgdn</w>/<w>pgup</w>、<w>home</w>、<w>end</w>）选择物品，\n使用<w>空格</w>/<w>.</w>切换所选物品的自动拾取状态。\n按下物品各类的字符键也将切换其自动拾取状态。\n\n<h>选择物品种类</h>\n以灰色显示的物品类型会采用配置文件中设定的默认自动拾取选项。\n（对你的角色无用的物品会以<darkgrey>深灰色</darkgrey>显示。）\n选定一类物品后会覆盖这些选项（以<w>白色</w>文本显示），切换物品为以下状态：\n <w>+</w>  将自动拾取此类物品。\n <w>-</w>  将不再自动拾取此类物品。\n还可以设定自动拾取所有未知物品。\n\n<h>各类物品的快捷键</h>\n你可以按以下键快速选择某类物品：\n<w>!</w>   药水      <w>?</w>   卷轴      <w>(</w>   弹药        <w>\"</w>   项链\n<w>=</w>   戒指      <w>/</w>   魔杖      <w>|</w>   法杖\n全局选择：<w>*</w>   反选   <w>^</w>   全部重置为默认状态\n\n<h>查看未知和已知物品</h>\n按<w>\\</w>和<w>-</w>切换已知和未知物品菜单。\n请注意，未知物品菜单中没有上述功能。\n\n<h>将自动拾取重置为默认设定</h>\n按<w>Ctrl-D</w>然后按物品字母或类别快捷键，会将所选物品重置为默认设定。\n\n<h>正规表示式</h>\n你可以按<w>Ctrl-F</w>输入一个单词（或一个正则表达式）。\n所匹配的物品都会被选定。\n","current_english":"                   <h>Known items menu</h>\n\n<h>Navigation and selecting items</h>\nUse the usual navigation keys (<w>arrow</w> keys, <w>pgdn</w>/<w>pgup</w>, <w>home</w>, <w>end</w>) to select\nitems, and <w>Space</w>/<w>.</w> to toggle the autopickup mode for the selected item type.\nPressing an item type's letter key will also toggle its autopickup state.\n\n<h>Selecting item types</h>\nItem types displayed in grey use the default autopickup options set in your\nconfiguration file. (Items that are useless to your character are displayed\nin <darkgrey>dark grey</darkgrey>.) Selecting a type of item overrides those options\n(indicated by <w>white</w> text), and toggles the item between the states:\n <w>+</w>  Items of this type will be picked up automatically.\n <w>-</w>  Items of this type will not be picked up automatically.\nAutopickup can also be toggled for all unknown items of a given category.\n\n<h>Category shortcuts</h>\nYou can quickly select all item types in a given category by pressing:\n<w>!</w>   potions      <w>?</w>   scrolls      <w>(</w>   ammunition        <w>\"</w>   amulets\n<w>=</w>   rings        <w>/</w>   wands        <w>|</w>   magical staves\nGlobal selects:  <w>*</w>   invert all   <w>^</w>   reset all to default\n\n<h>Viewing unknown and known items</h>\n<w>\\</w> and <w>-</w> toggle between the known and unknown items menus. Note\nthat none of the above features are available from the unknown items menu.\n\n<h>Restoring autopickup defaults</h>\nPressing <w>Ctrl-D</w> followed by an item letter or a category shortcut\ncancels the override and restores the selected item or items to their default\nsettings.\n\n<h>Regular expressions</h>\nYou can press <w>Ctrl-F</w> to enter a word (or a regular expression).\nAll matching items will be selected.\n","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"items, pickup, and known-items menus","display_context":"Specific HelpDB topic rendered by the formatted help scroller.","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/known-items.cc:63","crawl-ref/source/dat/database/help.txt:31","crawl-ref/source/dat/database/zh/help.txt:35"],"fact_sha256":"d3009b743d94d649e6034a88c1704c43a0190cfc99257853ac19504435a35827","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md@95eeacf9704e046c2010ef34859b750d2f8a1937ad87c4a86e8a404c98689407","identity":"help:known-menu","lifecycle":"current-player-help","producer":[{"guards":[],"location":"crawl-ref/source/known-items.cc:63","path":"crawl-ref/source/known-items.cc"}],"production_facts":{"chinese":" your\nconfiguration file. (Items that are useless to your character are displayed\nin <darkgrey>dark grey</darkgrey>.) Selecting a type of item overrides those options\n(indicated by <w>white</w> text), and toggles the item between the states:\n <w>+</w>  Items of this type will be picked up automatically.\n <w>-</w>  Items of this type will not be picked up automatically.\nAutopickup can also be toggled for all unknown items of a given category.\n\n<h>Category shortcuts</h>\nYou can quickly select all item types in a given category by pressing:\n<w>!</w>   potions      <w>?</w>   scrolls      <w>(</w>   ammunition        <w>\"</w>   amulets\n<w>=</w>   rings        <w>/</w>   wands        <w>|</w>   magical staves\nGlobal selects:  <w>*</w>   invert all   <w>^</w>   reset all to default\n\n<h>Viewing unknown and known items</h>\n<w>\\</w> and <w>-</w> toggle between the known and unknown items menus. Note\nthat none of the above features are available from the unknown items menu.\n\n<h>Restoring autopickup defaults</h>\nPressing <w>Ctrl-D</w> followed by an item letter or a category shortcut\ncancels the override and restores the selected item or items to their default\nsettings.\n\n<h>Regular expressions</h>\nYou can press <w>Ctrl-F</w> to enter a word (or a regular expression).\nAll matching items will be selected.\n","english_key_line":31,"english_tokens":{"blank_line_bullet_count":0,"bullet_line_count":0,"lua":{"blocks":[],"errors":[]},"markup":{"balance_errors":[],"malformed":[],"tag_counts":{"</darkgrey>":1,"</h>":7,"</w>":23,"<darkgrey>":1,"<h>":7,"<w>":23},"tag_sequence":["<h>","</h>","<h>","</h>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<h>","</h>","<darkgrey>","</darkgrey>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<h>","</h>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<h>","</h>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<h>","</h>","<w>","</w>","<h>","</h>","<w>","</w>"],"technical_angle_counts":{},"technical_angle_sequence":[]},"numbers":[],"option_literals":[],"path_literals":[],"quoted_command_literals":[],"urls":[],"w_contents":["arrow","pgdn","pgup","home","end","Space",".","white","+","-","!","?","(","\"","=","/","|","*","^","\\","-","Ctrl-D","Ctrl-F"]},"fact_sha256":"d3009b743d94d649e6034a88c1704c43a0190cfc99257853ac19504435a35827","identity":"help:known-menu","key":"known-menu","kind":"help","lifecycle":"current-player-help","producer":[{"guards":[],"location":"crawl-ref/source/known-items.cc:63","path":"crawl-ref/source/known-items.cc"}],"structural_differences":["w_contents"],"token_contract_equal":{"blank_line_bullet_count":true,"bullet_line_count":true,"format_tag_counts":true,"format_tag_sequence":true,"lua_block_count":true,"lua_comparison_literals":true,"lua_control_skeleton":true,"numbers":true,"option_literals":true,"path_literals":true,"quoted_command_literals":true,"technical_angle_counts":true,"technical_angle_sequence":true,"urls":true,"w_contents":false}},"proposed_translation":"                   <h>已知物品菜单</h>\n\n<h>定位和选择物品</h>\n使用通常的定位键（<w>方向</w>键、<w>pgdn</w>/<w>pgup</w>、<w>home</w>、<w>end</w>）选择物品，\n使用<w>空格</w>/<w>.</w>切换所选物品的自动拾取状态。\n按下某类物品对应的字母键，也会切换该类物品的自动拾取状态。\n\n<h>选择物品种类</h>\n以灰色显示的物品类型会采用配置文件中设定的默认自动拾取选项。\n（对你的角色无用的物品会以<darkgrey>深灰色</darkgrey>显示。）\n选定一类物品后会覆盖这些选项（以<w>白色</w>文本显示），切换物品为以下状态：\n <w>+</w>  将自动拾取此类物品。\n <w>-</w>  将不再自动拾取此类物品。\n还可以切换某一类别中所有未知物品的自动拾取状态。\n\n<h>各类物品的快捷键</h>\n你可以按以下键快速选择某类物品：\n<w>!</w>   药水      <w>?</w>   卷轴      <w>(</w>   弹药        <w>\"</w>   项链\n<w>=</w>   戒指      <w>/</w>   魔杖      <w>|</w>   法杖\n全局选择：<w>*</w>   反选   <w>^</w>   全部重置为默认状态\n\n<h>查看未知和已知物品</h>\n按<w>\\</w>和<w>-</w>切换已知和未知物品菜单。\n请注意，未知物品菜单中没有上述功能。\n\n<h>将自动拾取重置为默认设定</h>\n按<w>Ctrl-D</w>，再按物品字母或类别快捷键，会取消覆盖设置，\n并将所选的一件或多件物品恢复为默认自动拾取设置。\n\n<h>正则表达式</h>\n你可以按<w>Ctrl-F</w>输入一个单词（或一个正则表达式）。\n所匹配的物品都会被选定。\n","reentry_trigger":"对应英文源文、生产 consumer、格式约定或 docs/glossary.md 裁决发生变化时重新审阅。","rejected_alternatives":["原样保留基线译文：已确认的局部歧义、遗漏或术语问题仍会存在。","整项重译：问题范围局部，扩大改写会增加不必要的回归面。"],"reviewer_rationale":"基线把未知物品范围、取消 override 和正则术语说得不完整；局部修订足以恢复菜单行为。","terminal_conclusion":"adjust"}
{"actual_behavior":"楼层地图按运行环境展示移动、排除、传送、巫师模式等可用按键，并通过原有 Lua 条件决定部分文本是否显示。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:531","dynamic_menu":["crawl-ref/source/menu.cc:1552","crawl-ref/source/menu.cc:1832"],"lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2341"},"current_chinese":"<h>楼层地图（在主界面中按“<w>X</w>”）：</h>\n<w>Esc</w>        ：离开楼层地图（也可以使用<w>空格</w>或其他未被占用的键）。\n<w>方向</w>       ：移动光标。\n<w>Shift-方向键</w> ：以更大的步长移动光标（默认为7）。\n<w>-</w>或<w>+</w>  ：向上或向下滚动20个网格。\n<w>.</w>          ：开始移动（也可以用<w>Enter</w>、<w>,</w>和<w>;</w>）。\n             （如果仍在@上，则将光标移动到最后一次移动目的地。）\n<w>[</w> or <w>]</w>     ：查看更高或更低的一个楼层。\n             （将光标移动到楼梯，如果有已知的楼梯被高亮显示的话。）\n<w>G</w>          ：设定分支和深度，显示另一个楼层。\n<w>o</w>          ：将光标移动到下一个自动探索目标。\n<w>{</w>或<w>}</w>     ：缩放（只适用于贴图版）。\n\n<h>移动禁区</h>\n<w>e</w>          ：创建一个移动禁区，可以改变它的半径，或是移除它。\n<w>R#</w>         ：创建一个具有特定半径的移动禁区，或是将其半径调整为#，\n                    #为1到8的数字。\n<w>E</w>          ：循环显示移动禁区。\n<w>Ctrl-E</w>     ：清除本层的所有移动禁区。\n\n<h>导航点</h>\n<w>W</w>          ：在当前位置创建导航点。\n<w>W</w>          ：循环显示本层的导航点。\n\n<h>循环显示地标</h>\n<w><<</w> or <w>></w>     ：循环显示上楼或下楼楼梯。\n<w>^</w>          ：循环显示陷阱。\n<w>\\</w>或<w>Tab</w>   ：循环显示商店和门户。\n<w>I</w>          ：循环正向显示所有物品。\n<w>O</w>          ：循环反向显示所有物品。\n<w>_</w>          ：循环显示祭坛。\n\n<h>其他</h>\n<w>v</w>          ：查看所示贴图的上次记录状态。\n<w>!</w>          ：注释楼层。\n<w>Ctrl-C</w>     ：清除怪物记录。\n<w>Ctrl-F</w>     ：忘记楼层地图。\n<w>Ctrl-U</w>     ：恢复被遗忘的楼层地图{{\nif you.wizard() then\n    return \"\\n\\n\"\n          .. \"<h>向导模式命令</h>\\n\"\n          .. \"<w>T</w>          ：传送到光标处。\\n\"\n          .. \"<w>Ctrl-X</w>     ：完全擦除地图\"\nend }}","current_english":"<h>Level Map ('<w>X</w>' in the main screen):</h>\n<w>Esc</w>        : Leave level map (also <w>Space</w> or any unused key).\n<w>Dir.</w>       : Move the cursor.\n<w>Shift-Dir.</w> : Move the cursor in larger steps (7 by default).\n<w>-</w> or <w>+</w>     : Scroll level map 20 grids up or down.\n<w>.</w>          : Start travel (also <w>Enter</w> and <w>,</w> and <w>;</w>).\n             (Moves cursor to the last travel destination if still on @.)\n<w>[</w> or <w>]</w>     : Examine the next higher or lower level.\n             (Moves cursor to the staircase destination if a known staircase is\n             highlighted.)\n<w>G</w>          : View another level by choosing a branch and depth.\n<w>o</w>          : Move the cursor to the next autoexplore target.\n<w>{</w> or <w>}</w>     : Zoom out or in (tiles only).\n\n<h>Travel exclusions</h>\n<w>e</w>          : Create a travel exclusion, change its radius, or remove it.\n<w>R#</w>         : Create a travel exclusion with a specified radius or change\n             its radius to #, where # is a number from 1 to 8.\n<w>E</w>          : Cycle through travel exclusions.\n<w>Ctrl-E</w>     : Erase all travel exclusions on the level.\n\n<h>Waypoints</h>\n<w>w</w>          : Set waypoint to current position.\n<w>W</w>          : Cycle through all waypoints on the level.\n\n<h>Cycle through features</h>\n<w><<</w> or <w>></w>     : Cycle through up or down stairs.\n<w>^</w>          : Cycle through traps.\n<w>\\</w> or <w>Tab</w>   : Cycle through shops and portals.\n<w>I</w>          : Cycle forward through all items.\n<w>O</w>          : Cycle backward through all items.\n<w>_</w>          : Cycle through altars.\n\n<h>Other</h>\n<w>v</w>          : Examine the last remembered state of the indicated tile.\n<w>!</w>          : Annotate level.\n<w>Ctrl-C</w>     : Clear monster memory.\n<w>Ctrl-F</w>     : Forget level map.\n<w>Ctrl-U</w>     : Restore forgotten level map.{{\nif you.wizard() then\n    return \"\\n\\n\"\n          .. \"<h>Wizard commands</h>\\n\"\n          .. \"<w>T</w>          : Teleport to cursor.\\n\"\n          .. \"<w>Ctrl-X</w>     : Completely wipe out map.\"\nend }}","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"map, search, travel, and annotation","display_context":"Specific HelpDB topic rendered by the formatted help        ：离开楼层地图（也可按<w>Space</w>或任意未使用的键）。\n<w>Dir.</w>       ：移动光标。\n<w>Shift-Dir.</w> ：大步移动光标（默认每次 7 格）。\n<w>-</w>或<w>+</w>     ：将楼层地图向上或向下滚动 20 格。\n<w>.</w>          ：开始自动旅行（也可按<w>Enter</w>、<w>,</w>或<w>;</w>）。\n             （如果光标仍在 @ 处，则将其移到上一次旅行的目的地。）\n<w>[</w>或<w>]</w>     ：查看相邻的上一层或下一层。\n             （如果当前高亮的是已知楼梯，则将光标移到该楼梯通往的位置。）\n<w>G</w>          ：选择分支和层数，查看另一个楼层。\n<w>o</w>          ：将光标移动到下一个自动探索目标。\n<w>{</w>或<w>}</w>     ：缩小或放大地图（仅限贴图版）。\n\n<h>旅行禁区</h>\n<w>e</w>          ：创建旅行禁区、调整其半径或将其移除。\n<w>R#</w>         ：创建指定半径的旅行禁区，或将其半径改为 #；\n                    # 为 1 到 8 的数字。\n<w>E</w>          ：循环查看旅行禁区。\n<w>Ctrl-E</w>     ：清除本层的所有旅行禁区。\n\n<h>导航点</h>\n<w>w</w>          ：在当前位置设置导航点。\n<w>W</w>          ：循环查看本层的所有导航点。\n\n<h>循环查看地图要素</h>\n<w><<</w>或<w>></w>     ：循环查看上行或下行楼梯。\n<w>^</w>          ：循环查看陷阱。\n<w>\\</w>或<w>Tab</w>   ：循环查看商店和传送门。\n<w>I</w>          ：正向循环查看所有物品。\n<w>O</w>          ：反向循环查看所有物品。\n<w>_</w>          ：循环查看祭坛。\n\n<h>其他</h>\n<w>v</w>          ：查看所指地图格最后一次记忆的状态。\n<w>!</w>          ：注释楼层。\n<w>Ctrl-C</w>     ：清除怪物记忆。\n<w>Ctrl-F</w>     ：遗忘楼层地图。\n<w>Ctrl-U</w>     ：恢复已遗忘的楼层地图。{{\nif you.wizard() then\n    return \"\\n\\n\"\n          .. \"<h>巫师模式命令</h>\\n\"\n          .. \"<w>T</w>          ：传送到光标位置。\\n\"\n          .. \"<w>Ctrl-X</w>     ：彻底清空地图。\"\nend }}","reentry_trigger":"对应英文源文、生产 consumer、格式约定或 docs/glossary.md 裁决发生变化时重新审阅。","rejected_alternatives":["仅作零散词语替换：跨段落的事实、列表或格式问题无法得到完整修复。","原样保留基线译文：已确认的语义或结构缺陷会继续展示给玩家。"],"reviewer_rationale":"基线对多项按键行为和条件化说明不自然或失真；完整重译并保持 Lua 比较字符串与控制骨架更可靠。","terminal_conclusion":"retranslate"}
{"actual_behavior":"宏菜单区分 keymap、macro 与 action；编辑结果保存到 Crawl 配置的宏文件，名称和位置依平台/构建，多数本地构建为 macro_dir 下的 macro.txt。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:531","dynamic_menu":["crawl-ref/source/menu.cc:1552","crawl-ref/source/menu.cc:1832"],"lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2341"},"current_chinese":"<h>编辑宏和键位</h>\n\n要编辑当前宏/键位，请用方向键或热键在菜单中选择。\n（可通过方向键选择用于定位菜单的热键）。要通过键入以创建新的键位，\n使用[<w>~</w>]键或相应的菜单项，然后输入该键。\n你可以通过它来创建（和编辑）使用菜单键的键位。\n使用[<w>!</w>]键切换到键位编辑模式（有关键位的详细信息，参见下文）。\n\n<w>Macros</w>可以在输入命令时重新修改传送到游戏的键位。\n这可用于重新设定命令键，或定义未使用的键。\n它们将一个键映射到一组按键，按键会传入到子菜单中。\n\n示例1：将<w>e</w> 映射到<w>p</w>会让“e”键触发与“p”键相同的命令\n（默认设定为自动开火）。\n\n示例2：将<w>1</w>映射到序列<w>zaf</w>\n会让“1”键字母朝默认目标施放标记为字母“a”的法术。\n\n示例3：将<w>F1</w>映射到 <w>Q&a</w>会让F1键预备标记为字母“a”的法术。\n\n<w>Keymaps</w>是较底层的键替换，用一系列按键击完全替换按键。\n建议使用它们来统一更改按键，以处理其他键盘布局。\n按键映射取决于情境，在非特定情境未激活的情况下，使用默认/\"regular\"情境。\n\n例如：在一些欧洲键盘布局中，§符号取代了~。因此，\n因此，在常规和菜单情境中将§映射为~会很有用，\n这样主菜单和宏菜单就更容易访问了。（在其他布局中，这适用于½。）\n\n当用于映射命令时，按键映射可能会产生意想不到的副作用：例如，\n不建议将<w>1</w>映射为<w>zaf</w>，因为这会使输入技能目标时很难键入“1”。\n\n<w> Keycodes</w>：宏和键映射在描述操作时都接受键码，\n以<w>\\{n}</w>的形式输入键码<w>n</w>。（使用<w>\\\\</w>输入反斜杠。）例如，\n使用<w>\\{9}</w> 为<w>tab</w>按钮输入键码。\n可以通过宏编辑菜单上的<w>~~</w>，使用键码绑定宏和键映射。\n\n<w>Saving</w>：在此菜单中编辑的绑定内容以.macro文件保存在save目录中。\n它们会被配置文件中的设定覆盖。\n参见options_guide.txt中的“macro”命令以获得更多信息。\n","current_english":"<h>Editing macros and keymaps</h>\n\nTo edit a current macro/keymap, select from the menu using the arrow keys or\nby hotkey. (Hotkeys that are used to navigate the menu can be selected by\narrow keys). To create a new mapping by entering a key, use the [<w>~</w>] key\nor the corresponding menu item, and then enter the key. This allows you to\ncreate (and edit) mappings that use menu keys. Use the [<w>!</w>] key to switch\nto keymap editing mode (see below for more information on keymaps).\n\n<w>Macros</w> allow remapping keys that are sent to the game when inputting\ncommands. This can be used to redefine a command key, or to define an unused\nkey. They map a key to a sequence of keystrokes, and keystrokes do carry over\ninto submenus.\n\nExample 1: mapping <w>e</w> to <w>p</w> would let the `e` key trigger the same\ncommand as the `p` key (which on the default settings is autofire).\n\nExample 2: mapping <w>1</w> to the sequence <w>zaf</w> would let the `1` key\ncast a spell that is on the letter `a` with default targeting.\n\nExample 3: mapping <w>F1</w> to <w>Q&a</w> would let the F1 key quiver the spell\nthat is on letter `a`.\n\n<w>Keymaps</w> are lower-level key substitutions that replace keys entirely with\na sequence of keystrokes. They are recommended for changing keys uniformly to\ndeal with alternative keyboard layouts. Keymaps are contextual, and the\ndefault/\"regular\" context is used anywhere that a non-specialised context is not\nactive. Macros take the result of keymaps as input.\n\nExample: In some European keyboard layouts, the § symbol replaces ~. Therefore,\nit could be useful to keymap § to ~ in both the regular and menu keymap contexts\nso that the main menu and macro menu become a bit easier to access. (In other\nlayouts, this applies instead to ½.)\n\nWhen used for mapping commands, keymaps can have unexpected side effects: e.g.\nmapping <w>1</w> to <w>zaf</w> via keymap is not recommended, because this will\nmake it hard to type `1` when entering a skill target.\n\n<w>Keycodes</w>: Both macros and keymaps accept keycodes when describing the\naction, in the form of <w>\\{n}</w> to enter keycode <w>n</w>. (Use <w>\\\\</w> to\nenter a backslash.) For example, use <w>\\{9}</w> to enter the keycode for the\n<w>tab</w> button. You can bind macros and keymaps by keycode by using <w>~~</w>\nat the macro edit menu.\n\n<w>Saving</w>: Bindings edited at this menu are saved in a .macro file in your\nsave directory. They will be overridden by settings in your options file. See\nthe `macro` command in options_guide.txt for more information.\n","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"macros, platform keys, and wizard","display_context":"Specific HelpDB topic rendered by the formatted help scroller.","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/macro.cc:1522","crawl-ref/source/macro.cc:1589","crawl-ref/source/dat/database/help.txt:609","crawl-ref/source/dat/database/zh/help.txt:579"],"fact_sha256":"45ebb6de3007410691e231d4411c57afff3c03c0f0db503053576162fb5070d8","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md@95eeacf9704e046c2010ef34859b750d2f8a1937ad87c4a86e8a404c98689407","identity":"help:macro-menu","lifecycle":"current-player-help","producer":[{"guards":[],"location":"crawl-ref/source/macro.cc:1522","path":"crawl-ref/source/macro.cc"},{"guards":[],"location":"crawl-ref/source/macro.cc:1589","path":"crawl-ref/source/macro.cc"}],"production_facts":{"chinese":"<h>编辑宏和键位</h>\n\n要编辑当前宏/键位，请用方向键或热键在菜单中选择。\n（可通过方向键选择用于定位菜单的热键）。要通过键入以创建新的键位，\n使用[<w>~</w>]键或相应的菜单项，然后输入该键。\n你可以通过它来创建（和编辑）使用菜单键的键位。\n使用[<w>!</w>]键切换到键位编辑模式（有关键位的详细信息，参见下文）。\n\n<w>Macros</w>可以在输入命令时重新修改传送到游戏的键位。\n这可用于重新设定命令键，或定义未使用的键。\n它们将一个键映射到一组按键，按键会传入到子菜单中。\n\n示例1：将<w>e</w> 映射到<w>p</w>会让“e”键触发与“p”键相同的命令\n（默认设定为自动开火）。\n\n示例2：将<w>1</w>映射到序列<w>zaf</w>\n会让“1”键字母朝默认目标施放标记为字母“a”的法术。\n\n示例3：将<w>F1</w>映射到 <w>Q&a</w>会让F1键预备标记为字母“a”的法术。\n\n<w>Keymaps</w>是较底层的键替换，用一系列按键击完全替换按键。\n建议使用它们来统一更改按键，以处理其他键盘布局。\n按键映射取决于情境，在非特定情境未激活的情况下，使用默认/\"regular\"情境。\n\n例如：在一些欧洲键盘布局中，§符号取代了~。因此，\n因此，在常规和菜单情境中将§映射为~会很有用，\n这样主菜单和宏菜单就更容易访问了。（在其他布局中，这适用于½。）\n\n当用于映射命令时，按键映射可能会产生意想不到的副作用：例如，\n不建议将<w>1</w>映射为<w>zaf</w>，因为这会使输入技能目标时很难键入“1”。\n\n<w> Keycodes</w>：宏和键映射在描述操作时都接受键码，\n以<w>\\{n}</w>的形式输入键码<w>n</w>。（使用<w>\\\\</w>输入反斜杠。）例如，\n使用<w>\\{9}</w> 为<w>tab</w>按钮输入键码。\n可以通过宏编辑菜单上的<w>~~</w>，使用键码绑定宏和键映射。\n\n<w>Saving</w>：在此菜单中编辑的绑定内容以.macro文件保存在save目录中。\n它们会被配置文件中的设定覆盖。\n参见options_guide.txt中的“macro”命令以获得更多信息。\n","chinese_key_line":579,"chinese_tokens":{"blank_line_bullet_count":0,"bullet_line_count":0,"lua":{"blocks":[],"errors":[]},"markup":{"balance_errors":[],"malformed":[],"tag_counts":{"</h>":1,"</w>":20,"<h>":1,"<w>":20},"tag_sequence":["<h>","</h>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>"],"technical_angle_counts":{},"technical_angle_sequence":[]},"numbers":["1","2","1","1","3","1","1","9"],"option_literals":[],"path_literals":[],"quoted_command_literals":[],"urls":[],"w_contents":["~","!","Macros","e","p","1","zaf","F1","Q&a","Keymaps","1","zaf"," Keycodes","\\{n}","n","\\\\","\\{9}","tab","~~","Saving"]},"consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:531","dynamic_menu":["crawl-ref/source/menu.cc:1552","crawl-ref/source/menu.cc:1832"],"lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2341"},"dependency_group":"macros, platform keys, and wizard","display_context":"Specific HelpDB topic rendered by the formatted help scroller.","english":"<h>Editing macros and keymaps</h>\n\nTo edit a current macro/keymap, select from the menu using the arrow keys or\nby hotkey. (Hotkeys that are used to navigate the menu can be selected by\narrow keys). To create a new mapping by entering a key, use the [<w>~</w>] key\nor the corresponding menu item, and then enter the key. This allows you to\ncreate (and edit) mappings that use menu keys. Use the [<w>!</w>] key to switch\nto keymap editing mode (see below for more information on keymaps).\n\n<w>Macros</w> allow remapping keys that are sent to the game when inputting\ncommands. This can be used to redefine a command key, or to define an unused\nkey. They map a key to a sequence of keystrokes, and keystrokes do carry over\ninto submenus.\n\nExample 1: mapping <w>e</w> to <w>p</w> would let the `e` key trigger the same\ncommand as the `p` key (which on the default settings is autofire).\n\nExample 2: mapping <w>1</w> to the sequence <w>zaf</w> would let the `1` key\ncast a spell that is on the letter `a` with default targeting.\n\nExample 3: mapping <w>F1</w> to <w>Q&a</w> would let the F1 key quiver the spell\nthat is on letter `a`.\n\n<w>Keymaps</w> are lower-level key substitutions that replace keys entirely with\na sequence of keystrokes. They are recommended for changing keys uniformly to\ndeal with alternative keyboard layouts. Keymaps are contextual, and the\ndefault/\"regular\" context is used anywhere that a non-specialised context is not\nactive. Macros take the result of keymaps as input.\n\nExample: In some European keyboard layouts, the § symbol replaces ~. Therefore,\nit could be useful to keymap § to ~ in both the regular and menu keymap contexts\nso that the main menu and macro menu become a bit easier to access. (In other\nlayouts, this applies instead to ½.)\n\nWhen used for mapping commands, keymaps can have unexpected side effects: e.g.\nmapping <w>1</w> to <w>zaf</w> via keymap is not recommended, because this will\nmake it hard to type `1` when entering a skill target.\n\n<w>Keycodes</w>: Both macros and keymaps accept keycodes when describing the\naction, in the form of <w>\\{n}</w> to enter keycode <w>n</w>. (Use <w>\\\\</w> to\nenter a backslash.) For example, use <w>\\{9}</w> to enter the keycode for the\n<w>tab</w> button. You can bind macros and keymaps by keycode by using <w>~~</w>\nat the macro edit menu.\n\n<w>Saving</w>: Bindings edited at this menu are saved in a .macro file in your\nsave directory. They will be overridden by settings in your options file. See\nthe `macro` command in options_guide.txt for more information.\n","english_key_line":609,"english_tokens":{"blank_line_bullet_count":0,"bullet_line_count":0,"lua":{"blocks":[],"errors":[]},"markup":{"balance_errors":[],"malformed":[],"tag_counts":{"</h>":1,"</w>":20,"<h>":1,"<w>":20},"tag_sequence":["<h>","</h>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>"],"technical_angle_counts":{},"technical_angle_sequence":[]},"numbers":["1","2","1","1","3","1","1","9"],"option_literals":[],"path_literals":["macro/","default/"],"quoted_command_literals":[],"urls":[],"w_contents":["~","!","Macros","e","p","1","zaf","F1","Q&a","Keymaps","1","zaf","Keycodes","\\{n}","n","\\\\","\\{9}","tab","~~","Saving"]},"fact_sha256":"45ebb6de3007410691e231d4411c57afff3c03c0f0db503053576162fb5070d8","identity":"help:macro-menu","key":"macro-menu","kind":"help","lifecycle":"current-player-help","producer":[{"guards":[],"location":"crawl-ref/source/macro.cc:1522","path":"crawl-ref/source/macro.cc"},{"guards":[],"location":"crawl-ref/source/macro.cc:1589","path":"crawl-ref/source/macro.cc"}],"structural_differences":["path_literals","w_contents"],"token_contract_equal":{"blank_line_bullet_count":true,"bullet_line_count":true,"format_tag_counts":true,"format_tag_sequence":true,"lua_block_count":true,"lua_comparison_literals":true,"lua_control_skeleton":true,"numbers":true,"option_literals":true,"path_literals":false,"quoted_command_literals":true,"technical_angle_counts":true,"technical_angle_sequence":true,"urls":true,"w_contents":false}},"proposed_translation":"<h>编辑宏和键映射</h>\n\n要编辑现有宏或键映射，请用方向键或热键在菜单中选择。\n（用来浏览菜单的热键项也可通过方向键选中。）要通过输入按键创建新映射，\n请按[<w>~</w>]或选择相应菜单项，再输入该键。\n这样也能创建（或编辑）使用菜单按键的映射。\n按[<w>!</w>]可切换到键映射编辑模式（键映射的详细说明见下文）。\n\n<w>Macros</w>可重新映射输入命令时发送给游戏的按键。\n宏可以重新定义命令键，也可以定义未使用的键。\n宏将一个按键映射为一串按键，而且这些按键会继续传入子菜单。\n\n示例 1：将<w>e</w>映射为<w>p</w>后，按“e”键会触发与“p”键相同的命令\n（默认设置中是自动射击）。\n\n示例 2：将<w>1</w>映射为按键序列<w>zaf</w>后，\n按“1”键会施放字母“a”对应的法术，并使用默认瞄准。\n\n示例 3：将<w>F1</w>映射为<w>Q&a</w>后，\n按F1键会把字母“a”对应的法术设为待发。\n\n<w>Keymaps</w>是更底层的按键替换，会把一个按键完全替换为一串按键。\n建议用键映射统一修改按键，以适配不同的键盘布局。\n键映射取决于情境；未启用特殊情境时，各处都使用默认的“regular”情境。\n系统先处理键映射，宏以键映射的结果为输入。\n\n例如，在某些欧洲键盘布局中，§符号取代了~。因此，\n在常规和菜单键映射情境中都把§映射为~会很有用，\n这样会更容易打开主菜单和宏菜单。（在其他布局中，对应的符号是½。）\n\n用键映射映射命令可能产生意外副作用。例如，不建议通过键映射\n将<w>1</w>映射为<w>zaf</w>，因为输入技能训练目标时会很难键入“1”。\n\n<w>Keycodes</w>：宏和键映射在描述操作时都接受键码，\n输入格式为<w>\\{n}</w>，表示键码<w>n</w>。（使用<w>\\\\</w>输入反斜杠。）\n例如，使用<w>\\{9}</w>输入<w>tab</w>键的键码。\n在宏编辑菜单中使用<w>~~</w>，可以按键码绑定宏和键映射。\n\n<w>Saving</w>：在此菜单编辑的绑定会写入 Crawl 配置的宏文件；\n文件名和位置取决于平台与构建。\n多数本地构建中，该文件是 macro_dir 下的 macro.txt。\noptions 文件中的设置会覆盖这些绑定。\n有关详情，请参阅 options_guide.txt 中的 macro 命令。\n","reentry_trigger":"对应英文源文、生产 consumer、格式约定或 docs/glossary.md 裁决发生变化时重新审阅。","rejected_alternatives":["仅作零散词语替换：跨段落的事实、列表或格式问题无法得到完整修复。","原样保留基线译文：已确认的语义或结构缺陷会继续展示给玩家。"],"reviewer_rationale":"基线对绑定类型和保存位置的说明不够准确；源事实同时更新后，整段重译能保持覆盖优先级和配置说明一致。","terminal_conclusion":"retranslate"}
{"actual_behavior":"拾取菜单支持选择、反选、查看、过滤、全部操作以及容器/负重相关显示，具体按键由现有菜单消费者解释。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:531","dynamic_menu":["crawl-ref/source/menu.cc:1552","crawl-ref/source/menu.cc:1832"],"lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2341"},"current_chinese":"                   <h>拾取和丢弃物品</h>\n\n<h>定位和选择物品</h>\n使用通常的定位键（<w>方向</w>键，<w>pgdn</w>/<w>pgup</w>、<w>home</w>、<w>end</w>）选择物品，\n使用<w>空格</w>/<w>.</w>切换所选物品的拾取或丢弃状态。\n按下物品的字母键也将切换其拾取/丢弃状态。\n装备、药水、卷轴和可激活物品分别位于不同页面，可用<w>左方向键</w>和<w>右方向键</w>切换。\n按<w>回车</w>确认当前选择的物品。\n\n在按字母选择物品之前输入数字，可以指定要操作的那堆物品的数量。\n\n<h>正规表示式</h>\n你可以按<w>Ctrl-F</w>输入一个单词（或一个正则表达式）。\n所匹配的物品都会被选定。\n\n<h>各类物品的快捷键</h>\n你可以按以下键快速选择某类物品：\n<w>!</w>   药水      <w>(</w>   武器        <w>)</w>   弹药      <w>[</w>   防具\n<w>/</w>   魔杖      <w>|</w>   法杖        <w>?</w>   卷轴      <w>:</w>   书\n<w>\"</w>   项链      <w>=</w>   戒指        <w>}</w>   杂项\n全局选择：<w>,</w>   全选     <w>*</w>   反选      <w>-</w>   取消全选\n          <w>;</w>   选择最后一个未装备的\n请注意，对于丢弃，<w>,</w>命令使用了<w>drop_filter</w>选项，\n那会缩小（取消）选择物品的范围。默认为所有无用物品。\n\n<h>通过丢弃菜单禁用自动拾取</h>\n在丢弃菜单上，按<w>\\</w>后选择的物品会被标记为<w>*</w>，\n而不是通常的<w>+</w>。\n当你离开这个菜单后，这些物品不会再被自动拾取。\n要返回正常选择模式，请再次按<w>\\</w>。","current_english":"                   <h>Picking up and dropping items</h>\n\n<h>Navigation and selecting items</h>\nUse the usual navigation keys (<w>arrow</w> keys, <w>pgdn</w>/<w>pgup</w>, <w>home</w>, <w>end</w>) to select\nitems, and <w>Space</w>/<w>.</w> to toggle whether the selected item will be picked up or\ndropped. Pressing an item's letter key will also toggle its pickup/drop state.\nGear, potions, scrolls, and evocable items are on separate pages which can be\nswitched between by <w>left arrow</w> and <w>right arrow</w>.\nPress <w>Return</w> to accept the current set of items.\n\nEntering a number before selecting by letter will toggle just that quantity of\na stack of items.\n\n<h>Regular expressions</h>\nYou can press <w>Ctrl-F</w> to enter a word (or a regular expression). All matching\nitems will be selected.\n\n<h>Category shortcuts</h>\nYou can quickly select items by type by pressing:\n<w>!</w>   potions      <w>(</w>   weapons        <w>)</w>   ammunition      <w>[</w>   armour\n<w>/</w>   wands        <w>|</w>   magical staves <w>?</w>   scrolls         <w>:</w>   books\n<w>\"</w>   amulets      <w>=</w>   rings          <w>}</w>   miscellaneous\nGlobal selects:  <w>,</w>   select all     <w>*</w>   invert all      <w>-</w>   deselect all\n                 <w>;</w>   select last unequipped\nNote that for dropping, the <w>,</w> command uses the <w>drop_filter</w> option, which\nnarrows the range of items to be (de)selected. The default is all useless items.","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"items, pickup, and known-items menus","display_context":"Specific HelpDB topic rendered by the formatted help                   <h>拾取和丢弃物品</h>\n\n<h>定位和选择物品</h>\n使用通常的定位键（<w>方向</w>键，<w>pgdn</w>/<w>pgup</w>、<w>home</w>、<w>end</w>）选择物品，\n使用<w>空格</w>/<w>.</w>切换所选物品的拾取或丢弃状态。\n按下物品的字母键也将切换其拾取/丢弃状态。\n装备、药水、卷轴和可激活物品分别位于不同页面，可用<w>左方向键</w>和<w>右方向键</w>切换。\n按<w>回车</w>确认当前选择的物品。\n\n在按字母选择物品之前输入数字，可以指定要操作的那堆物品的数量。\n\n<h>正规表示式</h>\n你可以按<w>Ctrl-F</w>输入一个单词（或一个正则表达式）。\n所匹配的物品都会被选定。\n\n<h>各类物品的快捷键</h>\n你可以按以下键快速选择某类物品：\n<w>!</w>   药水      <w>(</w>   武器        <w>)</w>   弹药      <w>[</w>   防具\n<w>/</w>   魔杖      <w>|</w>   法杖        <w>?</w>   卷轴      <w>:</w>   书\n<w>\"</w>   项链      <w>=</w>   戒指        <w>}</w>   杂项\n全局选择：<w>,</w>   全选     <w>*</w>   反选      <w>-</w>   取消全选\n          <w>;</w>   选择最后一个未装备的\n请注意，对于丢弃，<w>,</w>命令使用了<w>drop_filter</w>选项，\n那会缩小（取消）选择物品的范围。默认为所有无用物品。\n\n<h>通过丢弃菜单禁用自动拾取</h>\n在丢弃菜单上，按<w>\\</w>后选择的物品会被标记为<w>*</w>，\n而不是通常的<w>+</w>。\n当你离开这个菜单后，这些物品不会再被自动拾取。\n要返回正常选择模式，请再次按<w>\\</w>。","chinese_key_line":2,"chinese_tokens":{"blank_line_bullet_count":0,"bullet_line_count":0,"lua":{"blocks":[],"errors":[]},"markup":{"balance_errors":[],"malformed":[],"tag_counts":{"</h>":5,"</w>":32,"<h>":5,"<w>":32},"tag_sequence":["<h>","</h>","<h>","</h>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<h>","</h>","<w>","</w>","<h>","</h>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<h>","</h>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>"],"technical_angle_counts":{},"technical_angle_sequence":[]},"numbers":[],"option_literals":[],"path_literals":[],"quoted_command_literals":[],"urls":[],"w_contents":["方向","pgdn","pgup","home","end","空格",".","左方向键","右方向键","回车","Ctrl-F","!","(",")","[","/","|","?",":","\"","=","}",",","*","-",";",",","drop_filter","\\","*","+","\\"]},"consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:531","dynamic_menu":["crawl-ref/source/menu.cc:1552","crawl-ref/source/menu.cc:1832"],"lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2341"},"dependency_group":"items,                   <h>拾取和丢弃物品</h>\n\n<h>定位和选择物品</h>\n使用通常的定位键（<w>方向</w>键，<w>pgdn</w>/<w>pgup</w>、<w>home</w>、<w>end</w>）选择物品，\n使用<w>空格</w>/<w>.</w>切换所选物品的拾取或丢弃状态。\n按下物品的字母键也将切换其拾取/丢弃状态。\n装备、药水、卷轴和可激活物品分别位于不同页面，可用<w>左方向键</w>和<w>右方向键</w>切换。\n按<w>回车</w>确认当前选择的物品。\n\n在按字母选择物品之前输入数字，可以指定要操作的那堆物品的数量。\n\n<h>正则表达式</h>\n你可以按<w>Ctrl-F</w>输入一个单词（或一个正则表达式）。\n所匹配的物品都会被选定。\n\n<h>各类物品的快捷键</h>\n你可以按以下键快速选择某类物品：\n<w>!</w>   药水      <w>(</w>   武器        <w>)</w>   弹药      <w>[</w>   护甲\n<w>/</w>   魔杖      <w>|</w>   法杖        <w>?</w>   卷轴      <w>:</w>   书\n<w>\"</w>   项链      <w>=</w>   戒指        <w>}</w>   杂项\n全局选择：<w>,</w>   全选     <w>*</w>   反选      <w>-</w>   取消全选\n          <w>;</w>   选择最后一个未装备的\n请注意，在丢弃物品时，<w>,</w>命令会应用<w>drop_filter</w>选项，\n缩小可选择或取消选择的物品范围。默认范围是所有无用物品。","reentry_trigger":"对应英文源文、生产 consumer、格式约定或 docs/glossary.md 裁决发生变化时重新审阅。","rejected_alternatives":["原样保留基线译文：已确认的局部歧义、遗漏或术语问题仍会存在。","整项重译：问题范围局部，扩大改写会增加不必要的回归面。"],"reviewer_rationale":"基线存在局部动作和对象范围不清；按实际菜单行为调整措辞即可，无需改变结构。","terminal_conclusion":"adjust"}
{"actual_behavior":"技能菜单可启停训练、设置目标并切换显示训练百分比、技能成本或目标；目标达成后训练会自动停用。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:531","dynamic_menu":["crawl-ref/source/menu.cc:1552","crawl-ref/source/menu.cc:1832"],"lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2341"},"current_chinese":"                        <h>技能管理</h>\n\n你可以通过杀死怪物（及很少的其他一些活动）获得经验值。\n经验值被用来训练技能。\n在技能界面，你可以选择技能进行训练。\n\n通过按下字母，你可以循环调整是否及如何训练技能：\n  <darkgrey>- 取消选择</darkgrey>  不会训练此技能。\n  + 选择      会训练此技能\n  <w>* 聚焦</w>      此技能在训练时会有更高权重。\n\n按下大写字母可选择此技能并取消选择其他所有技能。\n当你只想训练营一种技能时，这个快捷键非常有用。\n\n训练可在两种模式下进行，通过<w>/</w>键切换。\n  - 自动：    技能权重取决于你对它们的使用。\n  - 手动：    经验会被均匀分配给所有激活的技能。\n\n在训练目标模式下，你可以设置技能的训练目标：\n在该技能达到某一等级后将不会自动训练它。\n\n训练技能的速度取决于以下因素：\n  - 天赋：在Apt栏中以<white>白色</white>显示。\n数值越高则意味着升级某一技能所需的经验越少。这取决于种族。\n  - 技能手册：这种罕见的书能为一个技能提供<lightred>+4</lightred>的天赋加成。\n\n除此之外，还有一些效果能直接调整技能，\n例如训练了相关技能（交叉训练）或某些神力。\n削弱的技能以<magenta>洋红色</magenta>显示，\n增强的技能以<green>绿色</green>显示。\n训练百分比（以<brown>棕色</brown>）显示了用于训练该技能的经验值比例。\n\n除了（取消）选择技能外，还可以进行以下切换：\n  <w>/</w> ：在自动和手动训练模式之间进行切换。\n  <w>*</w> ：显示所有技能，即使是那些目前无用的技能。\n  <w>?</w> ：阅读技能描述。\n  <w>_</w> ：显示或隐藏技能等级的调整（神力、交叉训练）。\n  <w>!</w> ：切换显示技能百分比、技能消耗或训练目标。","current_english":"                        <h>Skill management</h>\n\nYou gain experience for killing monsters (and very few other activities). This\nexperience is used to train skills. The skill screen allows you to select which\nskills to train.\n\nBy pressing its letter, you can cycle if and how a skill is trained:\n  <darkgrey>- deselected</darkgrey>    Skill will not be trained.\n  + selected      Skill will be trained.\n  <w>* focused</w>       Skill will be weighted higher for training.\n\nPressing a capital letter selects that skill and deselects all the others.\nThis shortcut is useful when you want to train only one skill.\n\nTraining can be done in two modes, which are toggled with the <w>/</w> key.\n  - automatic:    Skills are weighted based on your use of them.\n  - manual:       Experience is spread evenly across all activated skills.\n\nIn training target mode you can set skill training targets: target skill levels\nat which training of the skill will be automatically disabled.\n\nHow fast you train a skill is governed by the following factors:\n  - aptitude: Shown <white>white</white> in the Apt column. Higher numbers mean that less\n              experience is needed to advance a skill. Depends on species.\n  - skill manuals: Rare books giving an aptitude bonus of <lightred>+4</lightred> in one skill.\n\nOn top of that, there are some effects which directly modify a skill, for\nexample having trained a related skill (crosstraining), or certain god powers.\nReduced skills are shown in <magenta>magenta</magenta>, and enhanced skills in <green>green</green>.\nThe training percentage (in <brown>brown</brown>) shows the relative amount of experience\nwhich will be used to train this skill.\n\nApart from (de)selecting skills, you also have the following toggles:\n  <w>/</w> :  Switch between automatic and manual training modes.\n  <w>*</w> :  Display all skills, even those which aren't currently useful.\n  <w>?</w> :  Allows you to read the descriptions of skills.\n  <w>_</w> :  Show or hide skill level modifications (god powers, crosstraining).\n  <w>!</w> :  Switch between displaying training percentage, skill cost, or training\n       targets.","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"skills and spell library","display_context":"Specific HelpDB topic rendered by the formatted help scroller.","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/command.cc:589","crawl-ref/source/dat/database/help.txt:245","crawl-ref/source/dat/database/zh/help.txt:241"],"fact_sha256":"ef187c87dd5d8cc3f56b7eacae1c269ce6d52cb98263668fe6b0b9e33f33e431","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md@95eeacf9704e046c2010ef34859b750d2f8a1937ad87c4a86e8a404c98689407","identity":"help:skill-menu","lifecycle":"current-player-help","producer":[{"guards":[],"location":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:589","path":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc"}],"production_facts":{"chinese":"                        <h>技能管理</h>\n\n你可以通过杀死怪物（及很少的其他一些活动）获得经验值。\n经验值被用来训练技能。\n在技能界面，你可以选择技能进行训练。\n\n通过按下字母，你可以循环调整是否及如何训练技能：\n  <darkgrey>- 取消选择</darkgrey>  不会训练此技能。\n  + 选择      会训练此技能\n  <w>* 聚焦</w>      此技能在训练时会有更高权重。\n\n按下大写字母可选择此技能并取消选择其他所有技能。\n当你只想训练营一种技能时，这个快捷键非常有用。\n\n训练可在两种模式下进行，通过<w>/</w>键切换。\n  - 自动：    技能权重取决于你对它们的使用。\n  - 手动：    经验会被均匀分配给所有激活的技能。\n\n在训练目标模式下，你可以设置技能的训练目标：\n在该技能达到某一等级后将不会自动训练它。\n\n训练技能的速度取决于以下因素：\n  - 天赋：在Apt栏中以<white>白色</white>显示。\n数值越高则意味着升级某一技能所需的经验越少。这取决于种族。\n  - 技能手册：这种罕见的书能为一个技能提供<lightred>+4</lightred>的天赋加成。\n\n除此之外，还有一些效果能直接调整技能，\n例如训练了相关技能（交叉训练）或某些神力。\n削弱的技能以<magenta>洋红色</magenta>显示，\n增强的技能以<green>绿色</green>显示。\n训练百分比（以<brown>棕色</brown>）显示了用于训练该技能的经验值比例。\n\n除了（取消）选择技能外，还可以进行以下切换：\n  <w>/</w> ：在自动和手动训练模式之间进行切换。\n  <w>*</w> ：显示所有技能，即使是那些目前无用的技能。\n  <w>?</w> ：阅读技能描述。\n  <w>_</w> ：显示或隐藏技能等级的调整（神力、交叉训练）。\n  <w>!</w> ：切换显示技能百分比、技能消耗或训练目标。","chinese_key_line":241,"chinese_tokens":{"blank_line_bullet_count":0,"bullet_line_count":0,"lua":{"blocks":[],"errors":[]},"markup":{"balance_errors":[],"malformed":[],"tag_counts":{"</brown>":1,"</darkgrey>":1,"</green>":1,"</h>":1,"</lightred>":1,"</magenta>":1,"</w>":7,"</white>":1,"<brown>":1,"<darkgrey>":1,"<green>":1,"<h>":1,"<lightred>":1,"<magenta>":1,"<w>":7,"<white>":1},"tag_sequence":["<h>","</h>","<darkgrey>","</darkgrey>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<white>","</white>","<lightred>","</lightred>","<magenta>","</magenta>","<green>","</green>","<brown>","</brown>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>"],"technical_angle_counts":{},"technical_angle_sequence":[]},"numbers":["4"],"option_literals":[],"path_literals":[],"quoted_command_literals":[],"urls":[],"w_contents":["* 聚焦","/","/","*","?","_","!"]},"consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:531","dynamic_menu":["crawl-ref/source/menu.cc:1552","crawl-ref/source/menu.cc:1832"],"lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2341"},"dependency_group":"skills and spell library","display_context":"Specific HelpDB topic rendered by the formatted help scroller.","english":"                        <h>Skill management</h>\n\nYou gain experience for killing monsters (and very few other activities). The skill screen allows you to select which\nskills to train.\n\nBy pressing its letter, you can cycle if and how a skill is trained:\n  <darkgrey>- deselected</darkgrey>    Skill will not be trained.\n  + selected      Skill will be trained.\n  <w>* focused</w>       Skill will be weighted higher for training.\n\nPressing a capital letter selects that skill and deselects all the others.\nThis shortcut is useful when you want to train only one skill.\n\nTraining can be done in two modes, which are toggled with the <w>/</w> key.\n  - automatic:    Skills are weighted based on your use of them.\n  - manual:       Experience is spread evenly across all activated skills.\n\nIn training target mode you can set skill training targets: target skill levels\nat which training of the skill will be automatically disabled.\n\nHow fast you train a skill is governed by the following factors:\n  - aptitude: Shown <white>white</white> in the Apt column. Depends on species.\n  - skill manuals: Rare books giving an aptitude bonus of <lightred>+4</lightred> in one skill.\n\nOn top of that, there are some effects which directly modify a skill, for\nexample having trained a related skill (crosstraining), or certain god powers.\nReduced skills are shown in <magenta>magenta</magenta>, and enhanced skills in <green>green</green>.\nThe training percentage (in <brown>brown</brown>) shows the relative amount of experience\nwhich will be used to train this skill.\n\nApart from (de)selecting skills, you also have the following toggles:\n focused","/","/","*","?","_","!"]},"fact_sha256":"ef187c87dd5d8cc3f56b7eacae1c269ce6d52cb98263668fe6b0b9e33f33e431","identity":"help:skill-menu","key":"skill-menu","kind":"help","lifecycle":"current-player-help","producer":[{"guards":[],"location":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:589","path":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc"}],"structural_differences":["w_contents"],"token_contract_equal":{"blank_line_bullet_count":true,"bullet_line_count":true,"format_tag_counts":true,"format_tag_sequence":true,"lua_block_count":true,"lua_comparison_literals":true,"lua_control_skeleton":true,"numbers":true,"option_literals":true,"path_literals":true,"quoted_command_literals":true,"technical_angle_counts":true,"technical_angle_sequence":true,"urls":true,"w_contents":false}},"proposed_translation":"                        <h>技能管理</h>\n\n你可以通过杀死怪物（及很少的其他一些活动）获得经验值。\n经验值被用来训练技能。\n在技能界面，你可以选择技能进行训练。\n\n通过按下字母，你可以循环调整是否及如何训练技能：\n  <darkgrey>- 取消选择</darkgrey>  不会训练此技能。\n  + 选择      会训练此技能\n  <w>* 聚焦</w>      此技能在训练时会有更高权重。\n\n按下大写字母可选择此技能并取消选择其他所有技能。\n当你只想训练一种技能时，这个快捷键非常有用。\n\n训练可在两种模式下进行，通过<w>/</w>键切换。\n  - 自动：    技能权重取决于你对它们的使用。\n  - 手动：    经验会被均匀分配给所有激活的技能。\n\n在训练目标模式下，你可以设置技能的训练目标：\n达到目标等级后，系统会自动停用该技能的训练。\n\n训练技能的速度取决于以下因素：\n  - 天赋：在Apt栏中以<white>白色</white>显示。\n数值越高则意味着升级某一技能所需的经验越少。这取决于种族。\n  - 技能手册：这种罕见的书能为一个技能提供<lightred>+4</lightred>的天赋加成。\n\n除此之外，还有一些效果能直接调整技能，\n例如训练了相关技能（交叉训练）或某些神力。\n削弱的技能以<magenta>洋红色</magenta>显示，\n增强的技能以<green>绿色</green>显示。\n训练百分比（以<brown>棕色</brown>显示）表示用于该技能的经验值占比。\n\n除了（取消）选择技能外，还可以进行以下切换：\n  <w>/</w> ：在自动和手动训练模式之间进行切换。\n  <w>*</w> ：显示所有技能，即使是那些目前无用的技能。\n  <w>?</w> ：阅读技能描述。\n  <w>_</w> ：显示或隐藏技能等级的调整（神力、交叉训练）。\n  <w>!</w> ：切换显示训练百分比、技能成本或训练目标。","reentry_trigger":"对应英文源文、生产 consumer、格式约定或 docs/glossary.md 裁决发生变化时重新审阅。","rejected_alternatives":["原样保留基线译文：已确认的局部歧义、遗漏或术语问题仍会存在。","整项重译：问题范围局部，扩大改写会增加不必要的回归面。"],"reviewer_rationale":"基线漏掉目标达成后的自动停用含义，并把若干显示名译得不精确；局部调整即可。","terminal_conclusion":"adjust"}
{"actual_behavior":"法术库用 Tab/! 与 Shift-Tab 双向切换记忆、描述、隐藏、显示页面；Ctrl-F 过滤并可用空输入清除过滤器。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:531","dynamic_menu":["crawl-ref/source/menu.cc:1552","crawl-ref/source/menu.cc:1832"],"lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2341"},"current_chinese":"<h>法术库</h>\n<w>!</w>        ：循环切换<w>记忆</w>、<w>描述</w>、<w>隐藏</w>和<w>显示</w>页面。\n<w>Ctrl-F</w>   ：使用正则表达式过滤列出的法术。\n<w>?</w>        ：显示帮助。\n\n默认情况下，法术库会显示<w>记忆</w>页面，要记忆一道咒语，只需按下它的字母。\n要阅读法术的描述，请先按<w>!</w>切换到<w>描述</w>页面，然后按法术的字母。\n\n大多数角色用不着记忆他们法术库中积累的大部分法术，\n这样，<w>显示</w>和<w>隐藏</w>页面就能派上用场。\n要将一道法术从<w>记忆</w>和<w>描述</w>页面中隐去，\n请在<w>隐藏</w>页面按下那个法术的字母。\n被隐藏的法术可以从<w>显示</w>页面再次显示。\n\n<w>auto_hide_spells</w>可能会很有用；启用时，默认会隐藏所有新发现的法术。\n\n<w>Ctrl-F</w>可以用来过滤列出的法术。\n以这种方式设置的任何过滤器都将持续存在，直到关闭并重新打开法术库，\n或按<w>Ctrl-F</w>设置新过滤器。\n如果在提示后按下<w>Enter</w>而不输入任何文本，则会将过滤器重制为空。\n你可以按名称和法术流派过滤法术。","current_english":"<h>Spell Library</h>\n<w>Tab</w> or <w>!</w>  : Cycle forward through <w>Memorise</w>, <w>Describe</w>, <w>Hide</w>, and <w>Show</w> pages.\n<w>Shift-Tab</w> : Cycle backward through these pages.\n<w>Ctrl-F</w>    : Filter the list of spells shown with a regex.\n<w>?</w>         : Show this help.\n\nBy default, the spell library opens to the <w>Memorise</w> page; to begin memorising a\nspell, just press its letter. To read a spell's description, first switch to\nthe <w>Describe</w> page by pressing <w>Tab</w>, then press the spell's letter.\n\nMost characters will not be interested in memorising the majority of spells that\naccumulate in their spell library; this is where the <w>Show</w> and <w>Hide</w> pages come in\nhandy. To hide a spell from the <w>Memorise</w> and <w>Describe</w> pages, press its spell\nletter from the <w>Hide</w> page. Hidden spells can be shown again from the <w>Show</w> page.\n\nThe <w>auto_hide_spells</w> option can be useful; when enabled, all newly discovered\nspells are hidden by default.\n\n<w>Ctrl-F</w> can be used to filter the list of spells. Any filter set in this way will\npersist until the spell library is closed and reopened, or until a new filter is\nset with <w>Ctrl-F</w>. If <w>Enter</w> is pressed at the prompt without entering any text,\nthat will reset the filter to none.\nYou can filter spells by name and by spell school.","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"skills and spell library","display_context":"Specific HelpDB topic rendered by the formatted help scroller.","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/command.cc:603","crawl-ref/source/command.cc:609","crawl-ref/source/dat/database/help.txt:287","crawl-ref/source/dat/database/zh/help.txt:282"],"fact_sha256":"ee9f05400073ed7e6a33815880275da28c6709df6e2201d5e81b43aa18d9d25e","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md@95eeacf9704e046c2010ef34859b750d2f8a1937ad87c4a86e8a404c98689407","identity":"help:spell-library","lifecycle":"current-player-help","producer":[{"guards":[],"location":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:603","path":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc"},{"guards":[],"location":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:609","path":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc"}],"production_facts":{"chinese":"<h>法术库</h>\n<w>!</w>        ：循环切换<w>记忆</w>、<w>描述</w>、<w>隐藏</w>和<w>显示</w>页面。\n<w>Ctrl-F</w>   ：使用正则表达式过滤列出的法术。\n<w>?</w>        ：显示帮助。\n\n默认情况下，法术库会显示<w>记忆</w>页面，要记忆一道咒语，只需按下它的字母。\n要阅读法术的描述，请先按<w>!</w>切换到<w>描述</w>页面，然后按法术的字母。\n\n大多数角色用不着记忆他们法术库中积累的大部分法术，\n这样，<w>显示</w>和<w>隐藏</w>页面就能派上用场。\n要将一道法术从<w>记忆</w>和<w>描述</w>页面中隐去，\n请在<w>隐藏</w>页面按下那个法术的字母。\n被隐藏的法术可以从<w>显示</w>页面再次显示。\n\n<w>auto_hide_spells</w>可能会很有用；启用时，默认会隐藏所有新发现的法术。\n\n<w>Ctrl-F</w>可以用来过滤列出的法术。\n以这种方式设置的任何过滤器都将持续存在，直到关闭并重新打开法术库，\n或按<w>Ctrl-F</w>设置新过滤器。\n如果在提示后按下<w>Enter</w>而不输入任何文本，则会将过滤器重制为空。\n你可以按名称和法术流派过滤法术。","chinese_key_line":282,"chinese_tokens":{"blank_line_bullet_count":0,"bullet_line_count":0,"lua":{"blocks":[],"errors":[]},"markup":{"balance_errors":[],"malformed":[],"tag_counts":{"</h>":1,"</w>":20,"<h>":1,"<w>":20},"tag_sequence":["<h>","</h>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>"],"technical_angle_counts":{},"technical_angle_sequence":[]},"numbers":[],"option_literals":[],"path_literals":[],"quoted_command_literals":[],"urls":[],"w_contents":["!","记忆","描述","隐藏","显示","Ctrl-F","?","记忆","!","描述","显示","隐藏","记忆","描述","隐藏","显示","auto_hide_spells","Ctrl-F","Ctrl-F","Enter"]},"consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:531","dynamic_menu":["crawl-ref/source/menu.cc:1552","crawl-ref/source/menu.cc:1832"],"lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2341"},"dependency_group":"skills and spell library","display_context":"Specific HelpDB topic rendered by the formatted help scroller.","english":"<h>Spell Library</h>\n<w>Tab</w> or <w>!</w>  : Cycle forward through <w>Memorise</w>, <w>Describe</w>, <w>Hide</w>, and <w>Show</w> pages.\n<w>Shift-Tab</w> : Cycle backward through these pages.\n<w>Ctrl-F</w>    : Filter the list of spells shown with a regex.\n<w>?</w>         : Show this help.\n\nBy default, the spell library opens to the <w>Memorise</w> page; to begin memorising a\nspell, just press its letter. To read a spell's description, first switch to\nthe <w>Describe</w> page by pressing <w>Tab</w>, then press the spell's letter.\n\nMost characters will not be interested in memorising the majority of spells that\naccumulate in their spell library; this is where the <w>Show</w> and <w>Hide</w> pages come in\nhandy. To hide a spell from the <w>Memorise</w> and <w>Describe</w> pages, press its spell\nletter from the <w>Hide</w> page. Hidden spells can be shown again from the <w>Show</w> page.\n\nThe <w>auto_hide_spells</w> option can be useful; when enabled, all newly discovered\nspells are hidden by default.\n\n<w>Ctrl-F</w> can be used to filter the list of spells. Any filter set in this way will\npersist until the spell library is closed and reopened, or until a new filter is\nset with <w>Ctrl-F</w>. If <w>Enter</w> is pressed at the prompt without entering any text,\nthat will reset the filter to none.\nYou can filter spells by name and by spell school.","english_key_line":287,"english_tokens":{"blank_line_bullet_count":0,"bullet_line_count":0,"lua":{"blocks":[],"errors":[]},"markup":{"balance_errors":[],"malformed":[],"tag_counts":{"</h>":1,"</w>":22,"<h>":1,"<w>":22},"tag_sequence":["<h>","</h>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>"],"technical_angle_counts":{},"technical_angle_sequence":[]},"numbers":[],"option_literals":[],"path_literals":[],"quoted_command_literals":[],"urls":[],"w_contents":["Tab","!","Memorise","Describe","Hide","Show","Shift-Tab","Ctrl-F","?","Memorise","Describe","Tab","Show","Hide","Memorise","Describe","Hide","Show","auto_hide_spells","Ctrl-F","Ctrl-F","Enter"]},"fact_sha256":"ee9f05400073ed7e6a33815880275da28c6709df6e2201d5e81b43aa18d9d25e","identity":"help:spell-library","key":"spell-library","kind":"help","lifecycle":"current-player-help","producer":[{"guards":[],"location":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:603","path":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc"},{"guards":[],"location":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:609","path":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc"}],"structural_differences":["format_tag_counts","format_tag_sequence","w_contents"],"token_contract_equal":{"blank_line_bullet_count":true,"bullet_line_count":true,"format_tag_counts":false,"format_tag_sequence":false,"lua_block_count":true,"lua_comparison_literals":true,"lua_control_skeleton":true,"numbers":true,"option_literals":true,"path_literals":true,"quoted_command_literals":true,"technical_angle_counts":true,"technical_angle_sequence":true,"urls":true,"w_contents":false}},"proposed_translation":"<h>法术库</h>\n<w>Tab</w>或<w>!</w>  ：正向循环切换<w>记忆</w>、<w>描述</w>、<w>隐藏</w>和<w>显示</w>页面。\n<w>Shift-Tab</w> ：反向循环切换这些页面。\n<w>Ctrl-F</w>    ：使用正则表达式筛选显示的法术。\n<w>?</w>         ：显示此帮助。\n\n默认情况下，法术库会打开<w>记忆</w>页面；要开始记忆某个法术，只需按其字母。\n要阅读法术描述，请先按<w>Tab</w>切换到<w>描述</w>页面，再按该法术的字母。\n\n大多数角色不会想记忆法术库中积累的大多数法术；\n这时<w>显示</w>和<w>隐藏</w>页面就很有用。\n要从<w>记忆</w>和<w>描述</w>页面隐藏某个法术，\n请在<w>隐藏</w>页面按该法术的字母。\n被隐藏的法术可以在<w>显示</w>页面重新显示。\n\n<w>auto_hide_spells</w>选项可能很有用；启用后，新发现的法术默认都会隐藏。\n\n<w>Ctrl-F</w>可用于筛选法术列表。\n以这种方式设置的筛选条件会一直保留，直到关闭并重新打开法术库，\n或再次按<w>Ctrl-F</w>设置新的筛选条件。\n如果在提示处不输入任何文本直接按<w>Enter</w>，就会清除筛选。\n你可以按法术名称和法术学派筛选法术。","reentry_trigger":"对应英文源文、生产 consumer、格式约定或 docs/glossary.md 裁决发生变化时重新审阅。","rejected_alternatives":["原样保留基线译文：已确认的局部歧义、遗漏或术语问题仍会存在。","整项重译：问题范围局部，扩大改写会增加不必要的回归面。"],"reviewer_rationale":"基线遗漏 Shift-Tab、误把页面切换只归于 `!`，且过滤器清除说明欠准；结构性内容仍可局部修复。","terminal_conclusion":"adjust"}
{"actual_behavior":"储藏搜索支持名称、正则、属性、地标、范围和逻辑运算符，并可从结果查看物品/商店或前往位置。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:531","dynamic_menu":["crawl-ref/source/menu.cc:1552","crawl-ref/source/menu.cc:1832"],"lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2341"},"current_chinese":"<h>追踪</h>\n\n你可以按名称（或被包含在名称中的子字符串）搜索物品和地牢地标。\n在搜索结果列表中，你可以通过热键选择一个搜索结果以移动到其位置。\n\n搜索字符串的一些示例：\n<w>mutation</w>      找出所有的变异药水，包括在商店中的。\n<w>throwing net</w>  找出单独的一个或堆叠的投掷网。\n<w>shop</w>          列出所有商店。按<w>!</w>再按商店的字母可查看库存。\n<w>Lair:2</w>        找出所有位于兽穴：2的已知东西。\n<w>/D:[2-4]</w>      找出所有位于地牢第2-4层的东西。可以使用正则表达式！\n              注意，/D:[3-10]将无法按预期运作，因为[x-y]是一个\n              正则表达式的字符范围。\n<w>rPois</w>         找出所有具有rPois抗性的物品。\n<w>rC++</w>          找出所有具有rC++抗性的物品。\n<w>+3</w>            找出所有具有+3附魔的物品。\n<w>/Lair.*axe</w>和<w>axe && Lair</w>都能显示所有位于兽穴的斧子。\n\n一些快捷键：\n<w>.</w>             列出当前层的所有东西。\n<w>..</w>            列出已知的所有物品。\n<w>0</w> to <w>9</w>        列出指定导航点上的所有物品。\n<w>*</w>             列出所有导航点上的所有物品。\n<w>@<<query></w>      列出当前层上所有匹配<<query>的东西。\n<w>/<<regex></w>      列出匹配<<regex>这一正则表达式的物品。\n<w>=<<string></w>     列出所有包含<<string>这一子字符串的物品。\n\n你也可以在搜索结果中按<w>?</w>查看商店和物品，并在搜索结果中用热键进行选择。\n这会给出物品或商店内容的描述。\n\n按字符串搜索时不区分大小写。\n\n<h>按照物品属性进行搜索：</h>\n\n你可以搜索法术名称（如<w>Ignite Poison</w>）以寻找包含这道法术的魔法书。\n\n你可以搜索神器的属性（如<w>/prevents.*teleport</w>）以寻找具有这项属性的神器。\n\n你还可以按物品的某些附加属性搜索：\n<w>artefact</w>或<w>artifact</w>会寻找所有鉴别出的神器；<w>ego</w>或<w>branded</w>\n会寻找具有印记的非神器和可能具有印记的未鉴定物品；\n<w>throwable</w>会寻找所有可投掷的物品；\n<w>dropped</w>会寻找所有丢弃过的物品；\n<w>in_shop</w>会寻找所有在商店中的物品；\n而（如果autopickup_search选项是开启的）<w>autopickup</w>\n会寻找能被自动拾取的物品。\n\n技能名（如<w>Polearms</w>或<w>Long Blades</w>）会寻找所有训练该技能的武器。\n你也可以搜寻<w>melee weapon</w>和<w>ranged weapon</w>。\n\n你也可以按物品类型搜索，如<w>book</w>或<w>manual</w>或<w>jewellery</w>。\n对于各部位的防具，\n使用<w>helmet</w>（所有帽子）<w>gloves</w>、<w>shield</w>、和<w>body</w>。\n使用<w>armour && !!body</w>会列出除了铠甲以外的所有防具。\n\n<h>寻找地牢地标：</h>\n\n你可以按名称搜索地牢地标：\n可以通过<w>shop</w>寻找所有商店（包含那些名字中不含“shop”的商店；\n其他地牢地标也可以按名称搜寻：<w>dispersal trap</w>、<w>altar</w>，等等。\n你也可以按照神的名字搜索祭坛：<w>辛</w>。\n\n<h>非正则表达式操作符：</h>\n\n你可以使用！！\n为了在搜索字符串时进行限定，&&表示逻辑与；||表示逻辑或；而<<<< >>则用以分组。\n\n例如，要寻找戒指（首饰），而不是连环甲，可以用：<w>ring && !!mail</w>。\n\n请注意，<w>/</w>和<w>=</w>适用于单个模式，而不是整体：\n你应该搜索<w>armour && /r(C|F|Pois|Elec)</w>\n而不是<w>/armour && r(C|F|Pois|Elec)</w>。","current_english":"<h>Searching the stash-tracker</h>\n\nYou can search for items and dungeon features by name (or a substring of the\nname). In the list of search results, you can select a search result by its\nhotkey to travel to its location.\n\nSome examples of search strings:\n<w>mutation</w>      find all potions of mutation, including potions in shops.\n<w>throwing net</w>  finds every single throwing net and piles of nets.\n<w>shop</w>          lists all shops. Press <w>!</w> and a shop's letter for the stock.\n<w>Lair:2</w>        find everything known to be on Lair:2.\n<w>/D:[2-4]</w>      finds everything on Dungeon levels 2-4. Regexes are allowed!\n              Note that /D:[3-10] will not work as intended, since [x-y] is a\n              regex character range.\n<w>rPois</w>         finds all items with an rPois resistance.\n<w>rC++</w>          finds all items with an rC++ resistance.\n<w>+3</w>            finds all items with a +3 enchantment.\n<w>/Lair.*axe</w> and <w>axe && Lair</w> both show all axes in the Lair.\n\nSome shortcuts:\n<w>.</w>             lists everything on your current level.\n<w>..</w>            lists all items you know.\n<w>0</w> to <w>9</w>        lists all items at the specified waypoint.\n<w>*</w>             lists all items at all waypoints.\n<w>@<<query></w>      lists everything matching <<query> on your current level.\n<w>/<<regex></w>      lists items matching <<regex> as a regular expression.\n<w>=<<string></w>     lists items containing <<string> as a substring.\n\nYou can also examine shops and items in the search results by pressing <w>?</w> and\nthen selecting the hotkey for the search result. This will give a description\nof the item or the contents of the shop.\n\nThe stash search string is case-insensitive.\n\n<h>Searching by item properties:</h>\n\nYou can search for spell names (such as <w>Ignite Poison</w>) to find spellbooks\nthat contain the spell.\n\nYou can search for artefact item properties (such as <w>/prevents.*teleport</w>)\nto find artefacts that have the property.\n\nYou can search by some additional item properties: <w>artefact</w> or\n<w>artifact</w> will find identified artefacts, <w>ego</w> or <w>branded</w>\nwill find non-artefacts with a brand and unidentified items which may be\nbranded, <w>throwable</w> will find things you can throw, <w>dropped</w> will\nfind items you have dropped, <w>in_shop</w> will find items in shops, and (if\nthe autopickup_search option is enabled) <w>autopickup</w> will find items that\nwould be automatically picked up.\n\nSkill names (such as <w>Polearms</w> or <w>Long Blades</w>) will find all weapons that\ntrain that skill. You can also look for <w>melee weapon</w> and <w>ranged weapon</w>.\n\nYou can also search by item types such as <w>book</w> or <w>manual</w> or <w>jewellery</w>.\nFor armour slots, use <w>helmet</w> (all headgear), <w>gloves</w>, <w>shield</w>, and <w>body</w>.\nUse <w>armour && !!body</w> to list all armour pieces except for body armour.\n\n<h>Finding Dungeon Features:</h>\n\nYou can search for dungeon features by name: all shops will be found by a search\nfor <w>shop</w> (including shops that do not have \"shop\" in their name); other\ndungeon features can also be found by name: <w>dispersal trap</w>, <w>altar</w>,\netc. You can also search for altars by deity name: <w>Zin</w>.\n\n<h>Non-regex operators:</h>\n\nYou can use !! to negate a search string, && for a boolean AND, || for\nboolean OR, and <<<< >> for grouping.\n\nFor instance, to find rings (jewellery) but not ring mail you could use:\n<w>ring && !!mail</w>\n\nNote that <w>/</w> and <w>=</w> apply to individual patterns, not the entire query:\nyou should search for <w>armour && /r(C|F|Pois|Elec)</w>, not\n<w>/armour && r(C|F|Pois|Elec)</w>.","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"map, search, travel, and annotation","display_context":"Specific HelpDB topic rendered by the formatted help scroller.","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/command.cc:584","crawl-ref/source/dat/database/help.txt:69","crawl-ref/source/dat/database/zh/help.txt:70"],"fact_sha256":"55043230158dece89939e04cd50c66d919d6ea271828c658c97f3f4d3f8eae1f","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md@95eeacf9704e046c2010ef34859b750d2f8a1937ad87c4a86e8a404c98689407","identity":"help:stash-search.prompt","lifecycle":"current-player-help","producer":[{"guards":[],"location":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:584","path":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc"}],"production_facts":{"chinese":"<h>追踪</h>\n\n你可以按名称（或被包含在名称中的子字符串）搜索物品和地牢地标。\n在搜索结果列表中，你可以通过热键选择一个搜索结果以移动到其位置。\n\n搜索字符串的一些示例：\n<w>mutation</w>      找出所有的变异药水，包括在商店中的。\n<w>throwing net</w>  找出单独的一个或堆叠的投掷网。\n<w>shop</w>          列出所有商店。按<w>!</w>再按商店的字母可查看库存。\n<w>Lair:2</w>        找出所有位于兽穴：2的已知东西。\n<w>/D:[2-4]</w>      找出所有位于地牢第2-4层的东西。可以使用正则表达式！\n              注意，/D:[3-10]将无法按预期运作，因为[x-y]是一个\n              正则表达式的字符范围。\n<w>rPois</w>         找出所有具有rPois抗性的物品。\n<w>rC++</w>          找出所有具有rC++抗性的物品。\n<w>+3</w>            找出所有具有+3附魔的物品。\n<w>/Lair.*axe</w>和<w>axe && Lair</w>都能显示所有位于兽穴的斧子。\n\n一些快捷键：\n<w>.</w>             列出当前层的所有东西。\n<w>..</w>            列出已知的所有物品。\n<w>0</w> to <w>9</w>        列出指定导航点上的所有物品。\n<w>*</w>             列出所有导航点上的所有物品。\n<w>@<<query></w>      列出当前层上所有匹配<<query>的东西。\n<w>/<<regex></w>      列出匹配<<regex>这一正则表达式的物品。\n<w>=<<string></w>     列出所有包含<<string>这一子字符串的物品。\n\n你也可以在搜索结果中按<w>?</w>查看商店和物品，并在搜索结果中用热键进行选择。\n这会给出物品或商店内容的描述。\n\n按字符串搜索时不区分大小写。\n\n<h>按照物品属性进行搜索：</h>\n\n你可以搜索法术名称（如<w>Ignite Poison</w>）以寻找包含这道法术的魔法书。\n\n你可以搜索神器的属性（如<w>/prevents.*teleport</w>）以寻找具有这项属性的神器。\n\n你还可以按物品的某些附加属性搜索：\n<w>artefact</w>或<w>artifact</w>会寻找所有鉴别出的神器；<w>ego</w>或<w>branded</w>\n会寻找具有印记的非神器和可能具有印记的未鉴定物品；\n<w>throwable</w>会寻找所有可投掷的物品；\n<w>dropped</w>会寻找所有丢弃过的物品；\n<w>in_shop</w>会寻找所有在商店中的物品；\n而（如果autopickup_search选项是开启的）<w>autopickup</w>\n会寻找能被自动拾取的物品。\n\n技能名（如<w>Polearms</w>或<w>Long Blades</w>）会寻找所有训练该技能的武器。\n你也可以搜寻<w>melee weapon</w>和<w>ranged weapon</w>。\n\n你也可以按物品类型搜索，如<w>book</w>或<w>manual</w>或<w>jewellery</w>。\n对于各部位的防具，\n使用<w>helmet</w>（所有帽子）<w>gloves</w>、<w>shield</w>、和<w>body</w>。\n使用<w>armour && !!body</w>会列出除了铠甲以外的所有防具。\n\n<h>寻找地牢地标：</h>\n\n你可以按名称搜索地牢地标：\n可以通过<w>shop</w>寻找所有商店（包含那些名字中不含“shop”的商店；\n其他地牢地标也可以按名称搜寻：<w>dispersal trap</w>、<w>altar</w>，等等。\n你也可以按照神的名字搜索祭坛：<w>辛</w>。\n\n<h>非正则表达式操作符：</h>\n\n你可以使用！！\n为了在搜索字符串时进行限定，&&表示逻辑与；||表示逻辑或；而<<<< >>则用以分组。\n\n例如，要寻找戒指（首饰），而不是连环甲，可以用：<w>ring && !!mail</w>。\n\n请注意，<w>/</w>和<w>=</w>适用于单个模式，而不是整体：\n你应该搜索<w>armour && /r(C|F|Pois|Elec)</w>\n而不是<w>/armour && r(C|F|Pois|Elec)</w>。","chinese_key_line":70,"chinese_tokens":{"blank_line_bullet_count":0,"bullet_line_count":0,"lua":{"blocks":[],"errors":[]},"markup":{"balance_errors":[],"malformed":[],"tag_counts":{"</h>":4,"</w>":51,"<h>":4,"<w>":51},"tag_sequence":["<h>","</h>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<h>","</h>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<h>","</h>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<h>","</h>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>"],"technical_angle_counts":{"<<query>":2,"<<regex>":2,"<<string>":2},"technical_angle_sequence":["<<query>","<<query>","<<regex>","<<regex>","<<string>","<<string>"]},"numbers":["2","2","2","4","2","4","3","10","3","3","0","9"],"option_literals":[],"path_literals":[],"quoted_command_literals":[],"urls":[],"w_contents":["mutation","throwing
