weapon","book","manual","jewellery","helmet","gloves","shield","body","armour && !!body","shop","dispersal trap","altar","辛","ring && !!mail","/","=","armour && /r(C|F|Pois|Elec)","/armour && r(C|F|Pois|Elec)"]},"consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:531","dynamic_menu":["crawl-ref/source/menu.cc:1552","crawl-ref/source/menu.cc:1832"],"lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2341"},"dependency_group":"map, search, travel, and annotation","display_context":"Specific HelpDB topic rendered by the formatted help scroller.","english":"<h>Searching the stash-tracker</h>\n\nYou can search for items and dungeon features by name (or a substring of the\nname). In the list of search results, you can select a search result by its\nhotkey to travel to its location.\n\nSome examples of search strings:\n<w>mutation</w>      find all potions of mutation, including potions in shops.\n<w>throwing net</w>  finds every single throwing net and piles of nets.\n<w>shop</w>          lists all shops. Press <w>!</w> and a shop's letter for the stock.\n<w>Lair:2</w>        find everything known to be on Lair:2.\n<w>/D:[2-4]</w>      finds everything on Dungeon levels 2-4. Regexes are allowed!\n              Note that /D:[3-10] will not work as intended, since [x-y] is a\n              regex character range.\n<w>rPois</w>         finds all items with an rPois resistance.\n<w>rC++</w>          finds all items with an rC++ resistance.\n<w>+3</w>            finds all items with a +3 enchantment.\n<w>/Lair.*axe</w> and <w>axe && Lair</w> both show all axes in the Lair.\n\nSome shortcuts:\n<w>.</w>             lists everything on your current level.\n<w>..</w>            lists all items you know.\n<w>0</w> to <w>9</w>        lists all items at the specified waypoint.\n<w>*</w>             lists all items at all waypoints.\n<w>@<<query></w>      lists everything matching <<query> on your current level.\n<w>/<<regex></w>      lists items matching <<regex> as a regular expression.\n<w>=<<string></w>     lists items containing <<string> as a substring.\n\nYou can also examine shops and items in the search results by pressing <w>?</w> and\nthen selecting the hotkey for the search result. This will give a description\nof the item or the contents of the shop.\n\nThe stash search string is case-insensitive.\n\n<h>Searching by item properties:</h>\n\nYou can search for spell names (such as <w>Ignite Poison</w>) to find spellbooks\nthat contain the spell.\n\nYou can search for artefact item properties (such as <w>/prevents.*teleport</w>)\nto find artefacts that have the property.\n\nYou can search by some additional item properties: <w>artefact</w> or\n<w>artifact</w> will find identified artefacts, <w>ego</w> or <w>branded</w>\nwill find non-artefacts with a brand and unidentified items which may be\nbranded, <w>throwable</w> will find things you can throw, <w>dropped</w> will\nfind items you have dropped, <w>in_shop</w> will find items in shops, and (if\nthe autopickup_search option is enabled) <w>autopickup</w> will find items that\nwould be automatically picked up.\n\nSkill names (such as <w>Polearms</w> or <w>Long Blades</w>) will find all weapons that\ntrain that skill. You can also look for <w>melee weapon</w> and <w>ranged weapon</w>.\n\nYou can also search by item types such as <w>book</w> or <w>manual</w> or <w>jewellery</w>.\nFor armour slots, use <w>helmet</w> (all headgear), <w>gloves</w>, <w>shield</w>, and <w>body</w>.\nUse <w>armour && !!body</w> to list all armour pieces except for body armour.\n\n<h>Finding Dungeon Features:</h>\n\nYou can search for dungeon features by name: all shops will be found by a search\nfor <w>shop</w> (including shops that do not have \"shop\" in their name); other\ndungeon features can also be found by name: <w>dispersal trap</w>, <w>altar</w>,\netc. You can also search for altars by deity name: <w>Zin</w>.\n\n<h>Non-regex operators:</h>\n\nYou can use !! to negate a search string, && for a boolean AND, || for\nboolean OR, and <<<< >> for grouping.\n\nFor instance, to find rings (jewellery) but not ring mail you could use:\n<w>ring && !!mail</w>\n\nNote that <w>/</w> and <w>=</w> apply to individual patterns, not the entire query:\nyou should search for <w>armour && /r(C|F|Pois|Elec)</w>, not\n<w>/armour && r(C|F|Pois|Elec)</w>.","english_key_line":69,"english_tokens":{"blank_line_bullet_count":0,"bullet_line_count":0,"lua":{"blocks":[],"errors":[]},"markup":{"balance_errors":[],"malformed":[],"tag_counts":{"</h>":4,"</w>":51,"<h>":4,"<w>":51},"tag_sequence":["<h>","</h>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<h>","</h>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<h>","</h>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<h>","</h>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>","<w>","</w>"],"technical_angle_counts":{"<<query>":2,"<<regex>":2,"<<string>":2},"technical_angle_sequence":["<<query>","<<query>","<<regex>","<<regex>","<<string>","<<string>"]},"numbers":["2","2","2","4","2","4","3","10","3","3","0","9"],"option_literals":[],"path_literals":[],"quoted_command_literals":[],"urls":[],"w_contents":["mutation","throwing Poison","/prevents.*teleport","artefact","artifact","ego","branded","throwable","dropped","in_shop","autopickup","Polearms","Long Blades","melee weapon","ranged weapon","book","manual","jewellery","helmet","gloves","shield","body","armour && !!body","shop","dispersal trap","altar","Zin","ring && !!mail","/","=","armour && /r(C|F|Pois|Elec)","/armour && r(C|F|Pois|Elec)"]},"fact_sha256":"55043230158dece89939e04cd50c66d919d6ea271828c658c97f3f4d3f8eae1f","identity":"help:stash-search.prompt","key":"stash-search.prompt","kind":"help","lifecycle":"current-player-help","producer":[{"guards":[],"location":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:584","path":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc"}],"structural_differences":["w_contents"],"token_contract_equal":{"blank_line_bullet_count":true,"bullet_line_count":true,"format_tag_counts":true,"format_tag_sequence":true,"lua_block_count":true,"lua_comparison_literals":true,"lua_control_skeleton":true,"numbers":true,"option_literals":true,"path_literals":true,"quoted_command_literals":true,"technical_angle_counts":true,"technical_angle_sequence":true,"urls":true,"w_contents":false}},"proposed_translation":"<h>搜索储藏追踪器</h>\n\n你可以按名称（或名称中的子字符串）搜索物品和地牢地标。\n在搜索结果列表中，按搜索结果对应的热键，即可选择该结果并前往其位置。\n\n搜索字符串示例：\n<w>mutation</w>      找出所有变异药水，包括商店中的变异药水。\n<w>throwing net</w>  找出每一张单独的投网以及成堆的投网。\n<w>shop</w>          列出所有商店。按<w>!</w>，再按商店对应的字母即可查看库存。\n<w>Lair:2</w>        找出已知位于兽巢第 2 层的所有东西。\n<w>/D:[2-4]</w>      找出地牢第 2 至 4 层的所有东西。可以使用正则表达式！\n              注意，/D:[3-10]不会按预期工作，因为[x-y]表示\n              正则表达式中的字符范围。\n<w>rPois</w>         找出所有具有rPois抗性的物品。\n<w>rC++</w>          找出所有具有rC++抗性的物品。\n<w>+3</w>            找出所有具有+3附魔的物品。\n<w>/Lair.*axe</w>和<w>axe && Lair</w>都会显示兽巢中的所有斧头。\n\n快捷方式：\n<w>.</w>             列出当前层的所有东西。\n<w>..</w>            列出你已知的所有物品。\n<w>0</w>-<w>9</w>        列出指定导航点上的所有物品。\n<w>*</w>             列出所有导航点上的所有物品。\n<w>@<<query></w>      列出当前层所有与<<query>匹配的东西。\n<w>/<<regex></w>      将<<regex>作为正则表达式，列出与之匹配的物品。\n<w>=<<string></w>     列出包含<<string>这一子字符串的物品。\n\n在搜索结果中按<w>?</w>，再按搜索结果对应的热键，还可以查看商店和物品。\n系统会显示物品描述或商店库存。\n\n储藏搜索字符串不区分大小写。\n\n<h>按物品属性搜索：</h>\n\n你可以搜索法术名称（如<w>Ignite Poison</w>），找出包含该法术的法术书。\n\n你可以搜索神器属性（如<w>/prevents.*teleport</w>），找出具有该属性的神器。\n\n你还可以按物品的其他属性搜索：\n<w>artefact</w>或<w>artifact</w>会找出已鉴定的神器；\n<w>ego</w>或<w>branded</w>会找出带有铭印的非神器，以及尚未鉴定、可能带有铭印的物品；\n<w>throwable</w>会找出可以投掷的物品；\n<w>dropped</w>会找出你丢弃过的物品；\n<w>in_shop</w>会找出商店中的物品；\n而（若启用了autopickup_search选项）<w>autopickup</w>会找出会被自动拾取的物品。\n\n技能名称（如<w>Polearms</w>或<w>Long Blades</w>）会找出可训练该技能的所有武器。\n你还可以搜索<w>melee weapon</w>和<w>ranged weapon</w>。\n\n你也可以按物品类型搜索，如<w>book</w>、<w>manual</w>或<w>jewellery</w>。\n对于护甲槽位，可以使用<w>helmet</w>（所有头部护具）、<w>gloves</w>、\n<w>shield</w>和<w>body</w>。\n使用<w>armour && !!body</w>会列出除躯干护甲以外的所有护甲。\n\n<h>查找地牢地标：</h>\n\n你可以按名称搜索地牢地标：搜索<w>shop</w>会找到所有商店\n（包括名称中不含“shop”的商店）；其他地牢地标也可按名称找到，\n如<w>dispersal trap</w>、<w>altar</w>等。\n你还可以按神祇名称搜索祭坛：<w>辛</w>。\n\n<h>非正则表达式运算符：</h>\n\n可以用!!对搜索字符串取反，用&&表示逻辑与，用||表示逻辑或，\n并用<<<< >>分组。\n\n例如，要查找戒指（首饰）而不是环甲，可以使用：<w>ring && !!mail</w>。\n\n请注意，<w>/</w>和<w>=</w>只作用于单个模式，而不是整个查询：\n应当搜索<w>armour && /r(C|F|Pois|Elec)</w>，\n而不是<w>/armour && r(C|F|Pois|Elec)</w>。","reentry_trigger":"对应英文源文、生产 consumer、格式约定或 docs/glossary.md 裁决发生变化时重新审阅。","rejected_alternatives":["仅作零散词语替换：跨段落的事实、列表或格式问题无法得到完整修复。","原样保留基线译文：已确认的语义或结构缺陷会继续展示给玩家。"],"reviewer_rationale":"基线多处术语、运算符和搜索对象表达错误，分散在整篇；重译可完整保存示例 token 和查询语义。","terminal_conclusion":"retranslate"}
{"actual_behavior":"巫师怪物规格支持物种、装备、命名、属性、召唤与法术槽；法术标志以生产 parser 实际支持的集合和沉默、反魔法、噪音、距离行为为准。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:531","dynamic_menu":["crawl-ref/source/menu.cc:1552","crawl-ref/source/menu.cc:1832"],"lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2341"},"current_chinese":"<h>生成指定怪物</h>\n\n要生成带有其种族的默认装备的怪物，只需输入物种名称，\n如<w>kobold</w>或<w>Yiuf</w>。\n\n可以按照n<w>-headed</w>\n<w>hydra</w>的格式指定多头蛇的头的数量（最多20个头）：\n\n<w>four-headed hydra</w>\n\n要设定一个（极）丑物的初始颜色，使用：\n\n<w>white very ugly thing</w>\n\n有效的颜色有：<w>brown</w>、<w>cyan</w>、<w>green</w>、<w>purple</w>、<w>red</w>和<w>white</w>。\n\n变异兽则可以通过在其前面加上成熟度等级和/或由连字符分隔的一组特征来进行设定，\n如下所示：\n\n<w>mature shock-weird mutant beast</w>\n\n有效的成熟度等级有：<w>larval</w>、<w>juvenile</w>、<w>mature</w>、<w>elder</w>和<w>primal</w>。\n特征有：<w>sting</w>、<w>bat</w>、<w>fire</w>、<w>weird</w>、<w>shock</w>和<w>ox</w>。\n\n黏液生物的体型可以通过前缀<w>large</w>、<w>very large</w>、<w>enormous</w>或<w>titanic</w>设置为非正常体型：\n\n<w>very large slime creature</w>\n\n如果某个怪物是某个群体的一员，你可以通过在名称中添加关键词<w>band</w>使之成为群体成员。例如：\n\n<w>orc warlord band</w>\n\n设定<w>band</w>并不会强制放置群体——它会用游戏的正常生成机率生成群体。\n只有在有空间的情况下，才会生成群体。\n\n通过在怪物名称后面加上分号，然后加上物品列表，可以指定怪物所携带的物品。\n例如：\n\n<w>orc ; rapier | quick blade . chain mail | plate armour</w>\n\n会生成一个兽人，他持握轻剑或快刃，身着锁子甲或板甲。\n神器和属性只有在明确设定时才会生成。\n请注意，怪物原先使用的任何物品都会被移除。这个能强制让怪物没有任何物品：\n\n<w>orc; nothing</w>\n\n有关详细信息，\n请参阅<cyan>docs/develop/levels/syntax.txt</cyan>，<cyan>ITEMS</cyan>章节。\n任何引用的斜线（<w>/</w>）都应换为管道符（<w>|</w>），\n任何逗号（<w>,</w>）都换为句号（<w>.</w>）。\n\n同样的语法也适用于创建特定类型的飞舞的武器武器：\n\n<w>dancing weapon; rapier</w>\n\n限制：如果给怪物一个随机投射器，就没有办法强制弹药类型与投射器类型匹配。\n然而，作为一种特殊情况，飞舞的武器如果是投射器，总会产生一些合适的弹药。\n\n你可以通过使用标签<w>always_corpse</w>指定怪物永远生成尸体，\n或使用<w>never_corpse</w>，这将永远不会生成尸体。\n\n默认情况下，怪物在被生成时处于睡眠状态。\n这可以通过在名称前加<w>generate_awake</w>来更改。\n\n<w>generate_awake boulder beetle</w>\n\n前缀<w>patrolling</w>防止它从生成的地方走开。\n\n你可以通过<w>god</w>:说明符设定怪物所皈依的神，如下所示：\n\n<w>orc god:the_shining_one</w>\n\n如果设定这个怪物是由指定的神所赋予的，则应以<w>god_gift</w>为后缀：\n\n<w>orc god:the_shining_one god_gift</w>\n\n注意，除非明确指定了<w>god_gift</w>，否则这个怪物是不会被视作由神所赋予的。\n\n你可以通过指定<w>att</w>:来设定怪物从属于以下之一派系：\n\n* <w>att:hostile</w> -- 默认\n* <w>att:friendly</w> -- 驯服的，会跟随你\n* <w>att:neutral</w> -- 对你和敌对怪物都有敌意\n* <w>att:good_neutral</w> -- 对敌对怪物有敌意\n* <w>att:fellow_slime</w> -- 驯服的，但不会跟随你\n\n给怪物设定颜色，覆盖其默认颜色：\n\n<w>col:darkgrey fungus</w>\n\n如果设定为<w>any</w>，将选择为一个随机的颜色。\n\n你可以使用<w>tile</w>:说明符以设定怪物的贴图，如下所示：\n\n<w>rat tile:mons_giant_bat</w>\n\n在图像版中，这会创建一个使用“<cyan>mons_giant_bat</cyan>”贴图的怪物，\n但在其他方面与老鼠相同。在ASCII模式下，这将不起作用。\n\n如果你想在生成的地图中放置一个与当前楼层匹配的随机怪物，你可以使用\n\n<w>random</w>\n\n如果你想在其他地方放置一个与之匹配的随机怪物，\n你可以在怪物语句中使用place:标签：\n\n<w>place:Abyss</w>\n\n使用<w>place</w>:表示你要生成一个随机怪物。\n你也可以在其他深度有随机怪物的楼层生成丧尸，例如：\n\n<w>place:Elf:3 zombie</w>\n\n可用的修饰语有<w>zombie</w>、<w>draugr</w>、<w>simulacrum</w>和<w>spectre</w>。\n\n<h>覆盖怪物名称</h>\n\n给单个怪物设定名字：\n\n<w>kobold name:Durwent</w>\n\n这会让怪物显示为“<cyan>Durwent the kobold</cyan>”。\n可以用_符号替换名称中的空格。\n值得注意的是，“<cyan>the <<race></cyan>”将被附加到所有名称上，\n在使用这些名称时应予以考虑。\n\n可以在名称上使用三种不同的修饰语：\n<w>name_adjective</w>、<w>name_suffix和</w>name_replace。\n<w>name_adjective</w>会把那个名称作为形容词。例如：\n\n<w>kobold name:ugly name_adjective</w>\n\n会生成“<cyan>An ugly kobold</cyan>”“<cyan>The ugly kobold hits you</cyan>”等文本。\n\n<w>name_suffix</w> does the same, but after the monster's base name:\n\n<w>kobold name:wearing_mittens name_suffix</w>\n\n会生成“<cyan>A kobold wearing mittens</cyan>”“<cyan>The kobold wearing mittens hits you</cyan>”等文本。\n\n<w>name_replace</w>会把基础名称替换为给定名称，就好像那个怪物是唯一的：\n\n<w>kobold name:Durwent name_replace</w>\n\n会生成“<cyan>Durwent</cyan>”而不是“<cyan>Durwent the kobold</cyan>”。\n而尸体则仍然是“<cyan>kobold corpse of Durwent</cyan>”。\n\n<w>name_species</w>按原样使用尸体的名称，\n而不使用“<cyan>kobold</cyan> <cyan>corpse of X</cyan>”\n作为<w>name_replace</w>的前缀。\n它也避免使通常的名称标签显示在此处贴图中独一无二的、被重命名的怪物上方。\n\n与<w>name_replace</w>或<w>name_species</w>一并使用，\n在重命名怪物时会有两个标签，但不会加到实际的“名字”中：\n<w>name_descriptor</w>, and <w>name_definite</w>。\n\n例如：\n\n<w>kobold name:goblin name_replace</w>\n\n会生成“<cyan>goblin hits you</cyan>”。然而，\n在以上标记中加入<w>name_descriptor</w>会生成“<cyan>The goblin hits you</cyan>”，\n那个怪物的描述会变为“<cyan>A goblin</cyan>”。\n\n<w>kobold name:goblin name_replace name_descriptor</w>\n\nThe <w>name_definite</w>标记会确保它永远不会被冠以“<cyan>a</cyan>”或\n“<cyan>an</cyan>”，但始终使用确定的宾语。因此，如上所述，\n你将看到“<cyan>The<cyan>goblin</cyan> <cyan>hits you</cyan>”，\n但在其描述中也将看到“<cyan>The goblin</cyan>”。\n设定<w>name_definite</w>将自动设定标记<w>name_descriptor</w>，因此意味着：\n\n<w>kobold name:goblin name_replace name_definite</w>\n\n完全等同于：\n\n<w>kobold name:goblin name_replace name_descriptor name_definite</w>\n\n<w>name_zombie</w>可以用来重新定义已经亡灵化的怪物（包括骷髅、拟像和幽灵）。\n\n最后，<w>name_nocorpse</w>能让尸体的名称完全被隐去。例如：\n\n<w>wizard name:sad name_adjective name_nocorpse</w>\n\n会让“<cyan>a sad wizard</cyan>”留下“<cyan>a wizard corpse</cyan>”。\n\n当在定义中空间受限时，可以使用以下标签的缩写形式：\n\n* <w>name_suffix</w> -> <w>n_suf</w>\n* <w>name_adjective</w> -> <w>n_adj</w>\n* <w>name_replace</w> -> <w>n_rpl</w>\n* <w>name_species</w> -> <w>n_spe</w>\n* <w>name_descriptor</w> -> <w>n_des</w>\n* <w>name_definite</w> -> <w>n_the</w>\n* <w>name_zombie</w> -> <w>n_zom</w>\n* <w>name_nocorpse</w> -> <w>n_noc</w>\n\n<h>Overriding Monster Stats</h>\n\n使用<w>hd</w>和<w>hp</w>进一步对怪物进行定制。\n可以用这两个标签完全覆盖怪物的初始生命骰和生命值：\n\n<w>kobold hd:20</w>\n\n将生成一个有着20点生命骰的狗头人。它的生命值是随机的，但取决于它的生命骰，\n而生命骰也会被再次掷出。被设定了明确的生命值的怪物，这个值则不会变化。\n\n你也可以设定一个明确的生命值：\n\n<w>kobold hp:20</w>\n\n将生成一个有着20点生命值的狗头人。\n这对于生命值非随机的怪物（如雕像）也很有用。\n\n可以通过<w>dur</w>和<w>sum</w>标签，将怪物标记为召唤生成的。\n<w>dur</w>标签接受1到6之间的整数。The <w>sum</w>授受一个法术名称\n（根据<w>spells</w>标签进行解析，如下所述）或<w>clone</w>（罗刹天）、\n<w>animate</w>（源于活化死者）、<w>chaos</w>（由纯粹的混乱生成）、\n<w>miscast</w>（由施法失误召唤）、<w>zot</w>（由佐特陷阱召唤）、\n<w>wrath</w>（由神在惩罚时所召唤）、<w>aid</w>（由神在支援时所召唤）\n中的任何一个。\n\n在这些法术中，<w>shadow_creatures</w>的效果最为显著，\n因为怪物会隐没在阴影中，而不是消散在烟雾中。使用非召唤法术也没有效果。\n\n将一个怪物标记为<w>seen</w>将覆盖系统，并强制将怪物标记为已看到；\n这意味着它不会生成诸如“<cyan>A kobold comes into view</cyan>”之类的消息。\n\n<h>覆盖怪物的法术</h>\n\n怪物的法术集可以用<w>spells</w>: 标签进行覆盖，用法如下：\n\n*<w>goblin spells:throw_flame.50.wizard</w>\n*<w>ancient lich spells:symbol_of_torment.26.wizard; glaciate.51.wizard</w>\n\n（一个法术槽列表，由用下划线替换空格的法术名称、施法频率组成，\n法术槽后面用<w>.</w>标记，法术槽用<w>;</w>进行分隔。\n<w>;</w>周围不能有空格，<w>spells</w>: 前缀后不能有空格）\n\n法术名称必须与<cyan>spl-data.h</cyan>中的名称完全一致，空格用下划线替换。\n法术频率是施放该法术的几率（200分之x）。法术标记包括：\n\n* <w>natural</w>、<w>magical</w>、<w>demonic</w>、<w>wizard</w>或\n<w>priest</w> - 施放此法术的方式。巫师和祭司能被沉默，\n并触发某些行为（特洛格会常识杀死巫师的行为，比欧弗会赏识杀死祭司的行为）。\n魔法、恶魔和巫师法术受到反魔法效果的影响。自然法术不会产生噪音。\n* <w>emergency</w> - 怪物只会在生命值低时才会施展这个法术。\n* <w>breath</w> - 该法术是一种吐息武器，使用它时会闭气，\n在此期间该法术无法再次施放。\n* <w>no_silent</w> - 该法术不能在沉默时施放，\n即使它是一种通常不受影响的能力类型。\n* <w>instant</w> - 该法术不会耗费怪物的精力；\n他们可以在一个回合内同时进行另一动作。\n* <w>noisy</w> - 强制让该法术发出噪音，即使它通常是种自然能力。\n\n你可以使用<w>none</w> 将槽指定为空。你可以用以下方式强制生成一个无法术怪物：\n\n<w>orc wizard spells:none</w>\n\n<h>Setting enchantments</h>\n\n你可以给怪物施加任何魔力。它可以启用快速、失明、具有永久性驱逐飞弹等效果。\n语法为：<w>ench:<<name>:<<duration>:<<degree></w>或\n<w>perm_ench:<<name>:<<degree></w>。\n<w><<degree></w>和<w><<duration></w>字段可以省略。\n\n例如：\n\n* <w>orc perm_ench:blind perm_ench:mad</w>\n* <w>dream sheep ench:sticky_flame</w>\n* <w>stone giant dur:1 ench:berserk</w>\n","current_english":"<h>Create specified monster</h>\n\nTo create a monster with the default equipment for its species, simply enter\nthe species name, such as <w>kobold</w> or <w>Yiuf</w>.\n\nA hydra or slymdra can be given a specific number of heads by calling it an\nn<w>-headed</w> <w>hydra</w> or n<w>-headed</w> <w>slymdra</w> (with a maximum of 20 heads):\n\n<w>four-headed hydra</w>\n\nTo set the colour a (very) ugly thing takes when it is first created, use:\n\n<w>white very ugly thing</w>\n\nThe valid colours are <w>brown</w>, <w>cyan</w>, <w>green</w>, <w>purple</w>, <w>red</w> and <w>white</w>.\n\nA mutant beast can be customised by prefacing it with a maturity level and/or\nhyphen-separated set of traits, as follows:\n\n<w>mature shock-weird mutant beast</w>\n\nThe valid maturity levels are <w>larval</w>, <w>juvenile</w>, <w>mature</w>, <w>elder</w> and <w>primal</w>. The\nfacets are <w>sting</w>, <w>bat</w>, <w>fire</w>, <w>weird</w>, <w>shock</w> and <w>ox</w>.\n\nA slime creature's size can be set to other than normal using the prefixes\n<w>large</w>, <w>very large</w>, <w>enormous</w> or <w>titanic</w>:\n\n<w>very large slime creature</w>\n\nIf a monster is a member of a band, you can request that it be eligible for\nband members by adding the keyword <w>band</w> to the name. For instance:\n\n<w>orc warlord band</w>\n\nSpecifying <w>band</w> doesn't force bands to be placed - it requests that the game\nuse the normal chances of creating a band. A band will only be placed if there\nis space for it.\n\nA monster can be given specific items by following the monster name with a\nsemi-colon and then with an item list. For example:\n\n<w>orc ; rapier | quick blade . chain mail | plate armour</w>\n\nwill generate an orc wielding either a rapier or a quick blade and wearing\neither chain mail or plate armour. Randarts and ego items are only generated\nif they are explicitly requested. Note that any items that the monster was\noriginally generated with will be removed and destroyed. This can be used to\nforce a monster to have no items whatsoever:\n\n<w>orc; nothing</w>\n\nFor further information, see <cyan>docs/develop/levels/syntax.txt</cyan>, section <cyan>ITEMS</cyan>.\nAny reference to a slash (<w>/</w>) should be replaced with a pipe (<w>|</w>) and any comma\n(<w>,</w>) with a full stop (<w>.</w>).\n\nThe same syntax is also used to create a specific type of dancing weapon:\n\n<w>dancing weapon; rapier</w>\n\nLimitations: If a monster is given a random launcher, there is no way to force\nthe ammo type to match the launcher type. As a special case, however, a\nlauncher dancing weapon is always generated with some suitable ammunition.\n\nYou can specify that a monster will always generate a corpse by using the tag\n<w>always_corpse</w>, or that it will never generate a corpse with <w>never_corpse</w>.\n\nBy default, monsters are generated asleep. This is changed by prefixing the\nname with <w>generate_awake</w>.\n\n<w>generate_awake boulder beetle</w>\n\nA prefix of <w>patrolling</w> prevents it from walking out of sight of the the place\nwhere it was created.\n\nYou can set the monster's god using the <w>god</w>: specifier as follows:\n\n<w>orc god:the_shining_one</w>\n\nIf the monster is to be a gift of the specified god, this should be suffixed\nwith <w>god_gift</w>:\n\n<w>orc god:the_shining_one god_gift</w>\n\nNote that the monster will not be considered a god gift unless <w>god_gift</w> is\nexplicitly set.\n\nYou can set the monster's faction by specifying <w>att</w>: one of:\n\n* <w>att:hostile</w> -- the default\n* <w>att:friendly</w> -- tame, will follow you\n* <w>att:neutral</w> -- hostile to both you and att:hostile monsters\n* <w>att:good_neutral</w> -- hostile to att:hostile\n* <w>att:fellow_slime</w> -- tame, won't follow you\n\nMonsters can be given colours that override their default colour:\n\n<w>col:darkgrey fungus</w>\n\nThe colour <w>any</w> can be given, in which case a random colour will be chosen.\n\nYou can override the displayed monster tile using the <w>tile</w>: specifier as\nfollows:\n\n<w>rat tile:mons_giant_bat</w>\n\nIn tiles version, this would create a monster that uses the \"<cyan>mons_giant_bat</cyan>\"\ntile, but is otherwise identical to a rat. In ASCII mode, this will do\nnothing.\n\nIf you want to place a random monster suitable for the level the map is\ngenerated on, you can use\n\n<w>random</w>\n\nIf you want to place a random monster suitable for some other place, you can\nuse a place: tag in the monster spec:\n\n<w>place:Abyss</w>\n\nUsing <w>place</w>: implies that you want a random monster. You can also request\nzombies from random monsters suitable for some other depth as (e.g.):\n\n<w>place:Elf:3 zombie</w>\n\nThe available modifiers are <w>zombie</w>, <w>draugr</w>, <w>simulacrum</w>, and <w>spectre</w>.\n\n<h>Overriding Monster Names</h>\n\nIndividual monsters can be given names as follows:\n\n<w>kobold name:Durwent</w>\n\nThis will cause the monster to appear as \"<cyan>Durwent the kobold</cyan>\". Spaces can be\nplaced in the name by substituting them with the _ symbol. It is worth noting\nthat \"<cyan>the <<race></cyan>\" will be appended to all names, which should be considered\nwhen coming up with them.\n\nThere are three different modifiers that can be used on a name:\n<w>name_adjective</w>, <w>name_suffix and </w>name_replace. <w>name_adjective</w> makes the\nname act as an adjective. For example:\n\n<w>kobold name:ugly name_adjective</w>\n\nWill cause \"<cyan>An ugly kobold</cyan>\", \"<cyan>The ugly kobold hits you</cyan>\", and so on.\n\n<w>name_suffix</w> does the same, but after the monster's base name:\n\n<w>kobold name:wearing_mittens name_suffix</w>\n\nWill give \"<cyan>A kobold wearing mittens</cyan>\", \"<cyan>The kobold wearing mittens hits you</cyan>\",\nand so on.\n\n<w>name_replace</w> causes the base name to be replaced by given name, as if the\nmonster was a unique:\n\n<w>kobold name:Durwent name_replace</w>\n\nWill result in \"<cyan>Durwent</cyan>\" rather than \"<cyan>Durwent the kobold</cyan>\". The corpse will\nstill be a \"<cyan>kobold corpse of Durwent</cyan>\".\n\n<w>name_species</w> uses the name for corpses as-is, without prefixing it with\n\"<cyan>kobold</cyan> <cyan>corpse of X</cyan>\" for <w>name_replace</w>. It also avoids the usual name tag\nthat displays above unique and renamed monsters in local tiles.\n\nIn combination with <w>name_replace</w> or <w>name_species</w>, there are two further tags\nthat can be used when renaming monsters, but not giving them an actual \"name\":\n<w>name_descriptor</w>, and <w>name_definite</w>.\n\nFor example:\n\n<w>kobold name:goblin name_replace</w>\n\nWill result in \"<cyan>goblin hits you</cyan>\". However, adding <w>name_descriptor</w> from the\nabove flags will result in \"<cyan>The goblin hits you</cyan>\" and in the description of the\nmonster, \"<cyan>A goblin</cyan>\".\n\n<w>kobold name:goblin name_replace name_descriptor</w>\n\nThe <w>name_definite</w> flag ensures that it is never referred to as \"<cyan>a</cyan>\" or \"<cyan>an</cyan>\",\nbut that the definite object is always used. Therefore, you will see \"<cyan>The\n<cyan>goblin</cyan> <cyan>hits you</cyan>\", as per above, but also \"<cyan>The goblin</cyan>\" in its description.\nSetting <w>name_definite</w> will automatically set the flag <w>name_descriptor</w>,\ntherefore meaning that:\n\n<w>kobold name:goblin name_replace name_definite</w>\n\nis exactly the same as:\n\n<w>kobold name:goblin name_replace name_descriptor name_definite</w>\n\n<w>name_zombie</w> can be used to redefine already zombified monsters (including\ndraugr, simulacra and spectrals).\n\nFinally, <w>name_nocorpse</w> will cause the name to be completely ignored for\ncorpses. For example:\n\n<w>wizard name:sad name_adjective name_nocorpse</w>\n\nwill result in \"<cyan>a sad wizard</cyan>\" that leaves \"<cyan>a wizard corpse</cyan>\".\n\nFor instances where space in definitions is limited, you can use abbreviated\nforms of these tags:\n\n* <w>name_suffix</w> -> <w>n_suf</w>\n* <w>name_adjective</w> -> <w>n_adj</w>\n* <w>name_replace</w> -> <w>n_rpl</w>\n* <w>name_species</w> -> <w>n_spe</w>\n* <w>name_descriptor</w> -> <w>n_des</w>\n* <w>name_definite</w> -> <w>n_the</w>\n* <w>name_zombie</w> -> <w>n_zom</w>\n* <w>name_nocorpse</w> -> <w>n_noc</w>\n\n<h>Overriding Monster Stats</h>\n\nFurther monster customisation can be made by using <w>hd</w> and <w>hp</w>. These two tags\nallow you to completely overwrite the original hit dice and hit points of a\nmonster:\n\n<w>kobold hd:20</w>\n\nCreates a kobold with a hit dice of 20. As its hp is randomised, based on its\nhit dice, this will also be re-rolled. Monsters with a specific hit point\nvalue will not have this value change.\n\nYou can also have a specific value for hit points:\n\n<w>kobold hp:20</w>\n\nCreates a kobold with 20 hit points. This is also useful for monsters (such as\nstatues) which come with non-randomised HP values.\n\nMonsters can be marked as summoned by using <w>dur</w> and <w>sum</w> tags. The <w>dur</w> tag\naccepts an integer between 1 and 6 inclusive. The <w>sum</w> tag accepts the name of\na spell (parsed as per the <w>spells</w> tag, described below) or any of <w>clone</w>\n(Rakshasa), <w>animate</w> (from animate dead), <w>chaos</w> (made from pure chaos), <w>miscast</w>\n(summoned from by spell miscast effect), <w>zot</w> (summoned by a Zot trap), <w>wrath</w>\n(summoned by a god in an act of retribution), <w>aid</w> (summoned by a god in order\nto give aid).\n\nOf the spells, <w>shadow_creatures</w> has the most significant effect, in that\nmonsters will dissipate into the shadows rather than disappear in a puff of\nsmoke. Using non-summoning spells will also have no effect.\n\nTagging a monster with <w>seen</w> will override the system and force that monster to\nbe marked as already viewed; this means that it won't generate messages such\nas \"<cyan>A kobold comes into view</cyan>\".\n\n<h>Overriding Monster Spells</h>\n\nMonster spell sets can be overridden with a <w>spells</w>: tag, used as follows:\n\n*<w>goblin spells:throw_flame.50.wizard</w>\n*<w>ancient lich spells:symbol_of_torment.26.wizard; glaciate.51.wizard</w>\n\n(a list of spell slots, consisting of a spell name with spaces replaced with\nunderscores, a frequency of casting and set of spell slot flags specified\nafterwards with <w>.</w>, with slots separated by <w>;</w>. There should be no spaces around\nthe <w>;</w> or after the <w>spells</w>: prefix)\n\nSpell names must exactly match the names in <cyan>spl-data.h</cyan>, with spaces replaced\nby underscores. Spell frequencies are an (x in 200) chance that that spell\nwill be cast. Spell flags include:\n\n* <w>natural</w>, <w>magical</w>, <w>demonic</w>, <w>wizard</w>, or <w>priest</w> - the manner in which this\nspell is being cast. Wizards and priests can be silenced and will trigger\nappropriate conducts (Trog will appreciate killing wizards, Beogh will\nappreciate killing priests). Magical, demonic, and wizard spells are subject\nto anti-magic effects. Natural spells do not generate noise.\n* <w>emergency</w> - the monster will only cast the spell when low on health.\n* <w>breath</w> - the spell is a breath weapon, and using it triggers a breath\ntimeout during which the spell cannot be cast again.\n* <w>no_silent</w> - the spell cannot be used while silent, even if it is an ability\ntype normally not subject to silence.\n* <w>instant</w> - the spell does not expend the monster's energy; they may take\nanother action on the same turn.\n* <w>noisy</w> - forces the spell to make noise even if it is a natural ability.\n\nYou may use <w>none</w> specify that a slot should be left empty. You can force a\nspell-less monster with:\n\n<w>orc wizard spells:none</w>\n\n<h>Setting enchantments</h>\n\nYou may give a monster any number of enchantments. It may start hasted, blind,\nhave permanent RMsl, etc. The syntax is <w>ench:<<name>:<<duration>:<<degree></w> or\n<w>perm_ench:<<name>:<<degree></w>. The <w><<degree></w> and <w><<duration></w> fields may be\nomitted.\n\nFor example:\n\n* <w>orc perm_ench:blind perm_ench:mad</w>\n* <w>dream sheep ench:sticky_flame</w>\n* <w>stone giant dur:1 ench:berserk</w>\n","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"macros, platform keys, and wizard","display_context":"Specific HelpDB topic rendered by the formatted help scroller.","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/wiz-mon.cc:66","crawl-ref/source/dat/database/help.txt:312","crawl-ref/source/dat/database/zh/help.txt:305"],"fact_sha256":"3096b1600cb0f54a09a9e772e4abe462508a89b9224ae1a242dddebb20662761","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md@95eeacf9704e046c2010ef34859b750d2f8a1937ad87c4a86e8a404c98689407","identity":"help:wiz-monster","lifecycle":"wizard-only","producer":[{"guards":["WIZARD"],"location":"crawl-ref/source/wiz-mon.cc:66","path":"crawl-ref/source/wiz-mon.cc"}],"production_facts":{"chinese":"<h>生成指定怪物</h>\n\n要生成带有其种族的默认装备的怪物，只需输入物种名称，\n如<w>kobold</w>或<w>Yiuf</w>。\n\n可以按照n<w>-headed</w>\n<w>hydra</w>的格式指定多头蛇的头的数量（最多20个头）：\n\n<w>four-headed hydra</w>\n\n要设定一个（极）丑物的初始颜色，使用：\n\n<w>white very ugly thing</w>\n\n有效的颜色有：<w>brown</w>、<w>cyan</w>、<w>green</w>、<w>purple</w>、<w>red</w>和<w>white</w>。\n\n变异兽则可以通过在其前面加上成熟度等级和/或由连字符分隔的一组特征来进行设定，\n如下所示：\n\n<w>mature shock-weird mutant beast</w>\n\n有效的成熟度等级有：<w>larval</w>、<w>juvenile</w>、<w>mature</w>、<w>elder</w>和<w>primal</w>。\n特征有：<w>sting</w>、<w>bat</w>、<w>fire</w>、<w>weird</w>、<w>shock</w>和<w>ox</w>。\n\n黏液生物的体型可以通过前缀<w>large</w>、<w>very large</w>、<w>enormous</w>或<w>titanic</w>设置为非正常体型：\n\n<w>very large slime creature</w>\n\n如果某个怪物是某个群体的一员，你可以通过在名称中添加关键词<w>band</w>使之成为群体成员。例如：\n\n<w>orc warlord band</w>\n\n设定<w>band</w>并不会强制放置群体——它会用游戏的正常生成机率生成群体。\n只有在有空间的情况下，才会生成群体。\n\n通过在怪物名称后面加上分号，然后加上物品列表，可以指定怪物所携带的物品。\n例如：\n\n<w>orc ; rapier | quick blade . chain mail | plate armour</w>\n\n会生成一个兽人，他持握轻剑或快刃，身着锁子甲或板甲。\n神器和属性只有在明确设定时才会生成。\n请注意，怪物原先使用的任何物品都会被移除。这个能强制让怪物没有任何物品：\n\n<w>orc; nothing</w>\n\n有关详细信息，\n请参阅<cyan>docs/develop/levels/syntax.txt</cyan>，<cyan>ITEMS</cyan>章节。\n任何引用的斜线（<w>/</w>）都应换为管道符（<w>|</w>），\n任何逗号（<w>,</w>）都换为句号（<w>.</w>）。\n\n同样的语法也适用于创建特定类型的飞舞的武器武器：\n\n<w>dancing weapon; rapier</w>\n\n限制：如果给怪物一个随机投射器，就没有办法强制弹药类型与投射器类型匹配。\n然而，作为一种特殊情况，飞舞的武器如果是投射器，总会产生一些合适的弹药。\n\n你可以通过使用标签<w>always_corpse</w>指定怪物永远生成尸体，\n或使用<w>never_corpse</w>，这将永远不会生成尸体。\n\n默认情况下，怪物在被生成时处于睡眠状态。\n这可以通过在名称前加<w>generate_awake</w>来更改。\n\n<w>generate_awake boulder beetle</w>\n\n前缀<w>patrolling</w>防止它从生成的地方走开。\n\n你可以通过<w>god</w>:说明符设定怪物所皈依的神，如下所示：\n\n<w>orc god:the_shining_one</w>\n\n如果设定这个怪物是由指定的神所赋予的，则应以<w>god_gift</w>为后缀：\n\n<w>orc god:the_shining_one god_gift</w>\n\n注意，除非明确指定了<w>god_gift</w>，否则这个怪物是不会被视作由神所赋予的。\n\n你可以通过指定<w>att</w>:来设定怪物从属于以下之一派系：\n\n* <w>att:hostile</w> -- 默认\n* <w>att:friendly</w> -- 驯服的，会跟随你\n* <w>att:neutral</w> -- 对你和敌对怪物都有敌意\n* <w>att:good_neutral</w> -- 对敌对怪物有敌意\n* <w>att:fellow_slime</w> -- 驯服的，但不会跟随你\n\n给怪物设定颜色，覆盖其默认颜色：\n\n<w>col:darkgrey fungus</w>\n\n如果设定为<w>any</w>，将选择为一个随机的颜色。\n\n你可以使用<w>tile</w>:说明符以设定怪物的贴图，如下所示：\n\n<w>rat tile:mons_giant_bat</w>\n\n在图像版中，这会创建一个使用“<cyan>mons_giant_bat</cyan>”贴图的怪物，\n但在其他方面与老鼠相同。在ASCII模式下，这将不起作用。\n\n如果你想在生成的地图中放置一个与当前楼层匹配的随机怪物，你可以使用\n\n<w>random</w>\n\n如果你想在其他地方放置一个与之匹配的随机怪物，\n你可以在怪物语句中使用place:标签：\n\n<w>place:Abyss</w>\n\n使用<w>place</w>:表示你要生成一个随机怪物。\n你也可以在其他深度有随机怪物的楼层生成丧尸，例如：\n\n<w>place:Elf:3 zombie</w>\n\n可用的修饰语有<w>zombie</w>、<w>draugr</w>、<w>simulacrum</w>和<w>spectre</w>。\n\n<h>覆盖怪物名称</h>\n\n给单个怪物设定名字：\n\n<w>kobold name:Durwent</w>\n\n这会让怪物显示为“<cyan>Durwent the kobold</cyan>”。\n可以用_符号替换名称中的空格。\n值得注意的是，“<cyan>the <<race></cyan>”将被附加到所有名称上，\n在使用这些名称时应予以考虑。\n\n可以在名称上使用三种不同的修饰语：\n<w>name_adjective</w>、<w>name_suffix和</w>name_replace。\n<w>name_adjective</w>会把那个名称作为形容词。例如：\n\n<w>kobold name:ugly name_adjective</w>\n\n会生成“<cyan>An ugly kobold</cyan>”“<cyan>The ugly kobold hits you</cyan>”等文本。\n\n<w>name_suffix</w> does the same, but after the monster's base name:\n\n<w>kobold name:wearing_mittens name_suffix</w>\n\n会生成“<cyan>A kobold wearing mittens</cyan>”“<cyan>The kobold wearing mittens hits you</cyan>”等文本。\n\n<w>name_replace</w>会把基础名称替换为给定名称，就好像那个怪物是唯一的：\n\n<w>kobold name:Durwent name_replace</w>\n\n会生成“<cyan>Durwent</cyan>”而不是“<cyan>Durwent the kobold</cyan>”。\n而尸体则仍然是“<cyan>kobold corpse of Durwent</cyan>”。\n\n<w>name_species</w>按原样使用尸体的名称，\n而不使用“<cyan>kobold</cyan> <cyan>corpse of X</cyan>”\n作为<w>name_replace</w>的前缀。\n它也避免使通常的名称标签显示在此处贴图中独一无二的、被重命名的怪物上方。\n\n与<w>name_replace</w>或<w>name_species</w>一并使用，\n在重命名怪物时会有两个标签，但不会加到实际的“名字”中：\n<w>name_descriptor</w>, and <w>name_definite</w>。\n\n例如：\n\n<w>kobold name:goblin name_replace</w>\n\n会生成“<cyan>goblin hits you</cyan>”。然而，\n在以上标记中加入<w>name_descriptor</w>会生成“<cyan>The goblin hits you</cyan>”，\n那个怪物的描述会变为“<cyan>A goblin</cyan>”。\n\n<w>kobold name:goblin name_replace name_descriptor</w>\n\nThe <w>name_definite</w>标记会确保它永远不会被冠以“<cyan>a</cyan>”或\n“<cyan>an</cyan>”，但始终使用确定的宾语。因此，如上所述，\n你将看到“<cyan>The<cyan>goblin</cyan> <cyan>hits you</cyan>”，\n但在其描述中也将看到“<cyan>The goblin</cyan>”。\n设定<w>name_definite</w>将自动设定标记<w>name_descriptor</w>，因此意味着：\n\n<w>kobold name:goblin name_replace name_definite</w>\n\n完全等同于：\n\n<w>kobold name:goblin name_replace name_descriptor name_definite</w>\n\n<w>name_zombie</w>可以用来重新定义已经亡灵化的怪物（包括骷髅、拟像和幽灵）。\n\n最后，<w>name_nocorpse</w>能让尸体的名称完全被隐去。例如：\n\n<w>wizard name:sad name_adjective name_nocorpse</w>\n\n会让“<cyan>a sad wizard</cyan>”留下“<cyan>a wizard corpse</cyan>”。\n\n当在定义中空间受限时，可以使用以下标签的缩写形式：\n\n* <w>name_suffix</w> -> <w>n_suf</w>\n* <w>name_adjective</w> -> <w>n_adj</w>\n* <w>name_replace</w> -> <w>n_rpl</w>\n* <w>name_species</w> -> <w>n_spe</w>\n* <w>name_descriptor</w> -> <w>n_des</w>\n* <w>name_definite</w> -> <w>n_the</w>\n* <w>name_zombie</w> -> <w>n_zom</w>\n* <w>name_nocorpse</w> -> <w>n_noc</w>\n\n<h>Overriding Monster Stats</h>\n\n使用<w>hd</w>和<w>hp</w>进一步对怪物进行定制。\n可以用这两个标签完全覆盖怪物的初始生命骰和生命值：\n\n<w>kobold hd:20</w>\n\n将生成一个有着20点生命骰的狗头人。它的生命值是随机的，但取决于它的生命骰，\n而生命骰也会被再次掷出。被设定了明确的生命值的怪物，这个值则不会变化。\n\n你也可以设定一个明确的生命值：\n\n<w>kobold hp:20</w>\n\n将生成一个有着20点生命值的狗头人。\n这对于生命值非随机的怪物（如雕像）也很有用。\n\n可以通过<w>dur</w>和<w>sum</w>标签，将怪物标记为召唤生成的。\n<w>dur</w>标签接受1到6之间的整数。The <w>sum</w>授受一个法术名称\n（根据<w>spells</w>标签进行解析，如下所述）或<w>clone</w>（罗刹天）、\n<w>animate</w>（源于活化死者）、<w>chaos</w>（由纯粹的混乱生成）、\n<w>miscast</w>（由施法失误召唤）、<w>zot</w>（由佐特陷阱召唤）、\n<w>wrath</w>（由神在惩罚时所召唤）、<w>aid</w>（由神在支援时所召唤）\n中的任何一个。\n\n在这些法术中，<w>shadow_creatures</w>的效果最为显著，\n因为怪物会隐没在阴影中，而不是消散在烟雾中。使用非召唤法术也没有效果。\n\n将一个怪物标记为<w>seen</w>将覆盖系统，并强制将怪物标记为已看到；\n这意味着它不会生成诸如“<cyan>A kobold comes into view</cyan>”之类的消息。\n\n<h>覆盖怪物的法术</h>\n\n怪物的法术集可以用<w>spells</w>: 标签进行覆盖，用法如下：\n\n*<w>goblin spells:throw_flame.50.wizard</w>\n*<w>ancient lich spells:symbol_of_torment.26.wizard; glaciate.51.wizard</w>\n\n（一个法术槽列表，由用下划线替换空格的法术名称、施法频率组成，\n法术槽后面用<w>.</w>标记，法术槽用<w>;</w>进行分隔。\n<w>;</w>周围不能有空格，<w>spells</w>: 前缀后不能有空格）\n\n法术名称必须与<cyan>spl-data.h</cyan>中的名称完全一致，空格用下划线替换。\n法术频率是施放该法术的几率（200分之x）。法术标记包括：\n\n* <w>natural</w>、<w>magical</w>、<w>demonic</w>、<w>wizard</w>或\n<w>priest</w> - 施放此法术的方式。巫师和祭司能被沉默，\n并触发某些行为（特洛格会常识杀死巫师的行为，比欧弗会赏识杀死祭司的行为）。\n魔法、恶魔和巫师法术受到反魔法效果的影响。自然法术不会产生噪音。\n* <w>emergency</w> - 怪物只会在生命值低时才会施展这个法术。\n* <w>breath</w> - 该法术是一种吐息武器，使用它时会闭气，\n在此期间该法术无法再次施放。\n* <w>no_silent</w> - 该法术不能在沉默时施放，\n即使它是一种通常不受影响的能力类型。\n* <w>instant</w> - 该法术不会耗费怪物的精力；\n他们可以在一个回合内同时进行另一动作。\n* <w>noisy</w> - 强制让该法术发出噪音，即使它通常是种自然能力。\n\n你可以使用<w>none</w> 将槽指定为空。你可以用以下方式强制生成一个无法术怪物：\n\n<w>orc wizard spells:none</w>\n\n<h>Setting enchantments</h>\n\n你可以给怪物施加任何魔力。它可以启用快速、失明、具有永久性驱逐飞弹等效果。\n语法为：<w>ench:<<name>:<<duration>:<<degree></w>或\n<w>perm_ench:<<name>:<<degree></w>。\n<w><<degree></w>和<w><<duration></w>字段可以省略。\n\n例如：\n\n* <w>orc perm_ench:blind perm_ench:mad</w>\n* <w>dream sheep ench:sticky_flame</w>\n* <w>stone giant dur:1 ench:berserk</w>\n","chinese_key_line":305,"chinese_tokens":{"blank_line_bullet_count":5,"bullet_line_count":22,"lua":{"blocks":[],"errors":[]},"markup":{"balance_errors":["unclosed platform keys, and wizard","display_context":"Specific HelpDB topic rendered by the formatted help scroller.","english":"<h>Create specified monster</h>\n\nTo create a monster with the default equipment for its species, simply enter\nthe species name, such as <w>kobold</w> or <w>Yiuf</w>.\n\nA hydra or slymdra can be given a specific number of heads by calling it an\nn<w>-headed</w> <w>hydra</w> or n<w>-headed</w> <w>slymdra</w> (with a maximum of 20 heads):\n\n<w>four-headed hydra</w>\n\nTo set the colour a (very) ugly thing takes when it is first created, use:\n\n<w>white very ugly thing</w>\n\nThe valid colours are <w>brown</w>, <w>cyan</w>, <w>green</w>, <w>purple</w>, <w>red</w> and <w>white</w>.\n\nA mutant beast can be customised by prefacing it with a maturity level and/or\nhyphen-separated set of traits, as follows:\n\n<w>mature shock-weird mutant beast</w>\n\nThe valid maturity levels are <w>larval</w>, <w>juvenile</w>, <w>mature</w>, <w>elder</w> and <w>primal</w>. The\nfacets are <w>sting</w>, <w>bat</w>, <w>fire</w>, <w>weird</w>, <w>shock</w> and <w>ox</w>.\n\nA slime creature's size can be set to other than normal using the prefixes\n<w>large</w>, <w>very large</w>, <w>enormous</w> or <w>titanic</w>:\n\n<w>very large slime creature</w>\n\nIf a monster is a member of a band, you can request that it be eligible for\nband members by adding the keyword <w>band</w> to the name. For instance:\n\n<w>orc warlord band</w>\n\nSpecifying <w>band</w> doesn't force bands to be placed - it requests that the game\nuse the normal chances of creating a band. A band will only be placed if there\nis space for it.\n\nA monster can be given specific items by following the monster name with a\nsemi-colon and then with an item list. For example:\n\n<w>orc ; rapier | quick blade . chain mail | plate armour</w>\n\nwill generate an orc wielding either a rapier or a quick blade and wearing\neither chain mail or plate armour. Randarts and ego items are only generated\nif they are explicitly requested. Note that any items that the monster was\noriginally generated with will be removed and destroyed. This can be used to\nforce a monster to have no items whatsoever:\n\n<w>orc; nothing</w>\n\nFor further information, see <cyan>docs/develop/levels/syntax.txt</cyan>, section <cyan>ITEMS</cyan>.\nAny reference to a slash (<w>/</w>) should be replaced with a pipe (<w>|</w>) and any comma\n(<w>,</w>) with a full stop (<w>.</w>).\n\nThe same syntax is also used to create a specific type of dancing weapon:\n\n<w>dancing weapon; rapier</w>\n\nLimitations: If a monster is given a random launcher, there is no way to force\nthe ammo type to match the launcher type. As a special case, however, a\nlauncher dancing weapon is always generated with some suitable ammunition.\n\nYou can specify that a monster will always generate a corpse by using the tag\n<w>always_corpse</w>, or that it will never generate a corpse with <w>never_corpse</w>.\n\nBy default, monsters are generated asleep. This is changed by prefixing the\nname with <w>generate_awake</w>.\n\n<w>generate_awake boulder beetle</w>\n\nA prefix of <w>patrolling</w> prevents it from walking out of sight of the the place\nwhere it was created.\n\nYou can set the monster's god using the <w>god</w>: specifier as follows:\n\n<w>orc god:the_shining_one</w>\n\nIf the monster is to be a gift of the specified god, this should be suffixed\nwith <w>god_gift</w>:\n\n<w>orc god:the_shining_one god_gift</w>\n\nNote that the monster will not be considered a god gift unless <w>god_gift</w> is\nexplicitly set.\n\nYou can set the monster's faction by specifying <w>att</w>: one of:\n\n* <w>att:hostile</w> -- the default\n* <w>att:friendly</w> -- tame, will follow you\n* <w>att:neutral</w> -- hostile to both you and att:hostile monsters\n* <w>att:good_neutral</w> -- hostile to att:hostile\n* <w>att:fellow_slime</w> -- tame, won't follow you\n\nMonsters can be given colours that override their default colour:\n\n<w>col:darkgrey fungus</w>\n\nThe colour <w>any</w> can be given, in which case a random colour will be chosen.\n\nYou can override the displayed monster tile using the <w>tile</w>: specifier as\nfollows:\n\n<w>rat tile:mons_giant_bat</w>\n\nIn tiles version, this would create a monster that uses the \"<cyan>mons_giant_bat</cyan>\"\ntile, but is otherwise identical to a rat. In ASCII mode, this will do\nnothing.\n\nIf you want to place a random monster suitable for the level the map is\ngenerated on, you can use\n\n<w>random</w>\n\nIf you want to place a random monster suitable for some other place, you can\nuse a place: tag in the monster spec:\n\n<w>place:Abyss</w>\n\nUsing <w>place</w>: implies that you want a random monster. You can also request\nzombies from random monsters suitable for some other depth as (e.g.):\n\n<w>place:Elf:3 zombie</w>\n\nThe available modifiers are <w>zombie</w>, <w>draugr</w>, <w>simulacrum</w>, and <w>spectre</w>.\n\n<h>Overriding Monster Names</h>\n\nIndividual monsters can be given names as follows:\n\n<w>kobold name:Durwent</w>\n\nThis will cause the monster to appear as \"<cyan>Durwent the kobold</cyan>\". Spaces can be\nplaced in the name by substituting them with the _ symbol. It is worth noting\nthat \"<cyan>the <<race></cyan>\" will be appended to all names, which should be considered\nwhen coming up with them.\n\nThere are three different modifiers that can be used on a name:\n<w>name_adjective</w>, <w>name_suffix and </w>name_replace. <w>name_adjective</w> makes the\nname act as an adjective. For example:\n\n<w>kobold name:ugly name_adjective</w>\n\nWill cause \"<cyan>An ugly kobold</cyan>\", \"<cyan>The ugly kobold hits you</cyan>\", and so on.\n\n<w>name_suffix</w> does the same, but after the monster's base name:\n\n<w>kobold name:wearing_mittens name_suffix</w>\n\nWill give \"<cyan>A kobold wearing mittens</cyan>\", \"<cyan>The kobold wearing mittens hits you</cyan>\",\nand so on.\n\n<w>name_replace</w> causes the base name to be replaced by given name, as if the\nmonster was a unique:\n\n<w>kobold name:Durwent name_replace</w>\n\nWill result in \"<cyan>Durwent</cyan>\" rather than \"<cyan>Durwent the kobold</cyan>\". The corpse will\nstill be a \"<cyan>kobold corpse of Durwent</cyan>\".\n\n<w>name_species</w> uses the name for corpses as-is, without prefixing it with\n\"<cyan>kobold</cyan> <cyan>corpse of X</cyan>\" for <w>name_replace</w>. It also avoids the usual name tag\nthat displays above unique and renamed monsters in local tiles.\n\nIn combination with <w>name_replace</w> or <w>name_species</w>, there are two further tags\nthat can be used when renaming monsters, but not giving them an actual \"name\":\n<w>name_descriptor</w>, and <w>name_definite</w>.\n\nFor example:\n\n<w>kobold name:goblin name_replace</w>\n\nWill result in \"<cyan>goblin hits you</cyan>\". However, adding <w>name_descriptor</w> from the\nabove flags will result in \"<cyan>The goblin hits you</cyan>\" and in the description of the\nmonster, \"<cyan>A goblin</cyan>\".\n\n<w>kobold name:goblin name_replace name_descriptor</w>\n\nThe <w>name_definite</w> flag ensures that it is never referred to as \"<cyan>a</cyan>\" or \"<cyan>an</cyan>\",\nbut that the definite object is always used. Therefore, you will see \"<cyan>The\n<cyan>goblin</cyan> <cyan>hits you</cyan>\", as per above, but also \"<cyan>The goblin</cyan>\" in its description.\nSetting <w>name_definite</w> will automatically set the flag <w>name_descriptor</w>,\ntherefore meaning that:\n\n<w>kobold name:goblin name_replace name_definite</w>\n\nis exactly the same as:\n\n<w>kobold name:goblin name_replace name_descriptor name_definite</w>\n\n<w>name_zombie</w> can be used to redefine already zombified monsters (including\ndraugr, simulacra and spectrals).\n\nFinally, <w>name_nocorpse</w> will cause the name to be completely ignored for\ncorpses. For example:\n\n<w>wizard name:sad name_adjective name_nocorpse</w>\n\nwill result in \"<cyan>a sad wizard</cyan>\" that leaves \"<cyan>a wizard corpse</cyan>\".\n\nFor instances where space in definitions is limited, you can use abbreviated\nforms of these tags:\n\n* <w>name_suffix</w> -> <w>n_suf</w>\n* <w>name_adjective</w> -> <w>n_adj</w>\n* <w>name_replace</w> -> <w>n_rpl</w>\n* <w>name_species</w> -> <w>n_spe</w>\n* <w>name_descriptor</w> -> <w>n_des</w>\n* <w>name_definite</w> -> <w>n_the</w>\n* <w>name_zombie</w> -> <w>n_zom</w>\n* <w>name_nocorpse</w> -> <w>n_noc</w>\n\n<h>Overriding Monster Stats</h>\n\nFurther monster customisation can be made by using <w>hd</w> and <w>hp</w>. These two tags\nallow you to completely overwrite the original hit dice and hit points of a\nmonster:\n\n<w>kobold hd:20</w>\n\nCreates a kobold with a hit dice of 20. As its hp is randomised, based on its\nhit dice, this will also be re-rolled. Monsters with a specific hit point\nvalue will not have this value change.\n\nYou can also have a specific value for hit points:\n\n<w>kobold hp:20</w>\n\nCreates a kobold with 20 hit points. This is also useful for monsters (such as\nstatues) which come with non-randomised HP values.\n\nMonsters can be marked as summoned by using <w>dur</w> and <w>sum</w> tags. The <w>dur</w> tag\naccepts an integer between 1 and 6 inclusive. The <w>sum</w> tag accepts the name of\na spell (parsed as per the <w>spells</w> tag, described below) or any of <w>clone</w>\n(Rakshasa), <w>animate</w> (from animate dead), <w>chaos</w> (made from pure chaos), <w>miscast</w>\n(summoned from by spell miscast effect), <w>zot</w> (summoned by a Zot trap), <w>wrath</w>\n(summoned by a god in an act of retribution), <w>aid</w> (summoned by a god in order\nto give aid).\n\nOf the spells, <w>shadow_creatures</w> has the most significant effect, in that\nmonsters will dissipate into the shadows rather than disappear in a puff of\nsmoke. Using non-summoning spells will also have no effect.\n\nTagging a monster with <w>seen</w> will override the system and force that monster to\nbe marked as already viewed; this means that it won't generate messages such\nas \"<cyan>A kobold comes into view</cyan>\".\n\n<h>Overriding Monster Spells</h>\n\nMonster spell sets can be overridden with a <w>spells</w>: tag, used as follows:\n\n*<w>goblin spells:throw_flame.50.wizard</w>\n*<w>ancient lich spells:symbol_of_torment.26.wizard; glaciate.51.wizard</w>\n\n(a list of spell slots, consisting of a spell name with spaces replaced with\nunderscores, a frequency of casting and set of spell slot flags specified\nafterwards with <w>.</w>, with slots separated by <w>;</w>. There should be no spaces around\nthe <w>;</w> or after the <w>spells</w>: prefix)\n\nSpell names must exactly match the names in <cyan>spl-data.h</cyan>, with spaces replaced\nby underscores. Spell frequencies are an (x in 200) chance that that spell\nwill be cast. Spell flags include:\n\n* <w>natural</w>, <w>magical</w>, <w>demonic</w>, <w>wizard</w>, or <w>priest</w> - the manner in which this\nspell is being cast. Wizards and priests can be silenced and will trigger\nappropriate conducts (Trog will appreciate killing wizards, Beogh will\nappreciate killing priests). Magical, demonic, and wizard spells are subject\nto anti-magic effects. Natural spells do not generate noise.\n* <w>emergency</w> - the monster will only cast the spell when low on health.\n* <w>breath</w> - the spell is a breath weapon, and using it triggers a breath\ntimeout during which the spell cannot be cast again.\n* <w>no_silent</w> - the spell cannot be used while silent, even if it is an ability\ntype normally not subject to silence.\n* <w>instant</w> - the spell does not expend the monster's energy; they may take\nanother action on the same turn.\n* <w>noisy</w> - forces the spell to make noise even if it is a natural ability.\n\nYou may use <w>none</w> specify that a slot should be left empty. You can force a\nspell-less monster with:\n\n<w>orc wizard spells:none</w>\n\n<h>Setting enchantments</h>\n\nYou may give a monster any number of enchantments. It may start hasted, blind,\nhave permanent RMsl, etc. The syntax is <w>ench:<<name>:<<duration>:<<degree></w> or\n<w>perm_ench:<<name>:<<degree></w>. The <w><<degree></w> and <w><<duration></w> fields may be\nomitted.\n\nFor example:\n\n* <w>orc perm_ench:blind perm_ench:mad</w>\n* <w>dream sheep ench:sticky_flame</w>\n* <w>stone giant dur:1 ench:berserk</w>\n","english_key_line":312,"english_tokens":{"blank_line_bullet_count":5,"bullet_line_count":22,"lua":{"blocks":[],"errors":[]},"markup":{"balance_errors":["unclosed ench:berserk"]},"fact_sha256":"3096b1600cb0f54a09a9e772e4abe462508a89b9224ae1a242dddebb20662761","identity":"help:wiz-monster","key":"wiz-monster","kind":"help","lifecycle":"wizard-only","producer":[{"guards":["WIZARD"],"location":"crawl-ref/source/wiz-mon.cc:66","path":"crawl-ref/source/wiz-mon.cc"}],"structural_differences":["format_tag_counts","format_tag_sequence","path_literals","w_contents"],"token_contract_equal":{"blank_line_bullet_count":true,"bullet_line_count":true,"format_tag_counts":false,"format_tag_sequence":false,"lua_block_count":true,"lua_comparison_literals":true,"lua_control_skeleton":true,"numbers":true,"option_literals":true,"path_literals":false,"quoted_command_literals":true,"technical_angle_counts":true,"technical_angle_sequence":true,"urls":true,"w_contents":false}},"proposed_translation":"<h>生成指定怪物</h>\n\n要生成带有其物种默认装备的怪物，只需输入物种名称，\n例如<w>kobold</w>或<w>Yiuf</w>。\n\n可以把hydra或slymdra生成为指定头数，分别写作\nn<w>-headed</w> <w>hydra</w>或n<w>-headed</w> <w>slymdra</w>（头数为1至20）：\n\n<w>four-headed hydra</w>\n\n要设置（极）丑物刚生成时的颜色，可使用：\n\n<w>white very ugly thing</w>\n\n有效颜色为<w>brown</w>、<w>cyan</w>、<w>green</w>、<w>purple</w>、\n<w>red</w>和<w>white</w>。\n\n要定制变异兽，可在其名称前加上成熟度和/或一组以连字符分隔的特征：\n\n<w>mature shock-weird mutant beast</w>\n\n有效成熟度为<w>larval</w>、<w>juvenile</w>、<w>mature</w>、\n<w>elder</w>和<w>primal</w>。特征为<w>sting</w>、<w>bat</w>、<w>fire</w>、\n<w>weird</w>、<w>shock</w>和<w>ox</w>。\n\n要让黏液生物使用非正常体型，可加上前缀<w>large</w>、\n<w>very large</w>、<w>enormous</w>或<w>titanic</w>：\n\n<w>very large slime creature</w>\n\n如果怪物属于某种怪物群，可以在名称中加入关键词<w>band</w>，\n请求让它有资格带上同群成员。例如：\n\n<w>orc warlord band</w>\n\n指定<w>band</w>不会强制放置怪物群，只会让游戏按正常概率尝试生成。\n只有空间足够时才会放置怪物群。\n\n要给怪物指定物品，可在怪物名称后加分号，再接物品列表。例如：\n\n<w>orc ; rapier | quick blade . chain mail | plate armour</w>\n\n这会生成一个持握刺剑或迅捷之刃、穿戴锁子甲或板甲的兽人。\n只有明确请求时才会生成随机神器或附魔装备。\n请注意，怪物最初生成时携带的所有物品都会被移除并销毁。\n也可以借此强制怪物完全不携带物品：\n\n<w>orc; nothing</w>\n\n更多信息请参阅<cyan>docs/develop/levels/syntax.txt</cyan>中的\n<cyan>ITEMS</cyan>一节。这里的斜线（<w>/</w>）都应替换为竖线（<w>|</w>），\n逗号（<w>,</w>）都应替换为句点（<w>.</w>）。\n\n同一语法也可用于生成特定类型的舞动武器：\n\n<w>dancing weapon; rapier</w>\n\n限制：如果给怪物随机生成发射武器，就无法强制弹药类型与发射武器匹配。\n但有一个特例：舞动武器若为发射武器，总会同时生成一些合适的弹药。\n\n使用标签<w>always_corpse</w>可指定怪物必定生成尸体，\n使用<w>never_corpse</w>则可指定它绝不生成尸体。\n\n怪物默认在睡眠状态下生成。在名称前加上<w>generate_awake</w>可改变这一点。\n\n<w>generate_awake boulder beetle</w>\n\n前缀<w>patrolling</w>会阻止怪物走出其生成地点的视野范围。\n\n可以用<w>god</w>:说明符设置怪物的神祇：\n\n<w>orc god:the_shining_one</w>\n\n如果该怪物应当是指定神祇赐予的礼物，还需在其后加上<w>god_gift</w>：\n\n<w>orc god:the_shining_one god_gift</w>\n\n请注意，只有明确指定<w>god_gift</w>，怪物才会被视为神祇赠礼。\n\n可以用<w>att</w>:指定怪物的阵营，取值如下：\n\n* <w>att:hostile</w> -- 默认\n* <w>att:friendly</w> -- 驯服，会跟随你\n* <w>att:neutral</w> -- 对你和att:hostile怪物都抱有敌意\n* <w>att:good_neutral</w> -- 对att:hostile怪物抱有敌意\n* <w>att:fellow_slime</w> -- 驯服，但不会跟随你\n\n可以给怪物指定颜色，覆盖其默认颜色：\n\n<w>col:darkgrey fungus</w>\n\n也可以指定颜色<w>any</w>，此时会随机选择颜色。\n\n可以用<w>tile</w>:说明符覆盖怪物显示的贴图：\n\n<w>rat tile:mons_giant_bat</w>\n\n在贴图版中，这会生成一个使用“<cyan>mons_giant_bat</cyan>”贴图、\n其他方面与老鼠完全相同的怪物。在ASCII模式中，这项设置不起作用。\n\n要放置适合当前地图生成楼层的随机怪物，可以使用：\n\n<w>random</w>\n\n要放置适合其他地点的随机怪物，可以在怪物规格中使用place:标签：\n\n<w>place:Abyss</w>\n\n使用<w>place</w>:即表示要生成随机怪物。\n也可以请求把适合其他深度的随机怪物生成为亡灵，例如：\n\n<w>place:Elf:3 zombie</w>\n\n可用修饰语为<w>zombie</w>、<w>draugr</w>、<w>simulacrum</w>和<w>spectre</w>。\n\n<h>覆盖怪物名称</h>\n\n可以按如下方式给单个怪物命名：\n\n<w>kobold name:Durwent</w>\n\n这会让怪物显示为“<cyan>Durwent the kobold</cyan>”。\n名称中的空格可以用_符号代替。\n值得注意的是，“<cyan>the <<race></cyan>”将被附加到所有名称上，\n因此构思名称时应考虑这一点。\n\n名称可以使用三种不同的修饰标签：<w>name_adjective</w>、\n<w>name_suffix</w>和<w>name_replace</w>。<w>name_adjective</w>会让名称充当形容词。例如：\n\n<w>kobold name:ugly name_adjective</w>\n\n会产生“<cyan>An ugly kobold</cyan>”“<cyan>The ugly kobold hits you</cyan>”等文本。\n\n<w>name_suffix</w>的作用相同，但名称会放在怪物基础名称之后：\n\n<w>kobold name:wearing_mittens name_suffix</w>\n\n会产生“<cyan>A kobold wearing mittens</cyan>”\n“<cyan>The kobold wearing mittens hits you</cyan>”等文本。\n\n<w>name_replace</w>会用给定名称替换基础名称，如同该怪物是独有怪物：\n\n<w>kobold name:Durwent name_replace</w>\n\n结果是“<cyan>Durwent</cyan>”而不是“<cyan>Durwent the kobold</cyan>”，\n但尸体仍会显示为“<cyan>kobold corpse of Durwent</cyan>”。\n\n<w>name_species</w>会让尸体原样使用该名称；与<w>name_replace</w>一起使用时，\n不会在尸体名称前加上“<cyan>kobold</cyan> <cyan>corpse of X</cyan>”。\n在本地贴图版中，它还会避免在独有怪物和改名怪物上方显示通常的名称标签。\n\n与<w>name_replace</w>或<w>name_species</w>组合使用时，还有两个标签可用于\n重命名怪物、但不赋予怪物实际的“名字”：<w>name_descriptor</w>和<w>name_definite</w>。\n\n例如：\n\n<w>kobold name:goblin name_replace</w>\n\n这会产生“<cyan>goblin hits you</cyan>”。但加入<w>name_descriptor</w>后，\n会产生“<cyan>The goblin hits you</cyan>”，怪物描述则会显示为\n“<cyan>A goblin</cyan>”。\n\n<w>kobold name:goblin name_replace name_descriptor</w>\n\n<w>name_definite</w>标签确保怪物绝不会使用“<cyan>a</cyan>”或\n“<cyan>an</cyan>”，而是始终使用定冠词。因此会显示\n“<cyan>The goblin hits you</cyan>”，怪物描述也会显示为\n“<cyan>The goblin</cyan>”。设置<w>name_definite</w>会自动设置\n<w>name_descriptor</w>，所以：\n\n<w>kobold name:goblin name_replace name_definite</w>\n\n完全等同于：\n\n<w>kobold name:goblin name_replace name_descriptor name_definite</w>\n\n<w>name_zombie</w>可用于重新定义已经亡灵化的怪物\n（包括draugr（尸鬼）、拟像和幽魂）。\n\n最后，<w>name_nocorpse</w>会让尸体完全忽略该名称。例如：\n\n<w>wizard name:sad name_adjective name_nocorpse</w>\n\n这会让“<cyan>a sad wizard</cyan>”留下“<cyan>a wizard corpse</cyan>”。\n\n如果定义中的空间有限，可以使用这些标签的缩写：\n\n* <w>name_suffix</w> -> <w>n_suf</w>\n* <w>name_adjective</w> -> <w>n_adj</w>\n* <w>name_replace</w> -> <w>n_rpl</w>\n* <w>name_species</w> -> <w>n_spe</w>\n* <w>name_descriptor</w> -> <w>n_des</w>\n* <w>name_definite</w> -> <w>n_the</w>\n* <w>name_zombie</w> -> <w>n_zom</w>\n* <w>name_nocorpse</w> -> <w>n_noc</w>\n\n<h>覆盖怪物属性</h>\n\n可以用<w>hd</w>和<w>hp</w>进一步定制怪物。\n这两个标签能完全覆盖怪物原有的生命骰和生命值：\n\n<w>kobold hd:20</w>\n\n这会生成一个生命骰为20的狗头人。它的HP会根据生命骰随机生成，\n因此修改HD后也会按新的HD重新掷出HP。显式指定了HP的怪物，其HP不会因此改变。\n\n也可以显式指定生命值：\n\n<w>kobold hp:20</w>\n\n这会生成一个生命值为20的狗头人。\n这对生命值原本不随机的怪物（如雕像）也很有用。\n\n可以用<w>dur</w>和<w>sum</w>标签把怪物标记为召唤物。\n<w>dur</w>标签接受1到6（含）之间的整数。<w>sum</w>标签接受法术名称\n（按下文所述的<w>spells</w>标签解析），也接受以下任一值：\n<w>clone</w>（Rakshasa，罗刹）、<w>animate</w>（来自“操纵死尸”）、\n<w>chaos</w>（由纯粹混沌生成）、<w>miscast</w>（由施法失误效果召唤）、\n<w>zot</w>（由佐特陷阱召唤）、<w>wrath</w>（神祇降下惩罚时召唤）、\n<w>aid</w>（神祇提供援助时召唤）。\n\n在法术中，<w>shadow_creatures</w>（暗影生物）的影响最明显：\n怪物会消散在暗影中，而不是化作一团烟雾消失。使用非召唤法术则不会产生效果。\n\n给怪物加上<w>seen</w>标签会覆盖系统设置，强制把它标记为已经见过；\n因此不会产生“<cyan>A kobold comes into view</cyan>”之类的消息。\n\n<h>覆盖怪物法术</h>\n\n可以用<w>spells</w>:标签覆盖怪物的法术集：\n\n*<w>goblin spells:throw_flame.50.wizard</w>\n*<w>ancient lich spells:symbol_of_torment.26.wizard; glaciate.51.wizard</w>\n\n（这是法术槽列表。每个槽由法术名称、施法频率和随后指定的法术槽标志组成；\n法术名称中的空格用下划线替换，各部分以<w>.</w>分隔，法术槽以<w>;</w>分隔。\n<w>;</w>两侧以及<w>spells</w>:前缀之后都不能有空格。）\n\n法术名称必须与<cyan>spl-data.h</cyan>中的名称完全一致，空格用下划线替换。\n法术频率表示施放该法术的概率（x/200）。法术标志包括：\n\n* <w>natural</w>、<w>magical</w>、<w>vocal</w>、<w>wizard</w>或\n<w>priest</w> —— 施放法术的方式。wizard、priest和vocal法术受沉默影响；\nwizard和priest法术会触发相应的\n神祇行为判定（特洛格会赞赏杀死巫师，比欧弗会赞赏杀死祭司）。\nmagical和wizard法术受禁魔效果影响。\nnatural和magical法术默认无声，除非标有noisy。\n* <w>emergency</w> —— 怪物只会在生命值较低时施放该法术。\n* <w>breath</w> —— 该法术是吐息武器；使用后会触发吐息冷却，\n冷却期间不能再次施放该法术。\n* <w>short_range</w> —— 仅在目标距离较近时施放该法术。\n* <w>long_range</w> —— 仅在目标距离较远时施放该法术。\n* <w>instant</w> —— 该法术不消耗怪物的能量；怪物可在同一回合采取另一个行动。\n* <w>noisy</w> —— 强制该法术发出声响，即使它属于默认无声的类型。\n\n可以用<w>none</w>指定法术槽为空。要强制生成无法术怪物，可使用：\n\n<w>orc wizard spells:none</w>\n\n<h>设置附魔效果</h>\n\n可以给怪物添加任意数量的附魔效果。怪物可以在生成时处于加速、失明状态，\n具有永久 RMsl 等。语法为<w>ench:<<name>:<<duration>:<<degree></w>或\n<w>perm_ench:<<name>:<<degree></w>。\n<w><<degree></w>和<w><<duration></w>字段均可省略。\n\n例如：\n\n* <w>orc perm_ench:blind perm_ench:mad</w>\n* <w>dream sheep ench:sticky_flame</w>\n* <w>stone giant dur:1 ench:berserk</w>\n","reentry_trigger":"对应英文源文、生产 consumer、格式约定或 docs/glossary.md 裁决发生变化时重新审阅。","rejected_alternatives":["仅作零散词语替换：跨段落的事实、列表或格式问题无法得到完整修复。","原样保留基线译文：已确认的语义或结构缺陷会继续展示给玩家。"],"reviewer_rationale":"基线混入不支持的 demonic/no_silent、遗漏 vocal/range 标志，并有破损 markup 与大量语义缺口；必须按当前 parser 全文重译。","terminal_conclusion":"retranslate"}
{"actual_behavior":"胜利目标是取得佐特宝珠并返回地面，进入佐特领域前需从不同分支收集三个符文；新手可先尝试多种角色。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"answer_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2368","bullet_transform":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2374","key_enumeration":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2357","menu":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:456","question_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:472"},"current_chinese":{"answer":"A: 显然是为了生存和胜利。为此你需要深入地牢找到佐特宝珠，然后把它带回地面。\n你需要先要从地牢的不同分支收集三个符文，才能进入佐特领域。\n\n但如果你是刚开始玩这个游戏，这些你就都不用考虑了。\n先试着用各种角色、种族、技能集和信仰，尽可能深地探索Crawl的各个分支，\n体验其中的乐趣。","question":"这个游戏的终极目标是什么？"},"current_english":{"answer":"A: To survive and to win, obviously. For this you need to find the fabled Orb\nof Zot deep down in the dungeon and bring it back to the surface. To enter the\nrealm of Zot, you'll first need to collect three runes to be found in various\nbranches of the dungeon.\n\nHowever, if you're new to the game, you really shouldn't worry about this. Just\ntry to delve as deeply as possible and have fun exploring the many side\nbranches in Crawl with various characters of different species, skill sets, and\nreligions.","question":"What exactly is the goal of this game?"},"deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"gameplay goals, survival, and mechanics","display_context":"FAQ menu question plus unwrapped answer; To enter the\nrealm of Zot, you'll first need to collect three runes to be found in various\nbranches of the dungeon.\n\nHowever, if you're new to the game, you really shouldn't worry about this. Just\ntry to delve as deeply as possible and have fun exploring the many side\nbranches in Crawl with various characters of different species, skill sets, and\nreligions.","question":"What exactly is the goal of this game?"},"english_key_lines":{"answer":47,"question":43},"english_tokens":{"answer":{"blank_line_bullet_count":0,"bullet_line_count":0,"lua":{"blocks":[],"errors":[]},"markup":{"balance_errors":[],"malformed":[],"tag_counts":{},"tag_sequence":[],"technical_angle_counts":{},"technical_angle_sequence":[]},"numbers":[],"option_literals":[],"path_literals":[],"quoted_command_literals":[],"urls":[],"w_contents":[]},"question":{"blank_line_bullet_count":0,"bullet_line_count":0,"lua":{"blocks":[],"errors":[]},"markup":{"balance_errors":[],"malformed":[],"tag_counts":{},"tag_sequence":[],"technical_angle_counts":{},"technical_angle_sequence":[]},"numbers":[],"option_literals":[],"path_literals":[],"quoted_command_literals":[],"urls":[],"w_contents":[]}},"fact_sha256":"7a72f98c1347409e14f0de7e1ff8a8cec30096f5f7e8b5e2b1587c0ed86ab1a8","identity":"faq:goal","key":"goal","kind":"faq","lifecycle":"current-faq-menu-entry","producer":{"chinese_question_order":0,"english_question_order":0,"source_note":"File order is authoring evidence; runtime menu order is DBM iteration order and is not guaranteed by the current consumer."},"structural_differences":[],"token_contract_equal":{"answer":{"blank_line_bullet_count":true,"bullet_line_count":true,"format_tag_counts":true,"format_tag_sequence":true,"lua_block_count":true,"lua_comparison_literals":true,"lua_control_skeleton":true,"numbers":true,"option_literals":true,"path_literals":true,"quoted_command_literals":true,"technical_angle_counts":true,"technical_angle_sequence":true,"urls":true,"w_contents":true},"question":{"blank_line_bullet_count":true,"bullet_line_count":true,"format_tag_counts":true,"format_tag_sequence":true,"lua_block_count":true,"lua_comparison_literals":true,"lua_control_skeleton":true,"numbers":true,"option_literals":true,"path_literals":true,"quoted_command_literals":true,"technical_angle_counts":true,"technical_angle_sequence":true,"urls":true,"w_contents":true}}},"proposed_translation":{"answer":"A: 显然是生存并获胜。为此，你需要深入地牢，找到传说中的佐特宝珠，\n并将其带回地面。要进入佐特领域，首先需要收集散落在地牢各个分支中的三个符文。\n\n不过，如果你刚接触这个游戏，实在不必为此担心。只管尝试尽可能深入地前进，\n并用不同种族、技能组合和信仰的各种角色探索Crawl众多支线，享受其中的乐趣。","question":"这个游戏的目标到底是什么？"},"reentry_trigger":"对应英文源文、生产 consumer、格式约定或 docs/glossary.md 裁决发生变化时重新审阅。","rejected_alternatives":["原样保留基线译文：已确认的局部歧义、遗漏或术语问题仍会存在。","整项重译：问题范围局部，扩大改写会增加不必要的回归面。"],"reviewer_rationale":"基线基本正确但有语病和角色/分支表述不顺；局部润色并保留三符文事实。","terminal_conclusion":"adjust"}
{"actual_behavior":"本地游戏的 chardump 与结算高分会指出 morgue 位置，saves 与 morgue 同级，morgue_dir/save_dir 可分别配置。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"answer_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2368","bullet_transform":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2374","key_enumeration":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2357","menu":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:456","question_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:472"},"current_chinese":{"answer":"如果你玩的是单机版，chardump命令可以指引你那些死亡记录文件存在哪里，\n在游戏结束后的高分榜上也有这些信息。\n\nsaves/和morgue/目录位于同一目录之中。\n\n如果你想改变保存的位置，\n在init文件中更改morgue_dir和 save_dir的配置参数就可以了。","question":"我找不到保存的文件，死亡记录和角色记录。他们都在哪儿呢？"},"current_english":{"answer":"If you're playing locally (i.e. not on one of the servers), the chardump\ncommand will tell you where the morgue file is written, and this information is\nalso displayed below the highscore at the end of a game.\n\nThe saves/ directory can be found in the same directory as the morgue/ one.\n\nIf you'd like to change either location, modify the morgue_dir and/or save_dir\noptions in the init file.","question":"I can't find my save files, morgue and character dumps. {"actual_behavior":"条目列出种族/背景权重、骨骸文件、不可出售、抗性来源、可学习获胜，以及 o/G/X/Ctrl-F/Tab 等界面命令。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"answer_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2368","bullet_transform":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2374","key_enumeration":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2357","menu":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:456","question_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:472"},"current_chinese":{"answer":"A: 首先，你得去读读手册（'?N'）中“游戏哲学”这一章节，\n这里面写了关于石头汤这个游戏设计思路的很多关键要点。\n\n至于具体差异，最重要的可能是以下几点：\n\n* 种族要比角色职业（或“类别”）重要*得多*。\n\n* 骨档包含鬼魂，但不会记录生前的装备和死亡的原因。\n\n* 你无法在商店或别的地方卖出东西。\n\n* 可以通过变异、物品来获得抗性，但吃东西可不行。\n\n* 死亡是难免的，但新手也能在不看攻略的情况下取胜，\n  如果他们能从错误中汲取教训的话。优秀的玩家甚至以不断取得几次连胜。\n\n此外，我们旨在为Crawl提供一个真正人性化的界面，因此，\n你应该花些时间阅读命令列表('??')。下列命令非常方便实用：\n\n* 'o'，自动探索一个未知楼层\n\n* 'G'，自动前往另一楼层（'G?'查看帮助）\n\n* 'X'，显示楼层地图，以进行移动或执行其他任务（'X?'）\n\n* 'Ctrl-F'寻找之前遇到的物品（'Ctrl-F?'）\n\n* 'Tab'，自动接近、攻击附近的怪物","question":"和其他主流的roguelike游戏相比，最大的不同点在哪里呢？"},"current_english":{"answer":"A: First, you might want to read the philosophy section in the manual ('?N') to\nread about general important principles in Stone Soup's game design.\n\nAs for specific differences, the most important are probably the following:\n\n* Species is *much* more important than the character background (or \"class\").\n\n* Bones files only contain ghosts, never their equipment or cause of death.\n\n* You cannot sell items to shops or elsewhere.\n\n* Resistances can be gained via mutations or items, never through eating.\n\n* There are inevitable deaths, but new players can win without spoilers, if\n  they learn from their mistakes. Very good players can win several consecutive\n  games.\n\nAlso, our aim is to give Crawl a really smooth interface, so you should really\nread through the command list ('??') some time. The following commands are\nparticularly convenient:\n\n* 'o', for automatic exploration of an unknown level\n\n* 'G', for automatic travel to another level ('G?' for help)\n\n* 'X', to access the level map for travelling and other tasks ('X?')\n\n* 'Ctrl-F', to find and travel to items you've seen before ('Ctrl-F?')\n\n* 'Tab', to move towards or attack the nearest monster","question":"What are the biggest differences to other popular roguelike games?"},"deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"gameplay goals, survival, and mechanics","display_context":"FAQ menu question plus unwrapped answer; blank lines before asterisk bullets become Unicode bullets.","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/database/FAQ.txt:74","crawl-ref/source/dat/database/FAQ.txt:78","crawl-ref/source/dat/database/zh/FAQ.txt:63","crawl-ref/source/dat/database/zh/FAQ.txt:67"],"fact_sha256":"bf16d842dbe02e65491b0985c9b988459cf7e465fc5c10815ade3928b4aa860e","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md@95eeacf9704e046c2010ef34859b750d2f8a1937ad87c4a86e8a404c98689407","identity":"faq:roguelike difference","lifecycle":"current-faq-menu-entry","producer":{"chinese_question_order":2,"english_question_order":2,"source_note":"File order is authoring evidence; blank lines before asterisk bullets become Unicode bullets.","english":{"answer":"A: First, you might want to read the philosophy section in the manual ('?N') to\nread about general important principles in Stone Soup's game design.\n\nAs for specific differences, the most important are probably the following:\n\n* Species is *much* more important than the character background (or \"class\").\n\n* Bones files only contain ghosts, never their equipment or cause of death.\n\n* You cannot sell items to shops or elsewhere.\n\n* Resistances can be gained via mutations or items, never through eating.\n\n* There are inevitable deaths, but new players can win without spoilers, if\n  they learn from their mistakes. Very good players can win several consecutive\n  games.\n\nAlso, our aim is to give Crawl a really smooth interface, so you should really\nread through the command list ('??') some time. The following commands are\nparticularly convenient:\n\n* 'o', for automatic exploration of an unknown level\n\n* 'G', for automatic travel to another level ('G?' for help)\n\n* 'X', to access the level map for travelling and other tasks ('X?')\n\n* 'Ctrl-F', to find and travel to items you've seen before ('Ctrl-F?')\n\n* 'Tab', to move towards or attack the nearest monster","question":"What are the biggest differences to other popular roguelike games?"},"english_key_lines":{"answer":78,"question":74},"english_tokens":{"answer":{"blank_line_bullet_count":10,"bullet_line_count":10,"lua":{"blocks":[],"errors":[]},"markup":{"balance_errors":[],"malformed":[],"tag_counts":{},"tag_sequence":[],"technical_angle_counts":{},"technical_angle_sequence":[]},"numbers":[],"option_literals":[],"path_literals":[],"quoted_command_literals":["?N","??","o","G","G?","X","X?","Ctrl-F","Ctrl-F?","Tab"],"urls":[],"w_contents":[]},"question":{"blank_line_bullet_count":0,"bullet_line_count":0,"lua":{"blocks":[],"errors":[]},"markup":{"balance_errors":[],"malformed":[],"tag_counts":{},"tag_sequence":[],"technical_angle_counts":{},"technical_angle_sequence":[]},"numbers":[],"option_literals":[],"path_literals":[],"quoted_command_literals":[],"urls":[],"w_contents":[]}},"fact_sha256":"bf16d842dbe02e65491b0985c9b988459cf7e465fc5c10815ade3928b4aa860e","identity":"faq:roguelike difference","key":"roguelike difference","kind":"faq","lifecycle":"current-faq-menu-entry","producer":{"chinese_question_order":2,"english_question_order":2,"source_note":"File order is authoring evidence; runtime menu order is DBM iteration order and is not guaranteed by the current consumer."},"structural_differences":[],"token_contract_equal":{"answer":{"blank_line_bullet_count":true,"bullet_line_count":true,"format_tag_counts":true,"format_tag_sequence":true,"lua_block_count":true,"lua_comparison_literals":true,"lua_control_skeleton":true,"numbers":true,"option_literals":true,"path_literals":true,"quoted_command_literals":true,"technical_angle_counts":true,"technical_angle_sequence":true,"urls":true,"w_contents":true},"question":{"blank_line_bullet_count":true,"bullet_line_count":true,"format_tag_counts":true,"format_tag_sequence":true,"lua_block_count":true,"lua_comparison_literals":true,"lua_control_skeleton":true,"numbers":true,"option_literals":true,"path_literals":true,"quoted_command_literals":true,"technical_angle_counts":true,"technical_angle_sequence":true,"urls":true,"w_contents":true}}},"proposed_translation":{"answer":"A: 首先，你或许应该阅读手册中的设计理念章节（'?N'），\n了解Stone Soup游戏设计的一般重要原则。\n\n至于具体差异，最重要的可能是以下几点：\n\n* 种族比角色背景（或“职业”）重要*得多*。\n\n* 骨骸文件只包含鬼魂，绝不包含其装备或死因。\n\n* 你不能向商店或其他任何地方出售物品。\n\n* 抗性可以通过变异或物品获得，绝不能通过进食获得。\n\n* 死亡是难免的，但新手也能在不看攻略的情况下取胜，\n  只要能从错误中吸取教训。非常优秀的玩家可以连续赢下好几局。\n\n此外，我们致力于为Crawl提供非常流畅的界面，所以你确实应该找时间\n通读命令列表（'??'）。以下命令尤其方便：\n\n* 'o'，自动探索未知楼层\n\n* 'G'，自动前往另一楼层（用'G?'查看帮助）\n\n* 'X'，打开楼层地图，以便旅行及执行其他任务（'X?'）\n\n* 'Ctrl-F'，查找并前往以前见过的物品（'Ctrl-F?'）\n\n* 'Tab'，移向或攻击最近的怪物","question":"与其他流行的roguelike游戏相比，最大的区别是什么？"},"reentry_trigger":"对应英文源文、生产 consumer、格式约定或 docs/glossary.md 裁决发生变化时重新审阅。","rejected_alternatives":["仅作零散词语替换：跨段落的事实、列表或格式问题无法得到完整修复。","原样保留基线译文：已确认的语义或结构缺陷会继续展示给玩家。"],"reviewer_rationale":"基线把 background 译成角色职业并有多项机制与命令表述失真；整段重译更能守住列表和快捷键。","terminal_conclusion":"retranslate"}
{"actual_behavior":"新手可用提示模式、选择较容易的组合、谨慎处理新怪物并关注 HP/MP；`#` 生成角色转储以供社区建议。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"answer_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2368","bullet_transform":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2374","key_enumeration":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2357","menu":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:456","question_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:472"},"current_chinese":{"answer":"A: 如果你连到达神庙都觉得困难，则可能需要在提示模式下尝试一两个游戏，\n以获得一些常规的游玩提示。\n\n此外，还有简单的组合和更具挑战性的组合（在选择屏幕上以深灰色标记）。\n如果你对Crawl不熟悉，建议先坚持使用更简单的组合。\n通常，要格外小心地处理遇到的每个新怪物，试着挑选与怪物战斗的位置，\n并时刻留意自己的生命值与魔法值。\n\n对于涉及特定角色的特定提示，\n你可以在游戏中按'#'以在morgue目录中创建“角色记录”，\n详细说明你的装备、物品、技能和可用法术。\n你可以在http://crawl.develz.org/ 这个网页上列出的社区中向其他玩家分享它，\n以获取反馈和建议。\n","question":"如何才能避免死亡？有什么游戏技巧？"},"current_english":{"answer":"A: If you've got trouble even reaching the Temple, you might want to try a game\nor two in hints mode for some general playing hints.\n\nAlso, there are easy playing combinations and more challenging ones (marked in\ndark grey on the selection screen). If you are new to Crawl, sticking to the\neasier ones is advisable. In general, treat each new monster you meet with\nutmost caution, try to choose the location where you fight monsters, and keep\nan eye on your health and magic.\n\nFor specific hints dealing with a particular character, you can press '#'\nin-game to create a \"character dump\" in your morgue directory that details your\nequipment, items, skilling, and available spells. You can share this with other\nplayers to get feedback and advice through any of the community discussion\nresources listed at http://crawl.develz.org/.\n","question":"How can I avoid dying all the time? Can you give me some playing tips?"},"deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"gameplay goals, survival, and mechanics","display_context":"FAQ menu question plus unwrapped answer; {"actual_behavior":"缺失的下楼梯可能位于本层不可从当前上楼梯到达的区域，也可能已到分支底层；Ctrl-O 与 ?/b 提供分支信息。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"answer_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2368","bullet_transform":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2374","key_enumeration":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2357","menu":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:456","question_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:472"},"current_chinese":{"answer":"A: 它们可能位于楼上的那个楼梯无法连通到本层一块区域中。尝试到上一层，\n然后从另一个楼梯那里下来。请注意，大多数楼层有着三个上楼楼梯，三个下楼楼梯，\n这里面不纳入任何逃生通道。\n\n也有可能，你已经到了某一分支的最低层，必须转而探索另一个分支。\n查看地牢概述（'Ctrl-O'）以了解你在某一分支的深入程度，\n或查看数据库（'?/b'）以了解未探索分支的楼层数。","question":"我找不到向下的楼梯。我是不是错过了什么啊？"},"current_english":{"answer":"A: They may be in a part of the level that is not reachable from the upstairs\nyou came from. Check the\ndungeon overview ('Ctrl-O') to see how deep you've delved into a given branch,\nor the database ('?/b') for the number of levels for unseen branches.","question":"I can't find any downstairs. runtime menu order is DBM iteration order and is not guaranteed by the current consumer."},"structural_differences":[],"token_contract_equal":{"answer":{"blank_line_bullet_count":true,"bullet_line_count":true,"format_tag_counts":true,"format_tag_sequence":true,"lua_block_count":true,"lua_comparison_literals":true,"lua_control_skeleton":true,"numbers":true,"option_literals":true,"path_literals":true,"quoted_command_literals":true,"technical_angle_counts":true,"technical_angle_sequence":true,"urls":true,"w_contents":true},"question":{"blank_line_bullet_count":true,"bullet_line_count":true,"format_tag_counts":true,"format_tag_sequence":true,"lua_block_count":true,"lua_comparison_literals":true,"lua_control_skeleton":true,"numbers":true,"option_literals":true,"path_literals":true,"quoted_command_literals":true,"technical_angle_counts":true,"technical_angle_sequence":true,"urls":true,"w_contents":true}}},"proposed_translation":{"answer":"A: 下楼梯可能位于本层某个区域，而从你来时的那座上楼梯无法到达该区域。\n试着返回上一层，再从另一组楼梯下到本层。请注意，大多数楼层都有\n三座下楼梯和三座上楼梯，逃生舱口不计在内。\n\n或者，如果你身处某个分支，也可能已经到达该分支的最底层，\n必须掉头去探索另一个分支。查看地牢概览（'Ctrl-O'）可了解你在指定分支中\n已经深入到哪一层；查看数据库（'?/b'）则可了解尚未见过的分支共有多少层。","question":"我找不到下楼梯。是不是漏掉了什么？"},"reentry_trigger":"对应英文源文、生产 consumer、格式约定或 docs/glossary.md 裁决发生变化时重新审阅。","rejected_alternatives":["仅作零散词语替换：跨段落的事实、列表或格式问题无法得到完整修复。","原样保留基线译文：已确认的语义或结构缺陷会继续展示给玩家。"],"reviewer_rationale":"基线首句逻辑混乱并弱化三上三下楼梯与逃生舱口例外；重译可清晰恢复空间关系。","terminal_conclusion":"retranslate"}

{"actual_behavior":"武器比较取决于技能、单双手、随机神器属性、铭印和目标；描述或装备时按 `@` 可查看延迟与伤害评级。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"answer_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2368","bullet_transform":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2374","key_enumeration":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2357","menu":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:456","question_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:472"},"current_chinese":{"answer":"A: 武器的好坏从很多方面来看，比如你的武器技能、它是单手还是双手武器、\n随机神器属性、武器印记，以及你要面对的敌人类型。通常，\n从一种你已经很熟练的武器类型切换到另一种你还不熟练的武器类型就是浪费时间。\n印记在伤害输出上可能有很大不同，\n你也可以通过攻击几个没什么威胁的怪物试试哪把武器更好。\n\n武器的攻击延迟可以通过提升武器技能等级来降低。","question":"我捡了两把很棒的武器。怎么才能比较哪个更好？"},"current_english":{"answer":"A: \"Better\" depends on a number of factors, such as your weapon skills, whether\nit's one- or two-handed, the randart properties, the weapon brand, and the\nmonsters you're about to face. In general, switching from a weapon type at\nwhich you are already highly skilled to another one that you have no skill in\nis a waste of time. The brands may greatly differ in damage output, but you can\nget a good idea by bashing a few comparatively harmless monsters with each and\ncomparing the results.\n\nThe delay in swinging a weapon can be reduced by the corresponding weapon skill.\nYou can view your delay and damage rating with a weapon by reading its\ndescription or pressing '@' while the weapon is equipped. '@' works while\nunarmed too.","question":"I've got two cool weapons. How can I tell which one is better?"},"deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"gameplay goals, survival, and mechanics","display_context":"FAQ menu question plus unwrapped answer; blank lines before asterisk bullets become Unicode bullets.","english":{"answer":"A: \"Better\" depends on a number of factors, such as your weapon skills, whether\nit's one- or two-handed, the randart properties, the weapon brand, and the\nmonsters you're about to face. In general, switching from a weapon type at\nwhich you are already highly skilled to another one that you have no skill in\nis a waste of time. runtime menu order is DBM iteration order and is not guaranteed by the current consumer."},"structural_differences":["answer.quoted_command_literals"],"token_contract_equal":{"answer":{"blank_line_bullet_count":true,"bullet_line_count":true,"format_tag_counts":true,"format_tag_sequence":true,"lua_block_count":true,"lua_comparison_literals":true,"lua_control_skeleton":true,"numbers":true,"option_literals":true,"path_literals":true,"quoted_command_literals":false,"technical_angle_counts":true,"technical_angle_sequence":true,"urls":true,"w_contents":true},"question":{"blank_line_bullet_count":true,"bullet_line_count":true,"format_tag_counts":true,"format_tag_sequence":true,"lua_block_count":true,"lua_comparison_literals":true,"lua_control_skeleton":true,"numbers":true,"option_literals":true,"path_literals":true,"quoted_command_literals":true,"technical_angle_counts":true,"technical_angle_sequence":true,"urls":true,"w_contents":true}}},"proposed_translation":{"answer":"A: “更好”取决于许多因素，例如你的武器技能、武器是单手还是双手、\n随机神器属性、武器铭印，以及你即将面对的怪物。一般来说，\n从已经非常熟练的武器类型改用完全没有技能的另一类型是在浪费时间。\n不同铭印造成的伤害可能相差很大；你可以分别用两把武器敲打几个相对无害的怪物，\n比较结果，从而得到大致判断。\n\n武器的攻击延迟可以通过提升武器技能等级来降低。\n你可以阅读武器描述，或在装备该武器时按'@'，查看攻击延迟和伤害评级。\n赤手空拳时也可以按'@'。","question":"我有两把很酷的武器。怎么判断哪把更好？"},"reentry_trigger":"对应英文源文、生产 consumer、格式约定或 docs/glossary.md 裁决发生变化时重新审阅。","rejected_alternatives":["仅作零散词语替换：跨段落的事实、列表或格式问题无法得到完整修复。","原样保留基线译文：已确认的语义或结构缺陷会继续展示给玩家。"],"reviewer_rationale":"基线遗漏 `@` 的延迟/伤害评级查询与赤手空拳用法，信息量缺失较大；整段重译。","terminal_conclusion":"retranslate"}
{"actual_behavior":"新手近战、远程和施法各有直观神祇选择；祭坛 `>` 阅读说明，信徒在 `^` 页面用 `!` 查看更详细描述。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"answer_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2368","bullet_transform":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2374","key_enumeration":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2357","menu":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:456","question_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:472"},"current_chinese":{"answer":"A: 最直接的神灵可能是特洛格或奥卡瓦鲁（近战），\n惠普利亚卡纳或奥卡瓦鲁（远程），\n维胡梅特或西芙·穆娜（施法）。\n另外，虽然不可靠，但佐姆可能会很有趣。\n\n每当你遇到一个祭坛时，请务必阅读相应神的描述（通过在祭坛上用 '>'祈祷），\n并花点时间决定是否皈依这个神灵。作为追随者，你可以重新阅读神的描述，\n现在还可以通过'!'在'^'界面上切换为更详细的描述。\n\n你一定会在地牢的前十层找到几乎所有神的祭坛。","question":"对于新手来说，推荐皈依哪些神？"},"current_english":{"answer":"A: The most straightforward gods to play are probably Trog or Okawaru (for melee\ncombat), Hepliaklqana or Okawaru (for ranged combat), Vehumet or Sif Muna (for\nspellcasting). Also, while unreliable, Xom can be a lot of fun.\n\nWhenever you encounter an altar, be sure to read the corresponding god's\ndescription (by praying on the altar with '>') and take your time in deciding\nwhether to pick this deity or not. As a follower, you can reread your god's\ndescription, now more detailed via the '!' toggle, on the '^' screen.\n\nYou're guaranteed to find altars to almost every god on the first ten levels of\nthe main dungeon.","question":"Which gods would you recommend to a beginning player?"},"deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"gameplay goals, survival, and mechanics","display_context":"FAQ menu question plus unwrapped answer; blank lines before asterisk bullets become Unicode bullets.","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/database/FAQ.txt:176","crawl-ref/source/dat/database/FAQ.txt:180","crawl-ref/source/dat/database/zh/FAQ.txt:155","crawl-ref/source/dat/database/zh/FAQ.txt:159"],"fact_sha256":"ea41e5e39f3fe0c42ba9e8798415eca3b8417c042bc384dd00b54ae0476f3488","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md@95eeacf9704e046c2010ef34859b750d2f8a1937ad87c4a86e8a404c98689407","identity":"faq:religion","lifecycle":"current-faq-menu-entry","producer":{"chinese_question_order":7,"english_question_order":7,"source_note":"File order is authoring evidence;
