runtime menu order is DBM iteration order and is not guaranteed by the current consumer."},"structural_differences":[],"token_contract_equal":{"answer":{"blank_line_bullet_count":true,"bullet_line_count":true,"format_tag_counts":true,"format_tag_sequence":true,"lua_block_count":true,"lua_comparison_literals":true,"lua_control_skeleton":true,"numbers":true,"option_literals":true,"path_literals":true,"quoted_command_literals":true,"technical_angle_counts":true,"technical_angle_sequence":true,"urls":true,"w_contents":true},"question":{"blank_line_bullet_count":true,"bullet_line_count":true,"format_tag_counts":true,"format_tag_sequence":true,"lua_block_count":true,"lua_comparison_literals":true,"lua_control_skeleton":true,"numbers":true,"option_literals":true,"path_literals":true,"quoted_command_literals":true,"technical_angle_counts":true,"technical_angle_sequence":true,"urls":true,"w_contents":true}}},"proposed_translation":{"answer":"A: 最直观易用的神祇大概是特洛格或奥卡瓦鲁（近战），\n惠普利亚卡纳或奥卡瓦鲁（远程战斗），维胡梅特或西芙·穆娜（施法）。\n此外，佐姆虽然不可靠，却可能非常有趣。\n\n每当遇到祭坛，都务必阅读对应神祇的描述（在祭坛上按'>'祈祷），\n并仔细考虑是否皈依该神祇。成为信徒后，可以在'^'页面重新阅读神祇描述，\n并用'!'切换显示更详细的说明。\n\n主地牢前十层保证能找到几乎所有神祇的祭坛。","question":"你会向新手推荐哪些神祇？"},"reentry_trigger":"对应英文源文、生产 consumer、格式约定或 docs/glossary.md 裁决发生变化时重新审阅。","rejected_alternatives":["原样保留基线译文：已确认的局部歧义、遗漏或术语问题仍会存在。","整项重译：问题范围局部，扩大改写会增加不必要的回归面。"],"reviewer_rationale":"基线神祇建议大致正确，但按键操作和保证出现范围表达不够精确；局部调整即可。","terminal_conclusion":"adjust"}
{"actual_behavior":"玩家鬼魂不能关闭，但生成在封闭宝库中，玩家可选择是否打开；也可用强势组合清除或在线遇到他人鬼魂。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"answer_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2368","bullet_transform":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2374","key_enumeration":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2357","menu":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:456","question_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:472"},"current_chinese":{"answer":"A: 没有任何这样的选项，并且我们也不打算添加一个。\n\n请记住，你不必和遇到的每个怪物战斗，而且与鬼魂战斗甚至要更容易：\n他们总是在被封印的宝库中生成，而你可以选择是否要打开各个鬼魂宝库。\n\n另外，你也可以选用强势的种族和职业，比如巨魔狂战士，专门用来消灭这些鬼魂。\n如果是在线游戏，那你很可能要被他人的鬼魂杀死！","question":"我一直被自己的鬼魂杀掉。我可以关掉它们吗？"},"current_english":{"answer":"A: There isn't any such option, and we're not planning to add one, either.\n\nKeep in mind that you don't absolutely have to fight every monster you\nmeet, and with ghosts it's even easier: they are generated in sealed\nvaults, and it is your choice whether to open each ghost vault.\n\nAlternatively, you could play a couple characters of strong species/background\ncombinations for the sole purpose of busting those ghosts, e.g. Troll\nBerserkers. Or play online, where you'll mostly be killed by others' ghosts\ninstead!","question":"I keep getting murdered by my own ghosts. {"actual_behavior":"逃离深渊依赖稀有出口、持续移动、防御装备、延迟传送、卢格努、深入层数和有选择地杀敌生成出口。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"answer_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2368","bullet_transform":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2374","key_enumeration":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2357","menu":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:456","question_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:472"},"current_chinese":{"answer":"A: 如果你是第一次进入深渊，那么能够离开的机会很小。不过，\n这里仍然有一些一般性提示：\n\n* 你需要找到能走出深渊的出口。它们很少！\n\n* 不要恋战，尽可能移动！\n\n* 如果你有的话，使用防御装备（防护武器等）。\n\n* 通过传送到达深渊的一个全新区域。（但要记住，\n  在深渊中传送花费的时间要长于在地牢之中……）\n\n* 考虑转而皈依深渊之神卢格努。一旦皈依，你就能立刻逃出深渊\n  （但这可能会触怒你之前的神！）。\n\n*  深入深渊会增加找到出路的机会，但也会增加你面临的危险。\n\n* 可以通过杀死敌人来创造出一个出口，但大多数敌人都过于危险，因此，\n  一定要小心地、有选择地战斗！","question":"我已经以某种方式到达了一个叫做“深渊”的地方。我怎么才能离开这里？"},"current_english":{"answer":"A: If you are in the Abyss for the first time, chances of getting out are\nslim. Still, here are a few general hints:\n\n* You need to find a gateway leading out of the Abyss. These are rare!\n\n* Don't stick around to fight; move as much as possible!\n\n* If you have it, use defensive equipment (weapons of protection, etc.).\n\n* Use teleportation to end up in a completely new part of the Abyss. (But keep\n  in mind that teleportation will take much longer to kick in than in the\n  Dungeon...)\n\n* Consider switching your allegiance to Lugonu, the god of the Abyss. Upon\n  conversion, you will be able to escape the Abyss immediately (but you may\n  anger your previous god!).\n\n* Descending deeper in the Abyss will increase the chance of finding an exit\n  out, but also increase the danger you face.\n\n* Killing enemies can create a gateway out, but most enemies will be\n  too dangerous to fight; pick your battles carefully!","question":"I've somehow ended up in a place called \"Abyss\". How do I get out of here?"},"deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"gameplay goals, survival, and mechanics","display_context":"FAQ menu question plus unwrapped answer; blank lines before asterisk bullets become Unicode bullets.","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/database/FAQ.txt:211","crawl-ref/source/dat/database/FAQ.txt:215","crawl-ref/source/dat/database/zh/FAQ.txt:186","crawl-ref/source/dat/database/zh/FAQ.txt:190"],"fact_sha256":"145cb84edff26e0d50787d32a3e98a9af5282df9ae4e304e5172feb7f1b77961","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md@95eeacf9704e046c2010ef34859b750d2f8a1937ad87c4a86e8a404c98689407","identity":"faq:abyss","lifecycle":"current-faq-menu-entry","producer":{"chinese_question_order":9,"english_question_order":9,"source_note":"File order is authoring evidence; runtime menu order is DBM iteration order and is not guaranteed by the current consumer."},"structural_differences":[],"token_contract_equal":{"answer":{"blank_line_bullet_count":true,"bullet_line_count":true,"format_tag_counts":true,"format_tag_sequence":true,"lua_block_count":true,"lua_comparison_literals":true,"lua_control_skeleton":true,"numbers":true,"option_literals":true,"path_literals":true,"quoted_command_literals":true,"technical_angle_counts":true,"technical_angle_sequence":true,"urls":true,"w_contents":true},"question":{"blank_line_bullet_count":true,"bullet_line_count":true,"format_tag_counts":true,"format_tag_sequence":true,"lua_block_count":true,"lua_comparison_literals":true,"lua_control_skeleton":true,"numbers":true,"option_literals":true,"path_literals":true,"quoted_command_literals":true,"technical_angle_counts":true,"technical_angle_sequence":true,"urls":true,"w_contents":true}}},"proposed_translation":{"answer":"A: 如果这是你第一次进入深渊，逃出去的机会很小。不过仍有一些通用建议：\n\n* 你需要找到通往外界的传送门。这种传送门很罕见！\n\n* 不要停下来战斗；尽可能多地移动！\n\n* 如果有防御性装备，就用上它们（防护铭印武器等）。\n\n* 使用传送，前往深渊中完全不同的区域。（但请记住，\n  在深渊中，传送要比在地牢中延迟更久才会生效……）\n\n* 考虑转而皈依深渊之神卢格努。一旦皈依，你就能立刻逃出深渊\n  （但可能会激怒你先前信奉的神祇！）。\n\n* 进入深渊的更深层会提高找到出口的概率，但也会增加你面临的危险。\n\n* 杀死敌人可能会生成通往外界的传送门，但大多数敌人都太危险，不适合交战；\n  请谨慎选择战斗！","question":"我不知怎么来到了一个叫“深渊”的地方。怎样才能离开？"},"reentry_trigger":"对应英文源文、生产 consumer、格式约定或 docs/glossary.md 裁决发生变化时重新审阅。","rejected_alternatives":["原样保留基线译文：已确认的局部歧义、遗漏或术语问题仍会存在。","整项重译：问题范围局部，扩大改写会增加不必要的回归面。"],"reviewer_rationale":"基线大体完整，但把出口、传送时机和深入层数的影响说得不够准确；按项目逐项调整。","terminal_conclusion":"adjust"}
{"actual_behavior":"randart 是随机神器，unrandart 是预定义神器；神器不能重新铭印，通常不能附魔，但神器附魔变异只允许随机神器武器/护甲强化到普通同类上限。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"answer_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2368","bullet_transform":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2374","key_enumeration":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2357","menu":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:456","question_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:472"},"current_chinese":{"answer":"A: 在地牢中除了可以找到的普通物品外，你还可能会偶然发现有两种不同的神器：\n\n* 随机神器：随机的神器通常是武器、防具或首饰，它们具有随机属性，\n  能带来抗性或能力。\n\n* 非随机神器：稀有的神器，其属性是预先定义好的，而非随机生成的。\n  大多数非随机神器一眼就能鉴定出来，具有别的地方没有的特殊效果或独特能力。\n\n这些神器都不能被暂时或永久地附魔或烙印。","question":"术语“ 随机神器”和“ 非随机神器”分别表示什么？"},"current_english":{"answer":"A: Apart from the standard items you'll find lying around in the dungeon, you\nmight also stumble upon artefacts which come in two varieties:\n\n* randart: a random artefact, usually a weapon, armour or jewellery, with\n  random properties such as conferring resistances or abilities.\n\n* unrandart: a rarer variety of artefact that is predefined instead of randomly\n  generated. Most unrandarts are identified on sight, and some come with\n  special effects or unique abilities that are not available elsewhere.\n\nNeither of these types can be enchanted or branded, either temporarily or\npermanently.","question":"What do the terms \"randart\" and \"unrandart\" signify?"},"deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"gameplay goals, survival, and mechanics","display_context":"FAQ menu question plus unwrapped answer; runtime menu order is DBM iteration order and is not guaranteed by the current consumer."},"structural_differences":[],"token_contract_equal":{"answer":{"blank_line_bullet_count":true,"bullet_line_count":true,"format_tag_counts":true,"format_tag_sequence":true,"lua_block_count":true,"lua_comparison_literals":true,"lua_control_skeleton":true,"numbers":true,"option_literals":true,"path_literals":true,"quoted_command_literals":true,"technical_angle_counts":true,"technical_angle_sequence":true,"urls":true,"w_contents":true},"question":{"blank_line_bullet_count":true,"bullet_line_count":true,"format_tag_counts":true,"format_tag_sequence":true,"lua_block_count":true,"lua_comparison_literals":true,"lua_control_skeleton":true,"numbers":true,"option_literals":true,"path_literals":true,"quoted_command_literals":true,"technical_angle_counts":true,"technical_angle_sequence":true,"urls":true,"w_contents":true}}},"proposed_translation":{"answer":"A: 除了散落在地牢中的普通物品，你还可能遇到神器。神器分为两类：\n\n* randart：随机神器，通常是武器、护甲或饰品，具有随机属性，\n  例如提供抗性或能力。\n\n* unrandart：较罕见的非随机神器，其属性预先定义，而非随机生成。\n  大多数非随机神器一眼就能鉴定出来，只有一部分带有其他地方无法获得的\n  特殊效果或独特能力。\n\n所有神器都不能被暂时或永久地重新铭印。神器通常也不能附魔；\n但拥有“神器附魔”变异时，可以用武器附魔卷轴或护甲附魔卷轴\n强化随机神器武器或随机神器护甲，且不能超过同类普通物品的强化上限。\n非随机神器即使拥有该变异也不能附魔。","question":"术语“randart”和“unrandart”分别是什么意思？"},"reentry_trigger":"对应英文源文、生产 consumer、格式约定或 docs/glossary.md 裁决发生变化时重新审阅。","rejected_alternatives":["仅作零散词语替换：跨段落的事实、列表或格式问题无法得到完整修复。","原样保留基线译文：已确认的语义或结构缺陷会继续展示给玩家。"],"reviewer_rationale":"基线沿用了过时的‘两类都不能附魔’绝对说法；英文源事实同步修正后必须重译，并明确 unrandart 例外外仍不可附魔。","terminal_conclusion":"retranslate"}

{"actual_behavior":"Stone Soup 的 0.X 编号不表示不稳定；编号主要因历史与避免混淆而保留，未来是否更改并不确定。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"answer_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2368","bullet_transform":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2374","key_enumeration":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2357","menu":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:456","question_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:472"},"current_chinese":{"answer":"A: 我们认为对于一款处于积极开发状态的游戏来说，石头汤已经相当稳定，\n所以请不要因为版本号是0.X而烦恼，尝试一下。\n\n因为某些历史原因，我们必须这么保持版本号，否则将会引起不必要的混乱。\n我们或许会在某个时候改变这一点，也有可能不会。","question":"什么时候才能开发出“稳定”的1.0版本？"},"current_english":{"answer":"A: Actually, by now we consider Stone Soup to be reasonably stable for a game\nof such active development, so please don't get hung up about the 0.X\nversioning and give it a try.\n\nWe've mostly kept the versioning for historic reasons and because it might\ncause undue confusion. blank lines before asterisk bullets become Unicode bullets.","english":{"answer":"A: Actually, by now we consider Stone Soup to be reasonably stable for a game\nof such active development, so please don't get hung up about the 0.X\nversioning and give it a try.\n\nWe've mostly kept the versioning for historic reasons and because it might\ncause undue confusion. runtime menu order is DBM iteration order and is not guaranteed by the current consumer."},"structural_differences":[],"token_contract_equal":{"answer":{"blank_line_bullet_count":true,"bullet_line_count":true,"format_tag_counts":true,"format_tag_sequence":true,"lua_block_count":true,"lua_comparison_literals":true,"lua_control_skeleton":true,"numbers":true,"option_literals":true,"path_literals":true,"quoted_command_literals":true,"technical_angle_counts":true,"technical_angle_sequence":true,"urls":true,"w_contents":true},"question":{"blank_line_bullet_count":true,"bullet_line_count":true,"format_tag_counts":true,"format_tag_sequence":true,"lua_block_count":true,"lua_comparison_literals":true,"lua_control_skeleton":true,"numbers":true,"option_literals":true,"path_literals":true,"quoted_command_literals":true,"technical_angle_counts":true,"technical_angle_sequence":true,"urls":true,"w_contents":true}}},"proposed_translation":{"answer":"A: 实际上，对于一款开发如此活跃的游戏，我们如今认为Stone Soup已经相当稳定，\n所以请不要纠结0.X版本号，尽管试一试。\n\n我们沿用这一版本编号，主要是出于历史原因，也因为改动它可能造成不必要的混乱。\n未来某个时候也许会改，也许不会。","question":"什么时候才会开发到“稳定”的1.0版本？"},"reentry_trigger":"对应英文源文、生产 consumer、格式约定或 docs/glossary.md 裁决发生变化时重新审阅。","rejected_alternatives":["原样保留基线译文：已确认的局部歧义、遗漏或术语问题仍会存在。","整项重译：问题范围局部，扩大改写会增加不必要的回归面。"],"reviewer_rationale":"基线把保留编号说成强制要求，语气过强；局部修正即可保留 1.0 与 0.X。","terminal_conclusion":"adjust"}
{"actual_behavior":"下一版本可通过在线 trunk 或 Windows/Mac 二进制提前体验；trunk 可能有 bug，极少使存档失效，报告需附 `?v` 版本。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"answer_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2368","bullet_transform":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2374","key_enumeration":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2357","menu":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:456","question_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:472"},"current_chinese":{"answer":"A: 当然有！你可以在任何在线服务器上游玩“ trunk”（最终将成为下一版本）；\nhttps://crawl.develz.org 还存储着可下载的Windows和Mac版本的游戏文件。\n请注意，trunk可能有问题，而且错误可能会有小概率让你保存的游戏无效。\n如果那不影响你，请继续玩！\n\n一定要在github上（https://github.com/crawl/crawl/issues）报告错误。\n如果你有mantis账号的话，也可以使用错误跟踪器。\n（https://github.com/crawl/crawl/issues）。\n\n当你这样做时，请提及当前正在游玩的游戏版本（见'?v'），\n以免出现错误已被修复或功能已经实现的情况。","question":"在新版本正式发布前，我可以玩玩测试版么？"},"current_english":{"answer":"A: There certainly is! {"actual_behavior":"GitHub bug 报告应包括操作、预期/实际结果、`?v` 版本、console/tiles；必要时提供存档、服务器/用户名、截图、错误消息和复现步骤。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"answer_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2368","bullet_transform":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2374","key_enumeration":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2357","menu":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:456","question_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:472"},"current_chinese":{"answer":"A: 请在github（https://github.com/crawl/crawl/issues）或GitHub Issues\n（https://github.com/crawl/crawl/issues）上提交错误报告，并提供尽可能多的信息：\n\n* 那时你在做什么？\n\n*你期望发生什么，实际发生了什么，等等。\n\n* 最重要的是，你玩的哪个版本？\n  （如果不确定，则可以在游戏中使用'?v'进行查看。）\n\n* 控制台或者图形版？\n\n对于崩溃或是怪物/物品/楼层的异常行为，我们可能需要一个存档文件。\n如果你玩的是在线控制台版本，则可以通过选择要游玩的版本，\n然后按'A'使用高级选项来创建保存文件的副本，以供开发人员访问。\n不要忘记在你提交的错误报告中包含服务器和用户名！\n\n有时截图会很有用。如果有错误消息，请记录下来。如果没有存档文件，\n则有关如何重现崩溃（或错误）的指导可能会很有用；为此，你可能会用到向导模式。\n\n先行致谢！","question":"游戏一直崩溃，/ 我觉得我找到了一个错误。我该怎么办？"},"current_english":{"answer":"A: Please submit a bug report on github (https://github.com/crawl/crawl/issues),\nand give as much information as you can:\n\n* What were you doing at the time?\n\n* What did you expect to happen, what happened instead, etc.\n\n* Most importantly, what version are you playing?\n  (If you're not sure, you can check in-game with '?v'.)\n\n* Console or tiles?\n\nFor crashes or monsters/items/levels behaving oddly, we probably need a save\nfile. If you're playing online console, you can create a copy of your save file\nfor the developers to access by selecting the version you are playing and then\npressing 'A' for Advanced options. Don't forget to include the server and your\nusername in your bug report!\n\nSometimes a screenshot can be helpful. If there are error messages, please\nwrite them down. Short of a save file, a guide on how to reproduce the crash\n(or bug) can be useful; you can use wizard mode for this.\n\nThanks in advance!","question":"The game keeps crashing. / I think I found a bug. What do I do?"},"deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"project, release, support, and performance","display_context":"FAQ menu question plus unwrapped answer; blank lines before asterisk bullets become Unicode bullets.","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/database/FAQ.txt:306","crawl-ref/source/dat/database/FAQ.txt:310","crawl-ref/source/dat/database/zh/FAQ.txt:274","crawl-ref/source/dat/database/zh/FAQ.txt:278"],"fact_sha256":"a0e78501dee0711bee87d37035f3e8cbfd3dda1cc05e9018efc83e42bee5498c","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md@95eeacf9704e046c2010ef34859b750d2f8a1937ad87c4a86e8a404c98689407","identity":"faq:bug","lifecycle":"current-faq-menu-entry","producer":{"chinese_question_order":14,"english_question_order":14,"source_note":"File order is authoring evidence; {"actual_behavior":"建议应放在开发者可见的论坛，说明对玩法的影响；界面建议可能较易采用，其他建议通常需要论证。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"answer_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2368","bullet_transform":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2374","key_enumeration":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2357","menu":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:456","question_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:472"},"current_chinese":{"answer":"A: 如果你希望将想法付诸实践，\n那么最好的选择就是将其发布在开发人员可以看到的地方。\n官方论坛是一个合适的地方，网址为https://tavern.dcss.io/。\n如果这个想法至少吸引了一个开发人员的兴趣，那就很有可能会加入游戏。\n\n注意，如果你在游戏玩法上有更好的建议，那它更有可能被接受。\n（“关于[添加/删除 XXX]会怎样使游戏变得更好？”）\n而关于如何改善界面的建议可能没有多大用处，另外，\n对于其他建议你可能需要辩护、证明其价值。","question":"我有个很酷的想法！该跟谁说？"},"current_english":{"answer":"A: If you'd like your idea to get in, your surest bet is to put it in a place\nwhere a developer can see it. (\"How does [adding/removing X] make the game\nbetter?\") A suggestion for how to improve the interface may go in without much\nado, but other suggestions will probably require to defend & justify them.","question":"I've got a cool idea! {"actual_behavior":"贡献范围包括地图/宝库、怪物台词、描述和贴图；补丁指南、提交入口及三类拒绝原因均需完整保留。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"answer_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2368","bullet_transform":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2374","key_enumeration":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2357","menu":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:456","question_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:472"},"current_chinese":{"answer":"A: 你可以在这几个方面发挥创造力，并且我们非常感谢你的外援。这些是：\n\n* 地图/宝库（见dat/ directory, docs/develop/levels/introduction.txt）\n\n* 怪物的语句（见dat/database/, docs/develop/monster_speech.txt）\n\n* 怪物/物品/法术的描述（见dat/descript/）\n\n* 贴图（见source/rltiles/, docs/develop/tiles_creation.txt）\n\n另外，如果你想编写补丁程序，但不知道该怎么做，\n请阅读docs/develop/目录中的patch_guide.txt。\n在我们的GitHub Issues跟踪器上提交它和其他新内容：https://github.com/crawl/crawl/issues\n\n请记住，如果没有事先的讨论，我们可能无法使用该补丁，\n或者可能需要对它进行大量修改。它也有可能不被接受，通常是由于以下三个原因：\n\n* 正如我们所看到的，它不适合Crawl的世界。\n\n* 它违反了Crawl的哲学，即避免磨损和破坏重物。\n\n* 也有可能我们只是认为它没有足够的趣味而没有实施。\n\n非常感谢你的支持！","question":"我想帮助改善游戏。我该怎么办？"},"current_english":{"answer":"A: There are several areas where you can get creative and outside help is much\nappreciated. These are:\n\n* maps/vaults (see dat/ directory, docs/develop/levels/introduction.txt)\n\n* monster speech (see dat/database/, docs/develop/monster_speech.txt)\n\n* monster/item/spell descriptions (see dat/descript/)\n\n* tiles (see source/rltiles/, docs/develop/tiles_creation.txt)\n\nAlso, if you'd like to write a patch but don't know how to go about it, read\npatch_guide.txt in the docs/develop/ directory. Submit it and other new things\nat our github repository: https://github.com/crawl/crawl/issues\n\nKeep in mind that without previous discussions, we may not be able to use the\npatch, or it might need heavy modification. It's possible that it won't be\naccepted, usually for one of three reasons:\n\n* It doesn't fit into the Crawl world as we see it.\n\n* It violates Crawl's philosophy to avoid grinding and spoiler-heavy stuff.\n\n* We just don't think it's interesting enough to ever be implemented.\n\nThanks a lot for your support!","question":"I'd like to help improve the game. What can I do?"},"deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"project, release, support, and performance","display_context":"FAQ menu question plus unwrapped answer; blank lines {"actual_behavior":"贴图版依赖 OpenGL/SDL 与硬件加速；更新 Windows 驱动、启用 Linux DRI、调整 tiles_help 选项或改用控制台版均是现有建议。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"answer_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2368","bullet_transform":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2374","key_enumeration":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2357","menu":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:456","question_lookup":"crawl-ref/source/command.cc:472"},"current_chinese":{"answer":"A: Crawl使用OpenGL和SDL渲染其图形。因为它用的是OpenGL，\n所以要依靠一点硬件加速才能使其迅速运行。如果你的系统未启用此功能，\n那么Crawl会运行得很差。\n\n在Windows上，Windows自带的默认驱动程序运行得非常缓慢。\n确保将你的显卡驱动程序更新为最新可用版本。在Linux上，你应确保启用了DRI。\n\n除了这些建议之外，你还可以尝试查看调整某些选项是否可以提高性能。\n有关详细信息，请参阅docs/tiles_help.txt。如果做了这一切还是太卡，\n则可以尝试使用非图形的控制台版本，或者等待不依赖OpenGL的未来版本。","question":"图像版真的好慢！怎样才能让它跑得快点？"},"current_english":{"answer":"A: Crawl renders its graphics using OpenGL and SDL. Because it uses OpenGL, it\ndepends on a little bit of hardware acceleration to not run slowly. If your\nsystem is not set up to enable this, then Crawl will run quite poorly.\n\nOn Windows, the default driver that ships with Windows is extremely slow. Make\nsure to update your graphics card driver to the latest one available. On Linux,\nyou should be sure to enable DRI.\n\nIn addition to these suggestions, you can also try to see whether tweaking some\noptions will improve performance. See docs/tiles_help.txt for details. If\nthings are still too laggy after all of that, you could try the non-graphical\nconsole version, or wait for a future version that does not depend on OpenGL.","question":"The Tiles version is awfully slow! How can I improve that?"},"deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"project, release, support, and performance","display_context":"FAQ menu question plus unwrapped answer; blank lines before asterisk bullets become Unicode bullets.","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/dat/database/FAQ.txt:395","crawl-ref/source/dat/database/FAQ.txt:399","crawl-ref/source/dat/database/zh/FAQ.txt:359","crawl-ref/source/dat/database/zh/FAQ.txt:363"],"fact_sha256":"af7ad2099dfe128dc92489281128112482d79d99b631e9ce6e225b85440cd581","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md@95eeacf9704e046c2010ef34859b750d2f8a1937ad87c4a86e8a404c98689407","identity":"faq:tiles lag","lifecycle":"current-faq-menu-entry","producer":{"chinese_question_order":18,"english_question_order":18,"source_note":"File order is authoring evidence;
